# フェーズ 4d-3: 骨組み・サイドバー・ツリー Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 見た目の基準と画面の骨組み、サイドバー、ツリー（表示・フィルタ・キーボード・ドラッグ）、異常系と空の状態、Onboarding と CreateStore を作り、その E2E を通す。まだない画面（詳細・作成・パレット）への入口は出さない。

**Architecture:** フェーズ 4d の4回のうちの1回。決定事項・spec にない細部・ファイル構成・共通の型は概要 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-02-phase4d-web.md` にあり、各 Task はそこを前提にする（細部の番号はそこを指す）。Task の番号は 4d 全体の通し番号。

**Tech Stack:** 概要のとおり。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-01-phase4-gui-design.md`（「4d・4e フロントエンド」「画面と範囲」「4c サーバー」）と、概要 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-02-phase4d-web.md`。

**この回の Task:** Task 7、8、9、10、15、16a

**着手の前に:** 4d-2 がマージ済みであること。

## Global Constraints

- 画面の文言はすべて日本語。コードの識別子・コメントは英語（既存のコードと同じ）
- コンポーネントは色・寸法の 16 進数や px を直接書かず、`web/app/styles/tokens.css` の CSS 変数を使う（Foundations: 「色はトークン名で実装し、画面に 16 進数を直書きしない」）
- ステータスは色だけでなく形で区別する（Foundations のステータスの形）
- 行とカードは ID だけを出し、ULID は ID にカーソルを合わせたときに出してコピーできるようにする。タスク詳細では ULID を常に出す。「キー未確定」・短縮 ID・「次の同期で確定」は作らない
- フォントは `@fontsource` で同梱し、外部（Google Fonts など）に通信しない
- ダークモードは作らない
- キーボード: ⌘K コマンドパレット、C タスクを作成、⇧⌘S 今すぐ同期、Enter 下に追加（ツリー）、Tab / ⇧Tab 字下げ・戻す、⌥↑↓ 並べ替え、Space 完了、S ステータス、K 種類、⌘↵ 詳細を開く・作成。Windows と Linux では ⌘ を Ctrl にする
- 保存は入力が 2 秒止まったとき、またはフォーカスが外れたときに `PATCH`。1操作1コミット
- トーストは左下に 6 秒、取り消し付きは 10 秒。取り消しにくい操作だけ確認ダイアログ。undo は確認しない
- ダイアログとフィルタ欄の足元には同じ操作の `hh` コマンドを出し、コピーできるようにする
- `svelte-check` は警告ゼロ、`biome ci` は指摘ゼロ
- spec・設計と違う挙動にしたくなったら、実装を止めて報告する（自分で決めない）
- Go のテストは `-race` を含めて通す。Go のジョブは `web/dist` なし（`.gitkeep` だけ）で通る

---

### Task 7: 見た目の基準と画面の骨組み

Foundations のトークン・アイコン・部品（`ui/*`）と、画面の骨組み（`App.svelte`、router、keys、トースト、選択）を作る。サイドバー（Task 8）、ツリー・詳細（Task 9・11）、状態の画面（Task 15）は、この Task が置く目印のコメント（`<!-- sidebar -->`・`<!-- banners -->`・`<!-- routes -->`）と `<Toasts />` を手がかりに `App.svelte` を Modify する。この Task では、ルートごとの仮の部品（見出しだけの画面）は置かない。

**Files:**
- Create: `web/app/styles/tokens.css`
- Create: `web/app/styles/base.css`
- Create: `web/app/icons/Icon.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/icons/StatusIcon.svelte`

- Create: `web/app/ui/Chip.svelte`

- Create: `web/app/ui/Avatar.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/ui/Dialog.svelte`

- Modify: `web/app/App.svelte`（Task 2 の最小版を置き換える）
- Modify: `web/app/main.ts`（Task 2 の最小版を置き換える）
- Test: `web/app/icons/icons.test.ts`、`web/app/ui/ui.test.ts`、`web/app/ui/dialog.test.ts`、`web/app/ui/menu.test.ts`、`web/app/router.test.ts`、`web/app/keys.test.ts`、`web/app/toast.test.ts`、`web/app/svelte-store.svelte.test.ts`、`web/app/core-factory.test.ts`、`web/app/App.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes（Task 3〜6）: `takeToken`、`createApi`、`Api`、`unwrap`、`errorMessage`、`Connection`、`TaskCache`、`SaveController`、`StoreDirectory`、`SchemaCache`、`StoreTone`、`initials`・`avatarTone`（plan 前半の「共通の型と関数」のとおり）。Task 3 の `takeToken` は、ハッシュに `#token=` がなければ `sessionStorage` に残っているトークンを返し、どちらにもなければ `null` を返す。Task 2 の `web/index.html` には `<div id="app"></div>` がある
- Produces: 次の表がこの Task の最終の名前。ほかのタスクが別の名前を仮定しているときは、この表に合わせて直す（Task 9・10・14 の仮定との対応は、表のあとにまとめた）

**`web/app/styles/tokens.css` の変数（すべて）**

| 区分 | 変数 |
|---|---|
| 色（Foundations の16） | `--ink` `--ink-2` `--muted` `--line` `--line-input` `--sunken` `--sidebar` `--surface` `--accent` `--accent-ink` `--accent-weak` `--success` `--danger` `--danger-weak` `--warning` `--warning-weak` |
| 色（デザインが使う補助） | `--muted-2`（`#8B8B93`）`--amber`（`#A16207`）`--icon` `--label` `--hover` `--line-soft` `--line-tree` `--accent-faint` `--accent-on` `--success-weak` `--success-ink` `--danger-ink` `--pending` `--alert-on-dark` |
| 面・影 | `--scrim`（ダイアログの背後）`--shadow-card` `--shadow-popover` `--shadow-dialog` `--shadow-toast` |
| Store の色（8 色 × 文字・背景・ドット） | `--store-{teal,orange,purple,sky,pink,olive,brown,readonly}-{fg,bg,dot}` |
| アバター（4 組） | `--avatar-{0,1,2,3}-{bg,fg}` |
| 書体 | `--font-sans` `--font-mono` |
| 文字の大きさ | `--fs-28` `--fs-19` `--fs-17` `--fs-14` `--fs-13` `--fs-12` `--fs-11`、`--fs-mono`（11.5px） |
| 間隔 | `--space-4` `--space-6` `--space-8` `--space-10` `--space-12` `--space-16` `--space-20` `--space-28` |
| 角丸 | `--radius-4`（チップ）`--radius-6`（ボタン）`--radius-8`（入力・カード）`--radius-10`（列・帯）`--radius-12`（パネル）`--radius-14`（ダイアログ）`--radius-pill` |
| 線 | `--hairline`（1px） |
| 高さ・レイアウト | `--control-h`（36px）`--control-h-sm`（28px）`--row-h`（40px）`--sidebar-w`（248px）`--gutter`（28px）`--dialog-top`（44px） |

**`web/app/ui/*`・`web/app/icons/*` の props**

| コンポーネント | props |
|---|---|
| `ui/Button.svelte` | `variant?: "primary" \| "default" \| "confirm" \| "danger" \| "ghost"`（既定 `default`）、`size?: "md" \| "sm"`（既定 `md`。36px と 28px）、`HTMLButtonAttributes` すべて（`type` の既定は `button`）、`children` |
| `ui/Chip.svelte` | `tone?: "neutral" \| "accent" \| "success" \| "warning" \| "danger" \| "orange"`（既定 `neutral`）、`mono?: boolean`、`children` |
| `ui/StoreBadge.svelte` | `store: string`、`tone: StoreTone`、`compact?: boolean` |
| `ui/Avatar.svelte` | `name: string`、`size?: 24 \| 32` |
| `ui/Dialog.svelte` | `open: boolean`、`title: string`、`width: 560 \| 620 \| 760`、`cli?: string`、`onclose: () => void`、`children: Snippet`、`footer?: Snippet`。Esc で閉じる、Tab は中で回る、開いたら本文の最初の入力欄にフォーカス、閉じたら元の要素に戻す。重ねたときは一番上だけが Esc に答える |
| `ui/Menu.svelte` | `anchor: HTMLElement \| null`（この要素の下に `position: fixed` で置く。入り切らなければ上。`null` なら画面の中央寄り）、`items: MenuItem[]`（`MenuItem = { value: string; label: string; disabled?: boolean; hint?: string }`）、`label?: string`（`aria-label`。既定「メニュー」）、`onselect: (value: string) => void`、`onclose: () => void`、`selected?: string`（`value`）、`item?: Snippet<[MenuItem]>`（項目の中身の差し替え）。親は `{#if open}` で出し入れする。↑↓・Home・End・Enter・Space・Esc・Tab、`anchor` の外を押すと閉じる |
| `ui/Toasts.svelte` | なし（`App.svelte` が1つ置く） |
| `ui/CliLine.svelte` | `command: string`（`$ ` なし。表示は `$ <command>`、クリックで `<command>` をコピー） |
| `ui/IdLabel.svelte` | `id: string`、`ulid: string`、`alwaysUlid?: boolean`（タスク詳細は `true`） |
| `icons/StatusIcon.svelte` | `status: string`、`category?: string`（`todo` `doing` `done` `canceled`。なければ名前だけで形を決め、知らない名前は輪）、`color?: string`、`size?: number` |
| `icons/KindIcon.svelte` | `kind: string`、`role?: string`（`container` `work`。知らない種類の基本形）、`size?: number` |
| `icons/Icon.svelte` | `name: IconName`、`size?: number`（既定 16）、`label?: string`（あれば `role="img"`、なければ装飾） |

`IconName` は次のすべて: `search` `settings` `sync` `close` `more` `chevron-right` `chevron-down` `plus` `check` `copy` `lock` `wifi-off` `warning` `spinner` `folder` `folder-plus` `workspace` `filter` `forbid` `checklist` `status`。

**関数と状態**

| ファイル | 公開するもの |
|---|---|
| `app/context.ts` | `AppCore`、`setCore(c)`、`core()`、`CORE_KEY`（テストが context を渡すキー） |
| `app/core-factory.ts` | `createCore(opts: { token: () => string \| null; fetch?: typeof fetch; onUnauthorized?: () => void }): AppCore` |
| `app/svelte-store.ts` | `useSubscribe(source, read): { readonly current: T }` |
| `app/router.ts` | `Route`、`router.route`、`router.go(path, opts?: { replace?: boolean })`（知らないパスは `/` に直す。`/tasks/<ULID>` は Task 11 が足す）、`router.href(r)`、`parseRoute(pathname)`、`hrefOf(r)`、`onLinkClick(e: MouseEvent, path)`（修飾キーなしの左クリックは `go`、⌘/Ctrl/⇧ クリックは `window.open(url, "_blank")`（`noopener` なし。新しいタブに `sessionStorage` が複製されてトークンが引き継がれる）、Alt と左以外のボタンはブラウザに任せる） |
| `app/keys.ts` | `isMac()`、`modKey(e)`、`registerShortcut(combo, fn): () => void`（`"mod+k"` `"c"` `"shift+mod+s"`。修飾キーなしの組み合わせは、入力欄の中とダイアログ（`[aria-modal="true"]`）が開いている間は効かない）、`kbdLabel(combo)`（Mac は `⌘K`、それ以外は `Ctrl+K`） |
| `app/toast.svelte.ts` | `toast({ message, undo?, link? })`、`toasts`（`{ list: ToastItem[] }`）、`dismissToast(id)`、`undoToast(id)`、`resetToasts()`（テスト用）。6 秒、取り消し付きは 10 秒 |
| `app/selection.svelte.ts` | `selection: { readonly ulid: string \| null; set(u: string \| null): void }` |
| `app/palette-state.svelte.ts`（Task 14 が置く） | `palette: { readonly open: boolean }`、`openPalette()`、`closePalette()` |
| `app/create-state.svelte.ts`（Task 13 が置く） | `type CreateRequest = { kind: "task" \| "project"; parent?: string }`（`parent` は ULID）、`createState: { readonly request: CreateRequest \| null }`、`openCreate(init: CreateRequest)`、`closeCreate()` |
| `app/ui/clipboard.ts` | `copyText(text): Promise<boolean>` |
| `app/test-utils.ts` | `emitter()`（`subscribe`・`emit`・`size`）、`fakeCore(over?)`、`renderWithCore(Component, props, over?)`（`core()` を差し替えて描画し、`flushSync` する） |

`App.svelte` の目印: `<!-- sidebar -->`（Task 8）、`<!-- banners -->`（Task 15）、`<!-- routes -->`（Task 9・11 が分岐を足す）、`<Toasts />`（Task 13・14 がこの直前にダイアログ類を足し、Task 15 がゲートの面を足す）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 失敗するテストを書く**

`web/app/icons/icons.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, render } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import Icon from "./Icon.svelte";
import KindIcon from "./KindIcon.svelte";
import StatusIcon, { shapeOf } from "./StatusIcon.svelte";

afterEach(cleanup);

describe("shapeOf", () => {
  it.each([
    ["backlog", "todo", "dashed"],
    ["todo", "todo", "ring"],
    ["on_hold", "todo", "pause"],
    ["in_progress", "doing", "half"],
    ["waiting", "doing", "clock"],
    ["done", "done", "check"],
    ["canceled", "canceled", "cross"],
  ])("%s (%s) は %s の形", (status, category, shape) => {
    expect(shapeOf(status, category as never)).toBe(shape);
  });

  it("Store が足したステータスはカテゴリの形になる", () => {
    expect(shapeOf("review", "doing")).toBe("half");
    expect(shapeOf("triage", "todo")).toBe("ring");
    expect(shapeOf("shipped", "done")).toBe("check");
    expect(shapeOf("wontfix", "canceled")).toBe("cross");
  });
});

describe("StatusIcon", () => {
  it("形を data-shape に出し、色だけに頼らない", () => {
    const { container } = render(StatusIcon, {
      props: { status: "in_progress", category: "doing" },
    });
    const svg = container.querySelector("svg");
    expect(svg?.getAttribute("data-shape")).toBe("half");
    expect(container.querySelectorAll("circle").length).toBe(1);
    expect(container.querySelector("path")?.getAttribute("d")).toBe("M8 4.5A3.5 3.5 0 0 1 8 11.5Z");
  });

  it("Store が足したステータスの color は使い、形はカテゴリのまま", () => {
    const { container } = render(StatusIcon, {
      props: { status: "review", category: "doing", color: "#0b6b61" },
    });
    const svg = container.querySelector("svg") as SVGElement;
    expect(svg.getAttribute("data-shape")).toBe("half");
    expect(svg.style.getPropertyValue("--c")).toBe("#0b6b61");
  });

  it("壊れた color は無視する", () => {
    const { container } = render(StatusIcon, {
      props: { status: "review", category: "doing", color: "red; background: url(x)" },
    });
    expect((container.querySelector("svg") as SVGElement).style.getPropertyValue("--c")).toBe(
      "var(--accent)",
    );
  });
});

describe("KindIcon", () => {
  it("知っている種類の形を出す。バグだけ danger", () => {
    const bug = render(KindIcon, { props: { kind: "bug" } });
    expect(bug.container.querySelector("svg")?.getAttribute("data-kind")).toBe("bug");
    expect(bug.container.querySelector("svg")?.style.getPropertyValue("--k")).toBe("var(--danger)");
  });

  it("知らない種類は role で container / work の基本形", () => {
    const c = render(KindIcon, { props: { kind: "initiative", role: "container" } });
    expect(c.container.querySelector("svg")?.getAttribute("data-kind")).toBe("container");
    cleanup();
    const w = render(KindIcon, { props: { kind: "chore", role: "work" } });
    expect(w.container.querySelector("svg")?.getAttribute("data-kind")).toBe("work");
  });
});

describe("Icon", () => {
  it("label がなければ装飾として隠し、あれば img にする", () => {
    const a = render(Icon, { props: { name: "search" } });
    expect(a.container.querySelector("svg")?.getAttribute("aria-hidden")).toBe("true");
    cleanup();
    const b = render(Icon, { props: { name: "search", label: "検索" } });
    expect(b.getByRole("img", { name: "検索" })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`web/app/router.test.ts`:
```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { hrefOf, onLinkClick, parseRoute, router } from "./router";

beforeEach(() => {
  history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
});

describe("parseRoute", () => {
  it.each([
    ["/", { name: "home" }],
    ["/workspaces/shared/01HWS", { name: "workspace", store: "shared", ulid: "01HWS" }],
    ["/workspaces/org-b%2Fops/01HWS", { name: "workspace", store: "org-b/ops", ulid: "01HWS" }],
    ["/projects/01HPR", { name: "project", ulid: "01HPR" }],
    ["/tasks/01HTK/", { name: "home" }], // the detail is another screen
    ["/nothing/here", { name: "home" }],
    ["/workspaces/shared", { name: "home" }],
  ])("%s", (path, route) => {
    expect(parseRoute(path)).toEqual(route);
  });
});

describe("hrefOf", () => {
  it("parseRoute と往復できる。Store 名の / は符号化する", () => {
    const r = { name: "workspace", store: "org-b/ops", ulid: "01HWS" } as const;
    expect(hrefOf(r)).toBe("/workspaces/org-b%2Fops/01HWS");
    expect(parseRoute(hrefOf(r))).toEqual(r);
    expect(hrefOf({ name: "home" })).toBe("/");
  });
});

describe("router", () => {
  it("go は履歴に積んで route を変える", () => {
    router.go("/projects/01HPR");
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/projects/01HPR");
    expect(router.route).toEqual({ name: "project", ulid: "01HPR" });
  });

  it("知らないパスは / に直す。replace なら履歴を積まない", () => {
    const before = history.length;
    router.go("/unknown", { replace: true });
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/");
    expect(history.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it("クエリとハッシュは捨てる", () => {
    router.go("/projects/01HPR?x=1#y");
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/projects/01HPR");
    expect(location.search).toBe("");
  });
});

describe("onLinkClick", () => {
  it("修飾キーなしの左クリックは router.go にする", () => {
    const e = new MouseEvent("click", { button: 0, cancelable: true });
    onLinkClick(e, "/projects/01HPR");
    expect(e.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/projects/01HPR");
  });

  it("⌘/Ctrl/⇧ クリックは window.open で開き、sessionStorage（トークン）を引き継ぐ", () => {
    const open = vi.spyOn(window, "open").mockReturnValue(null);
    for (const mod of [{ ctrlKey: true }, { metaKey: true }, { shiftKey: true }]) {
      const e = new MouseEvent("click", { button: 0, cancelable: true, ...mod });
      onLinkClick(e, "/projects/01HPR");
      expect(e.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(open).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    // No "noopener": a tab opened with it starts with an empty sessionStorage, and no token.
    expect(open).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`${location.origin}/projects/01HPR`, "_blank");
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/");
    open.mockRestore();
  });

  it("Alt クリックと左以外のボタンはブラウザに任せる", () => {
    for (const init of [{ altKey: true, button: 0 }, { button: 1 }]) {
      const e = new MouseEvent("click", { cancelable: true, ...init });
      onLinkClick(e, "/projects/01HPR");
      expect(e.defaultPrevented).toBe(false);
    }
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/");
  });
});
```

`web/app/keys.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { isMac, kbdLabel, modKey, registerShortcut } from "./keys";

function press(init: KeyboardEventInit, target: EventTarget = window) {
  const e = new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, ...init });
  target.dispatchEvent(e);
  return e;
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  document.body.innerHTML = "";
});

describe("isMac / modKey", () => {
  it("Mac は ⌘、ほかは Ctrl", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("MacIntel");
    expect(isMac()).toBe(true);
    expect(modKey(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { metaKey: true }))).toBe(true);
    expect(modKey(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { ctrlKey: true }))).toBe(false);
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("Win32");
    expect(isMac()).toBe(false);
    expect(modKey(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { ctrlKey: true }))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("kbdLabel", () => {
  it("Mac は記号、ほかは Ctrl+", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("MacIntel");
    expect(kbdLabel("mod+k")).toBe("⌘K");
    expect(kbdLabel("shift+mod+s")).toBe("⇧⌘S");
    expect(kbdLabel("mod+enter")).toBe("⌘↵");
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("Linux x86_64");
    expect(kbdLabel("mod+k")).toBe("Ctrl+K");
    expect(kbdLabel("shift+mod+s")).toBe("Ctrl+Shift+S");
  });
});

describe("registerShortcut", () => {
  it("mod+k は Ctrl+K で呼ばれ、解除すると呼ばれない", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("Linux x86_64");
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const off = registerShortcut("mod+k", fn);
    const e = press({ key: "k", ctrlKey: true });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(e.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    press({ key: "k" });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    off();
    press({ key: "k", ctrlKey: true });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("shift+mod+s は Shift が要る", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("Linux x86_64");
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const off = registerShortcut("shift+mod+s", fn);
    press({ key: "s", ctrlKey: true });
    expect(fn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    press({ key: "S", ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    off();
  });

  it("修飾キーなしのショートカットは入力欄の中では効かない", () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const off = registerShortcut("c", fn);
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    document.body.append(input);
    press({ key: "c" }, input);
    expect(fn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    press({ key: "c" });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    off();
  });

  it("修飾キーなしのショートカットはダイアログが開いている間は効かない。⌘ 付きは効く", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("Linux x86_64");
    const plain = vi.fn();
    const withMod = vi.fn();
    const offA = registerShortcut("c", plain);
    const offB = registerShortcut("mod+k", withMod);
    const dlg = document.createElement("div");
    dlg.setAttribute("aria-modal", "true");
    document.body.append(dlg);
    press({ key: "c" });
    press({ key: "k", ctrlKey: true });
    expect(plain).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(withMod).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    offA();
    offB();
  });
});
```

`web/app/toast.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { dismissToast, resetToasts, toast, toasts, undoToast } from "./toast.svelte";

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  resetToasts();
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("toast", () => {
  it("取り消しなしは 6 秒で消える", () => {
    toast({ message: "保存しました" });
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(1);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(5999);
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(1);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("取り消し付きは 10 秒で消える", () => {
    toast({ message: "削除しました", undo: () => {} });
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(9999);
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(1);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("undoToast は取り消しを呼んで閉じる", async () => {
    const undo = vi.fn();
    toast({ message: "削除しました", undo });
    await undoToast(toasts.list[0]?.id ?? 0);
    expect(undo).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("取り消しが失敗したらエラーの文言をトーストに出す", async () => {
    toast({
      message: "削除しました",
      undo: () => {
        throw new Error("boom");
      },
    });
    await undoToast(toasts.list[0]?.id ?? 0);
    expect(toasts.list.map((t) => t.message)).toEqual(["操作に失敗しました: boom"]);
  });

  it("dismissToast は指定の1つだけ閉じる", () => {
    toast({ message: "a" });
    toast({ message: "b" });
    dismissToast(toasts.list[0]?.id ?? 0);
    expect(toasts.list.map((t) => t.message)).toEqual(["b"]);
  });
});
```

`web/app/svelte-store.svelte.test.ts`:
```ts
import { flushSync } from "svelte";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { useSubscribe } from "./svelte-store";
import { emitter } from "./test-utils";

describe("useSubscribe", () => {
  it("core の通知で読み直し、購読は不要になれば外す", async () => {
    const e = emitter();
    let n = 0;
    const store = useSubscribe({ subscribe: e.subscribe }, () => n);
    const seen: number[] = [];
    const stop = $effect.root(() => {
      $effect(() => {
        seen.push(store.current);
      });
    });
    flushSync();
    n = 1;
    e.emit();
    flushSync();
    expect(seen).toEqual([0, 1]);
    expect(e.size()).toBe(1);
    stop();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(e.size()).toBe(0));
  });
});
```

`web/app/ui/ui.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { createRawSnippet } from "svelte";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { resetToasts, toast, toasts } from "../toast.svelte";
import Avatar from "./Avatar.svelte";
import Button from "./Button.svelte";
import Chip from "./Chip.svelte";
import CliLine from "./CliLine.svelte";
import IdLabel from "./IdLabel.svelte";
import StoreBadge from "./StoreBadge.svelte";
import Toasts from "./Toasts.svelte";

const label = (text: string) => createRawSnippet(() => ({ render: () => `<span>${text}</span>` }));
const writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);

beforeEach(() => {
  writeText.mockClear();
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", { value: { writeText }, configurable: true });
  resetToasts();
});
afterEach(cleanup);

describe("Button", () => {
  it("variant と size を属性に出し、押すと onclick が呼ばれる", async () => {
    const onclick = vi.fn();
    const { getByRole } = render(Button, {
      props: { variant: "primary", size: "sm", onclick, children: label("作成") },
    });
    const b = getByRole("button", { name: "作成" });
    expect(b.getAttribute("data-variant")).toBe("primary");
    expect(b.getAttribute("data-size")).toBe("sm");
    expect(b.getAttribute("type")).toBe("button");
    await fireEvent.click(b);
    expect(onclick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("disabled は無効の属性を持つ", () => {
    const { getByRole } = render(Button, { props: { disabled: true, children: label("作成") } });
    expect((getByRole("button") as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("Chip / StoreBadge / Avatar", () => {
  it("Chip は tone を属性に出す", () => {
    const { container } = render(Chip, { props: { tone: "warning", children: label("制約違反") } });
    expect(container.querySelector("[data-tone='warning']")?.textContent).toContain("制約違反");
  });

  it("StoreBadge は Store 名と tone を出す", () => {
    const { container, getByTitle } = render(StoreBadge, {
      props: { store: "shared", tone: "purple" },
    });
    expect(getByTitle("Store: shared").textContent).toContain("shared");
    expect(container.querySelector("[data-tone='purple']")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("Avatar は名前の先頭2文字を大文字で出す", () => {
    const { getByRole } = render(Avatar, { props: { name: "koki" } });
    expect(getByRole("img", { name: "koki" }).textContent).toBe("KO");
  });
});

describe("CliLine", () => {
  it("$ 付きで出し、クリックで $ なしをコピーする", async () => {
    const { getByRole, findByText } = render(CliLine, { props: { command: 'hh add "Fix bug"' } });
    const b = getByRole("button");
    expect(b.textContent).toContain('$ hh add "Fix bug"');
    await fireEvent.click(b);
    expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith('hh add "Fix bug"');
    expect(await findByText("コピーしました")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("IdLabel", () => {
  it("ID を出し、フォーカスで ULID とコピーのボタンを出す", async () => {
    const { getByRole, queryByText, getByText } = render(IdLabel, {
      props: { id: "REL-6", ulid: "01HXYZULID" },
    });
    expect(getByText("REL-6")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(queryByText("01HXYZULID")).toBeNull();
    getByRole("button", { name: /REL-6/ }).focus();
    expect(await findAfter(() => queryByText("01HXYZULID"))).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "ULID をコピー" }));
    expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("01HXYZULID");
  });

  it("alwaysUlid なら常に ULID を出す", () => {
    const { getByText } = render(IdLabel, {
      props: { id: "REL-6", ulid: "01HXYZULID", alwaysUlid: true },
    });
    expect(getByText("01HXYZULID")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("Toasts", () => {
  it("メッセージとリンクと取り消しを出し、取り消しで undo を呼ぶ", async () => {
    const undo = vi.fn();
    const { getByText, getByRole, queryByText } = render(Toasts);
    toast({ message: "REL-9 を作成しました", link: { label: "開く", href: "/tasks/01H" }, undo });
    expect(await findAfter(() => queryByText("REL-9 を作成しました"))).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("開く").getAttribute("href")).toBe("/tasks/01H");
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "取り消し" }));
    expect(undo).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

async function findAfter<T>(read: () => T | null): Promise<T | null> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
    const v = read();
    if (v) return v;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  }
  return null;
}
```

`web/app/ui/dialog.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { createRawSnippet, flushSync } from "svelte";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import Dialog from "./Dialog.svelte";

const body = createRawSnippet(() => ({
  render: () => `<div><input aria-label="名前" /><input aria-label="本文" /></div>`,
}));
const foot = createRawSnippet(() => ({ render: () => `<button type="button">作成</button>` }));

beforeEach(() => {
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", {
    value: { writeText: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) },
    configurable: true,
  });
});
afterEach(cleanup);

function open(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const onclose = vi.fn();
  const r = render(Dialog, {
    props: {
      open: true,
      title: "タスクを作成",
      width: 620,
      onclose,
      children: body,
      footer: foot,
      ...over,
    },
  });
  flushSync();
  return { onclose, ...r };
}

describe("Dialog", () => {
  it("title を見出しにし、role=dialog・aria-modal・aria-labelledby を付ける", () => {
    const { getByRole } = open();
    const d = getByRole("dialog", { name: "タスクを作成" });
    expect(d.getAttribute("aria-modal")).toBe("true");
    expect(getByRole("heading", { name: "タスクを作成" })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("open=false なら何も出さない", () => {
    const { queryByRole } = open({ open: false });
    expect(queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("幅を max-width にする", () => {
    const { getByRole } = open({ width: 760 });
    expect((getByRole("dialog") as HTMLElement).style.maxWidth).toBe("760px");
  });

  it("Esc で閉じる", async () => {
    const { onclose } = open();
    await fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "Escape" });
    expect(onclose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("閉じるボタンで閉じる", async () => {
    const { onclose, getByRole } = open();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "閉じる" }));
    expect(onclose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("開いたら本文の最初の入力欄にフォーカスする", async () => {
    const { getByLabelText } = open();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(document.activeElement).toBe(getByLabelText("名前")));
  });

  it("Tab は末尾から先頭へ、⇧Tab は先頭から末尾へ回り、外へ出ない", async () => {
    const { getByRole, getByLabelText } = open();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(document.activeElement).toBe(getByLabelText("名前")));
    const close = getByRole("button", { name: "閉じる" });
    const last = getByRole("button", { name: "作成" });
    last.focus();
    const tab = await fireEvent.keyDown(last, { key: "Tab" });
    expect(tab).toBe(false); // preventDefault された
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(close);
    const back = await fireEvent.keyDown(close, { key: "Tab", shiftKey: true });
    expect(back).toBe(false);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(last);
  });

  it("cli があれば足元に CliLine を出す", () => {
    const { getByRole } = open({ cli: "hh add Foo" });
    expect(getByRole("button", { name: /hh add Foo/ })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("重ねて開いたときは一番上のダイアログだけが Esc で閉じる", async () => {
    const a = vi.fn();
    const b = vi.fn();
    render(Dialog, { props: { open: true, title: "下", width: 560, onclose: a, children: body } });
    render(Dialog, { props: { open: true, title: "上", width: 560, onclose: b, children: body } });
    flushSync();
    await fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "Escape" });
    expect(b).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(a).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

`web/app/ui/menu.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { createRawSnippet, flushSync } from "svelte";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import Menu from "./Menu.svelte";

afterEach(cleanup);

const items = [
  { value: "todo", label: "未着手" },
  { value: "doing", label: "進行中", hint: "S" },
  { value: "off", label: "無効", disabled: true },
  { value: "done", label: "完了" },
];

function open(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const onselect = vi.fn();
  const onclose = vi.fn();
  const r = render(Menu, {
    props: { anchor: null, items, label: "ステータス", onselect, onclose, ...over },
  });
  flushSync();
  return { onselect, onclose, ...r };
}

const focused = () => document.activeElement?.textContent ?? "";

describe("Menu", () => {
  it("role=menu と項目を出し、最初の有効な項目にフォーカスする", async () => {
    const { getByRole, getAllByRole } = open();
    expect(getByRole("menu", { name: "ステータス" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getAllByRole("menuitem")).toHaveLength(4);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("未着手"));
  });

  it("selected の項目から始める", async () => {
    open({ selected: "done" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("完了"));
  });

  it("↓↑ で無効な項目を飛ばして回る", async () => {
    const { getByRole } = open();
    const menu = getByRole("menu");
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "ArrowDown" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("進行中"));
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "ArrowDown" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("完了"));
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "ArrowDown" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("未着手"));
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "ArrowUp" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("完了"));
  });

  it("Home / End", async () => {
    const { getByRole } = open();
    const menu = getByRole("menu");
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "End" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("完了"));
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "Home" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(focused()).toContain("未着手"));
  });

  it("Enter で選ぶ", async () => {
    const { getByRole, onselect } = open();
    const menu = getByRole("menu");
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "ArrowDown" });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(menu, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(onselect).toHaveBeenCalledWith("doing");
  });

  it("クリックで選ぶ。無効な項目は選べない", async () => {
    const { getByRole, onselect } = open();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("menuitem", { name: /無効/ }));
    expect(onselect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("menuitem", { name: /完了/ }));
    expect(onselect).toHaveBeenCalledWith("done");
  });

  it("Esc と Tab で閉じる", async () => {
    const { getByRole, onclose } = open();
    await fireEvent.keyDown(getByRole("menu"), { key: "Escape" });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(getByRole("menu"), { key: "Tab" });
    expect(onclose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("外側を押すと閉じる。anchor の中は除く", async () => {
    const anchor = document.createElement("button");
    const outside = document.createElement("div");
    document.body.append(anchor, outside);
    const { onclose } = open({ anchor });
    await fireEvent.pointerDown(anchor);
    expect(onclose).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await fireEvent.pointerDown(outside);
    expect(onclose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    anchor.remove();
    outside.remove();
  });

  it("anchor の下に置く", () => {
    const anchor = document.createElement("div");
    anchor.getBoundingClientRect = () =>
      ({ top: 100, bottom: 140, left: 30, right: 200, width: 170, height: 40 }) as DOMRect;
    const { getByRole } = open({ anchor });
    const menu = getByRole("menu") as HTMLElement;
    expect(menu.style.top).toBe("144px");
    expect(menu.style.left).toBe("30px");
  });

  it("item の snippet で項目の中身を差し替えられる", () => {
    const item = createRawSnippet(() => ({ render: () => '<span data-custom="1">★</span>' }));
    const { container } = open({ item });
    expect(container.querySelectorAll("[data-custom]")).toHaveLength(4);
  });
});
```

```css
/* Design tokens. The names follow the Foundations artboard; screens use var(--name) only. */
:root {
  /* Colors (Foundations) */
  --ink: #18181b;
  --ink-2: #3f3f46;
  --muted: #63636b;
  --muted-2: #8b8b93;
  --line: #e4e4df;
  --line-input: #dcdcd6;
  --sunken: #f6f6f3;
  --sidebar: #f4f4f1;
  --surface: #ffffff;
  --accent: #3451d1;
  --accent-ink: #1e2c78;
  --accent-weak: #e3e7f8;
  --success: #15803d;
  --danger: #b91c1c;
  --danger-weak: #fdecec;
  --warning: #854d0e;
  --warning-weak: #fbf0d9;
  --amber: #a16207;

  /* Colors the artboards use beyond the sixteen */
  --icon: #52525b;
  --label: #5f5f66;
  --hover: #f1f1ee;
  --line-soft: #e0e0da;
  --line-tree: #d4d4ce;
  --accent-faint: #f6f7fd;
  --accent-on: #c7d2fe;
  --success-weak: #e7f4ea;
  --success-ink: #14532d;
  --danger-ink: #991b1b;
  --pending: #b45309;
  --alert-on-dark: #fcd34d;
  --scrim: rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.42);
  --shadow-card: 0 1px 2px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.06), 0 8px 24px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.06);
  --shadow-popover: 0 8px 24px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.16);
  --shadow-dialog: 0 24px 60px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.3);
  --shadow-toast: 0 8px 24px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.24);

  /* Store colors: text, background, square dot */
  --store-teal-fg: #0b6b61;
  --store-teal-bg: #e3f3f0;
  --store-teal-dot: #0b6b61;
  --store-orange-fg: #9a4307;
  --store-orange-bg: #fbebdd;
  --store-orange-dot: #9a4307;
  --store-purple-fg: #5b33b8;
  --store-purple-bg: #eee8fb;
  --store-purple-dot: #6b3fd1;
  --store-sky-fg: #075985;
  --store-sky-bg: #e0f2fe;
  --store-sky-dot: #0369a1;
  --store-pink-fg: #9d174d;
  --store-pink-bg: #fce7f3;
  --store-pink-dot: #be185d;
  --store-olive-fg: #3f6212;
  --store-olive-bg: #ecfccb;
  --store-olive-dot: #4d7c0f;
  --store-brown-fg: #78350f;
  --store-brown-bg: #f5ebdd;
  --store-brown-dot: #92400e;
  --store-readonly-fg: #3f3f46;
  --store-readonly-bg: #eeeeec;
  --store-readonly-dot: #8b8b93;

  /* Avatars: four background / text pairs */
  --avatar-0-bg: #dde3fa;
  --avatar-0-fg: #26399a;
  --avatar-1-bg: #fce7d6;
  --avatar-1-fg: #8a3a06;
  --avatar-2-bg: #dcefe2;
  --avatar-2-fg: #14532d;
  --avatar-3-bg: #eee8fb;
  --avatar-3-fg: #5b33b8;

  /* Type */
  --font-sans: "IBM Plex Sans JP", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  --font-mono: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  --fs-28: 28px;
  --fs-19: 19px;
  --fs-17: 17px;
  --fs-14: 14px;
  --fs-13: 13px;
  --fs-12: 12px;
  --fs-11: 11px;
  --fs-mono: 11.5px;

  /* Spacing */
  --space-4: 4px;
  --space-6: 6px;
  --space-8: 8px;
  --space-10: 10px;
  --space-12: 12px;
  --space-16: 16px;
  --space-20: 20px;
  --space-28: 28px;

  /* Radius */
  --radius-4: 4px;
  --radius-6: 6px;
  --radius-8: 8px;
  --radius-10: 10px;
  --radius-12: 12px;
  --radius-14: 14px;
  --radius-pill: 999px;
  --hairline: 1px;

  /* Heights and layout */
  --control-h: 36px;
  --control-h-sm: 28px;
  --row-h: 40px;
  --sidebar-w: 248px;
  --gutter: 28px;
  --dialog-top: 44px;
}
```

`web/app/styles/base.css`:
```css
@import "@fontsource/ibm-plex-sans-jp/400.css";
@import "@fontsource/ibm-plex-sans-jp/500.css";
@import "@fontsource/ibm-plex-sans-jp/600.css";
@import "@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/400.css";
@import "@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/500.css";
@import "./tokens.css";

*,
*::before,
*::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html,
body {
  margin: 0;
  min-height: 100%;
}

body {
  background: var(--surface);
  color: var(--ink);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: var(--fs-13);
  line-height: 1.5;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
}

a {
  color: var(--accent);
}

a:hover {
  color: var(--accent-ink);
}

code,
kbd {
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
}

button,
input,
select,
textarea {
  font: inherit;
  color: inherit;
}

:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
  outline-offset: 1px;
}
```

`web/app/icons/StatusIcon.svelte`:
```svelte
<script lang="ts" module>
  export type StatusShape = "dashed" | "ring" | "pause" | "half" | "clock" | "check" | "cross";

  const byName: Record<string, StatusShape> = {
    backlog: "dashed",
    todo: "ring",
    on_hold: "pause",
    in_progress: "half",
    waiting: "clock",
    done: "check",
    canceled: "cross",
  };

  const byCategory: Record<string, StatusShape> = {
    todo: "ring",
    doing: "half",
    done: "check",
    canceled: "cross",
  };

  /** The default set has its own shapes; a status a store added keeps the shape of its category. */
  export function shapeOf(status: string, category?: string): StatusShape {
    return byName[status] ?? byCategory[category ?? ""] ?? "ring";
  }

  type Def = {
    r: number;
    filled: boolean;
    dash?: string;
    path?: string;
    pathFilled?: boolean;
    c: string;
    c2?: string;
  };

  // Paths and radii are the ones of the Foundations artboard (16px grid, 1.4 stroke).
  const defs: Record<StatusShape, Def> = {
    dashed: { r: 6, filled: false, dash: "2.2 2.2", c: "var(--muted-2)" },
    ring: { r: 6, filled: false, c: "var(--muted)" },
    pause: {
      r: 6,
      filled: false,
      path: "M6.5 5.6v4.8M9.5 5.6v4.8",
      c: "var(--muted-2)",
      c2: "var(--muted)",
    },
    half: {
      r: 6,
      filled: false,
      path: "M8 4.5A3.5 3.5 0 0 1 8 11.5Z",
      pathFilled: true,
      c: "var(--accent)",
    },
    clock: { r: 6, filled: false, path: "M8 5.2V8l2 1.4", c: "var(--amber)" },
    check: { r: 7, filled: true, path: "M5 8.3l2 2 4-4.5", c: "var(--success)" },
    cross: { r: 7, filled: true, path: "M5.9 5.9l4.2 4.2M10.1 5.9l-4.2 4.2", c: "var(--muted-2)" },
  };

  /** A color a store chose is used as is; anything that is not a plain color is ignored. */
  function safeColor(color: string | undefined): string | undefined {
    if (!color) return undefined;
    return /^(#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|[a-z]+|var\(--[a-z0-9-]+\))$/i.test(color) ? color : undefined;
  }
</script>

<script lang="ts">
  type Props = { status: string; category?: string; color?: string; size?: number };
  let { status, category, color, size = 16 }: Props = $props();

  const shape = $derived(shapeOf(status, category));
  const def = $derived(defs[shape]);
  const custom = $derived(safeColor(color));
  const c = $derived(custom ?? def.c);
  const c2 = $derived(custom ?? def.c2 ?? def.c);
  // A filled circle (done, canceled) draws its mark in the surface color.
  const pathStroke = $derived(def.pathFilled ? "none" : def.filled ? "var(--surface)" : c2);
</script>

<svg
  width={size}
  height={size}
  viewBox="0 0 16 16"
  data-shape={shape}
  style:--c={c}
  style:--fill={def.filled ? c : "none"}
  style:--path-fill={def.pathFilled ? c : "none"}
  style:--path-stroke={pathStroke}
  aria-hidden="true"
>
  <circle cx="8" cy="8" r={def.r} stroke-width="1.4" stroke-dasharray={def.dash} />
  {#if def.path}
    <path d={def.path} stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" />
  {/if}
</svg>

<style>
  svg {
    flex: 0 0 auto;
  }
  circle {
    fill: var(--fill);
    stroke: var(--c);
  }
  path {
    fill: var(--path-fill);
    stroke: var(--path-stroke);
  }
</style>
```

`web/app/icons/KindIcon.svelte`:
```svelte
<script lang="ts" module>
  // Paths are the ones of the Foundations artboard (16px grid, 1.4 stroke).
  const kinds: Record<string, string> = {
    project: "M2.5 4.5h4l1.5 1.5h5.5v6.5h-11z",
    epic: "M8 2.5l5.5 2.8L8 8.1 2.5 5.3zM2.5 8.2L8 11l5.5-2.8M2.5 11L8 13.8l5.5-2.8",
    story: "M4.5 2.5h7v11L8 11.2l-3.5 2.3z",
    task: "M3 3h10v10H3zM5.6 8.1l1.7 1.7 3.1-3.4",
    bug: "M5.5 7a2.5 2.5 0 0 1 5 0v3a2.5 2.5 0 0 1-5 0zM8 7v5.5M5.5 9H2.8M13.2 9h-2.7M6 5.2L4.4 3.6M10 5.2l1.6-1.6M5.6 11.6l-1.8 1.4M10.4 11.6l1.8 1.4",
  };

  // A kind a store added: a container gets the stacked shape, work gets a square.
  const containerShape = "M8 2.5l5.5 2.8L8 8.1 2.5 5.3zM2.5 8.2L8 11l5.5-2.8M2.5 11L8 13.8l5.5-2.8";
  const workShape = "M3 3h10v10H3z";
</script>

<script lang="ts">
  type Props = { kind: string; role?: string; size?: number };
  let { kind, role, size = 16 }: Props = $props();

  const known = $derived(kind in kinds);
  const d = $derived(kinds[kind] ?? (role === "container" ? containerShape : workShape));
  const shape = $derived(known ? kind : role === "container" ? "container" : "work");
</script>

<svg
  width={size}
  height={size}
  viewBox="0 0 16 16"
  data-kind={shape}
  style:--k={kind === "bug" ? "var(--danger)" : "var(--icon)"}
  aria-hidden="true"
>
  <path
    {d}
    fill="none"
    stroke="currentColor"
    stroke-width="1.4"
    stroke-linejoin="round"
    stroke-linecap="round"
  />
</svg>

<style>
  svg {
    flex: 0 0 auto;
    color: var(--k);
  }
</style>
```

`web/app/ui/clipboard.ts`:
```ts
/** Copies text to the clipboard; false when the browser refuses. */
export async function copyText(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import type { Snippet } from "svelte";
  import type { HTMLButtonAttributes } from "svelte/elements";

  type Props = HTMLButtonAttributes & {
    variant?: "primary" | "default" | "confirm" | "danger" | "ghost";
    size?: "md" | "sm";
    children: Snippet;
  };
  let { variant = "default", size = "md", type = "button", children, ...rest }: Props = $props();
</script>

<button {type} class="btn" data-variant={variant} data-size={size} {...rest}>

</button>

<style>
  .btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: var(--space-6);
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    border-radius: var(--radius-6);
    font-weight: 500;
    cursor: pointer;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .btn[data-size="md"] {
    min-height: var(--control-h);
    padding: 0 var(--space-16);
    font-size: var(--fs-13);
  }
  .btn[data-size="sm"] {
    min-height: var(--control-h-sm);
    padding: 0 var(--space-12);
    font-size: var(--fs-12);
  }
  .btn[data-variant="primary"] {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--surface);
  }
  .btn[data-variant="default"] {
    background: var(--surface);
    border-color: var(--line-input);
    color: var(--ink);
  }
  .btn[data-variant="confirm"] {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--surface);
  }
  .btn[data-variant="danger"] {
    background: var(--danger);
    color: var(--surface);
  }
  .btn[data-variant="ghost"] {
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--ink-2);
  }
  .btn[data-variant="ghost"]:hover:not(:disabled) {
    background: var(--hover);
  }
  .btn:disabled {
    background: var(--line);
    border-color: transparent;
    color: var(--muted);
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }
</style>
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { onDestroy } from "svelte";
  import { copyText } from "./clipboard";

  let { command }: { command: string } = $props();
  let copied = $state(false);
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

  async function copy() {
    if (!(await copyText(command))) return;
    copied = true;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      copied = false;
    }, 1500);
  }

  onDestroy(() => clearTimeout(timer));
</script>

<button
  type="button"
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { copyText } from "./clipboard";

  type Props = { id: string; ulid: string; alwaysUlid?: boolean };
  let { id, ulid, alwaysUlid = false }: Props = $props();

  let hover = $state(false);
  let focus = $state(false);
  const show = $derived(hover || focus);
  let copied = $state(false);

  async function copy(e: MouseEvent) {
    e.stopPropagation();

      copied = true;
      setTimeout(() => {
        copied = false;
      }, 1500);
```

`web/app/ui/Dialog.svelte`:
```svelte
<script lang="ts" module>
  // Dialogs that are open, the top one last. Only the top one answers Esc and Tab.
  const stack: symbol[] = [];

  const FOCUSABLE =
    'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
</script>

<script lang="ts">
  import { type Snippet, tick } from "svelte";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import CliLine from "./CliLine.svelte";

  type Props = {
    open: boolean;
    title: string;
    width: 560 | 620 | 760;
    cli?: string;
    onclose: () => void;
    children: Snippet;
    footer?: Snippet;
  };
  let { open, title, width, cli, onclose, children, footer }: Props = $props();

  const uid = $props.id();
  const me = Symbol("dialog");
  let panel: HTMLElement | undefined = $state();
  let body: HTMLElement | undefined = $state();

  function focusables(): HTMLElement[] {
    return panel ? [...panel.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)] : [];
  }

  $effect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const opener = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    stack.push(me);
    tick().then(() => {
      const first = body?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? focusables()[0] ?? panel;
      first?.focus();
    });
    return () => {
      const i = stack.indexOf(me);
      if (i >= 0) stack.splice(i, 1);
      opener?.focus?.();
    };
  });

  function onkeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (!open || !panel || stack[stack.length - 1] !== me) return;
    if (e.key === "Escape") {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      onclose();
      return;
    }
    if (e.key !== "Tab") return;
    const items = focusables();
    if (items.length === 0) {
      e.preventDefault();
      panel.focus();
      return;
    }
    const first = items[0];
    const last = items.at(-1);
    if (!first || !last) return;
    const active = document.activeElement;
    if (!panel.contains(active)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    } else if (e.shiftKey && active === first) {
      e.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && active === last) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  }
</script>

<svelte:window {onkeydown} />

{#if open}
  <div class="overlay">
    <div
      class="panel"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="{uid}-title"
      tabindex="-1"
      bind:this={panel}
      style:max-width="{width}px"
    >
      <header class="head">
        <h2 id="{uid}-title">{title}</h2>
        <button type="button" class="close" aria-label="閉じる" onclick={onclose}>
          <Icon name="close" size={16} />
        </button>
      </header>
      <div class="body" bind:this={body}>{@render children()}</div>
      {#if cli || footer}
        <footer class="foot">
          {#if cli}
            <div class="cli"><CliLine command={cli} /></div>
          {/if}
          {#if footer}
            <div class="actions">{@render footer()}</div>
          {/if}
        </footer>
      {/if}
    </div>
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  .overlay {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 100;
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: var(--dialog-top) var(--space-16);
```

`web/app/ui/Menu.svelte`:
```svelte
<script lang="ts" module>
  export type MenuItem = { value: string; label: string; disabled?: boolean; hint?: string };
</script>

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount, type Snippet, tick } from "svelte";

  type Props = {
    anchor: HTMLElement | null;
    items: MenuItem[];
    label?: string;
    onselect: (value: string) => void;
    onclose: () => void;
    selected?: string;
    item?: Snippet<[MenuItem]>;
  };
  let { anchor, items, label = "メニュー", onselect, onclose, selected, item }: Props = $props();

  let root: HTMLElement | undefined = $state();
  let active = $state(0);
  let pos = $state({ top: 0, left: 0 });

  function enabled(i: number): boolean {
    return !items[i]?.disabled;
  }

  function firstEnabled(): number {
    const i = items.findIndex((it) => !it.disabled);
    return Math.max(i, 0);
  }

  function lastEnabled(): number {
    for (let i = items.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (enabled(i)) return i;
    return 0;
  }

  function step(from: number, dir: 1 | -1): number {
    for (let n = 1; n <= items.length; n++) {
      const i = (from + dir * n + items.length * n) % items.length;
      if (enabled(i)) return i;
    }
    return from;
  }

  // Below the anchor, or above it when there is no room; centered when there is no anchor.
  function place() {
    if (!root) return;
    const h = root.offsetHeight;
    const w = root.offsetWidth;
    if (!anchor) {
      pos = {
        top: Math.max(0, (window.innerHeight - h) / 3),
        left: Math.max(0, (window.innerWidth - w) / 2),
      };
      return;
    }
    const r = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
    let top = r.bottom + 4;
    if (top + h > window.innerHeight && r.top - 4 - h >= 0) top = r.top - 4 - h;
    pos = { top, left: Math.max(0, Math.min(r.left, window.innerWidth - w)) };
  }

  async function focusActive() {
    await tick();
    root?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[role="menuitem"]')[active]?.focus();
  }

  function move(i: number) {
    active = i;
    focusActive();
  }

  onMount(() => {
    place();
    const at = items.findIndex((it) => it.value === selected && !it.disabled);
    active = at >= 0 ? at : firstEnabled();
    focusActive();
  });

  function choose(i: number) {
    const it = items[i];
    if (!it || it.disabled) return;
    onselect(it.value);
  }

  function onkeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    switch (e.key) {
      case "ArrowDown":
        e.preventDefault();
        move(step(active, 1));
        break;
      case "ArrowUp":
        e.preventDefault();
        move(step(active, -1));
        break;
      case "Home":
        e.preventDefault();
        move(firstEnabled());
        break;
      case "End":
        e.preventDefault();
        move(lastEnabled());
        break;
      case "Enter":
      case " ":
        e.preventDefault();
        choose(active);
        break;
      case "Escape":
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        onclose();
        break;
      case "Tab":
        onclose();
        break;
    }
  }

  function onwindowpointerdown(e: PointerEvent) {
    const t = e.target as Node | null;
    if (!root || root.contains(t) || anchor?.contains(t)) return;
    onclose();
  }
</script>

<svelte:window onpointerdown={onwindowpointerdown} />

<div
  class="menu"
  role="menu"
  aria-label={label}
  tabindex="-1"
  bind:this={root}
  style:top="{pos.top}px"
  style:left="{pos.left}px"
  {onkeydown}
>
  {#each items as it, i (it.value)}
    <button
      type="button"
      role="menuitem"
      class="item"
      class:selected={it.value === selected}
      tabindex={i === active ? 0 : -1}
      disabled={it.disabled}
      onclick={() => choose(i)}
      onmouseenter={() => {
        if (!it.disabled) active = i;
      }}
    >
      {#if item}
        {@render item(it)}
      {:else}
        <span class="label">{it.label}</span>
      {/if}
      {#if it.hint}
        <span class="hint">{it.hint}</span>
      {/if}
    </button>
  {/each}
</div>

<style>
  .menu {
    position: fixed;
    z-index: 120;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-width: 180px;
    max-height: calc(100vh - 2 * var(--space-16));
    padding: var(--space-4);
    overflow-y: auto;
    border-radius: var(--radius-8);
    background: var(--surface);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-popover);
  }
  .item {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    min-height: var(--control-h-sm);
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    border: 0;
    border-radius: var(--radius-6);
    background: transparent;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .item:hover:not(:disabled),
  .item:focus-visible {
    background: var(--hover);
    outline: none;
  }
  .item.selected {
    background: var(--accent-weak);
    color: var(--accent-ink);
  }
  .item:disabled {
    color: var(--muted-2);
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }
  .label {
    flex: 1;
  }
  .hint {
    color: var(--label);
    font-size: var(--fs-11);
  }
</style>
```

`web/app/toast.svelte.ts`:
```ts
import { errorMessage } from "../core/messages";

export type ToastInput = {
  message: string;
  undo?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  link?: { label: string; href: string };
};
export type ToastItem = ToastInput & { id: number };

const SHORT_MS = 6000;
const UNDO_MS = 10000;

export const toasts = $state<{ list: ToastItem[] }>({ list: [] });

let seq = 0;
const timers = new Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

export function toast(t: ToastInput): void {
  const id = ++seq;
  toasts.list = [...toasts.list, { ...t, id }];
  timers.set(
    id,
    setTimeout(() => dismissToast(id), t.undo ? UNDO_MS : SHORT_MS),
  );
}

export function dismissToast(id: number): void {
  clearTimeout(timers.get(id));
  timers.delete(id);
  toasts.list = toasts.list.filter((t) => t.id !== id);
}

/** Runs the undo of a toast and closes it; a failure becomes a toast of its own. */
export async function undoToast(id: number): Promise<void> {
  const t = toasts.list.find((x) => x.id === id);
  dismissToast(id);
  if (!t?.undo) return;
  try {
    await t.undo();
  } catch (e) {
    toast({ message: errorMessage(e) });
  }
}

/** For tests: drops every toast and timer. */
export function resetToasts(): void {
  for (const t of timers.values()) clearTimeout(t);
  timers.clear();
  toasts.list = [];
}
```

`web/app/context.ts`:
```ts
import { getContext, setContext } from "svelte";
import type { Api } from "../core/api/client";
import type { TaskCache } from "../core/cache";
import type { Connection } from "../core/connection";
import type { SaveController } from "../core/save";
import type { SchemaCache } from "../core/schema";
import type { StoreDirectory } from "../core/stores";

export type AppCore = {
  api: Api;
  conn: Connection;
  cache: TaskCache;
  save: SaveController;
  stores: StoreDirectory;
  schema: SchemaCache;
};

/** The key of the context; tests pass it to render to replace the core. */
export const CORE_KEY = Symbol("hedgehug.core");

export function setCore(c: AppCore): void {
  setContext(CORE_KEY, c);
}

export function core(): AppCore {
  const c = getContext<AppCore | undefined>(CORE_KEY);
  if (!c) throw new Error("core() was called outside the App");
  return c;
}
```

`web/app/core-factory.ts`:
```ts
import { createApi } from "../core/api/client";
import { TaskCache } from "../core/cache";
import { Connection } from "../core/connection";
import { SaveController } from "../core/save";
import { SchemaCache } from "../core/schema";
import { StoreDirectory } from "../core/stores";
import type { AppCore } from "./context";

/** Builds the one set of core objects the whole app shares. */
export function createCore(opts: {
  token: () => string | null;
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
  onUnauthorized?: () => void;
}): AppCore {
  const api = createApi(opts);
  const conn = new Connection({ token: opts.token, fetch: opts.fetch, api });
  const cache = new TaskCache({ api, conn });
  const save = new SaveController({ api, cache, conn });
  const stores = new StoreDirectory({ api, conn, cache });
  const schema = new SchemaCache({ api, conn });
  return { api, conn, cache, save, stores, schema };
}
```

`web/app/svelte-store.ts`:
```ts
import { createSubscriber } from "svelte/reactivity";

/**
 * Reads a core object from runes. The read re-runs whenever the object notifies,
 * and the subscription lives only while something reads it.
 */
export function useSubscribe<T>(
  source: { subscribe(fn: () => void): () => void },
  read: () => T,
): { readonly current: T } {
  const subscribe = createSubscriber((update) => source.subscribe(update));
  return {
    get current(): T {
      subscribe();
      return read();
    },
  };
}
```

`web/app/router.ts`:
```ts
import { createSubscriber } from "svelte/reactivity";

export type Route =
  | { name: "home" }
  | { name: "workspace"; store: string; ulid: string }
  | { name: "project"; ulid: string };

function decode(s: string): string {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(s);
  } catch {
    return s;
  }
}

/** The route of a path, or null when the path is none of ours. */
function matchRoute(pathname: string): Route | null {
  const seg = pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean).map(decode);
  const [head, a, b] = seg;
  if (head === undefined) return { name: "home" };
  if (head === "workspaces" && seg.length === 3 && a && b) {
    return { name: "workspace", store: a, ulid: b };
  }
  if (head === "projects" && seg.length === 2 && a) return { name: "project", ulid: a };
  return null;
}

/** Unknown paths are the home route. */
export function parseRoute(pathname: string): Route {
  return matchRoute(pathname) ?? { name: "home" };
}

export function hrefOf(r: Route): string {
  switch (r.name) {
    case "home":
      return "/";
    case "workspace":
      return `/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(r.store)}/${encodeURIComponent(r.ulid)}`;
    case "project":
      return `/projects/${encodeURIComponent(r.ulid)}`;
  }
}

const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const subscribe = createSubscriber((update) => {
  listeners.add(update);
  window.addEventListener("popstate", update);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(update);
    window.removeEventListener("popstate", update);
  };
});

let last: { path: string; route: Route } | null = null;

export const router = {
  get route(): Route {
    subscribe();
    const path = location.pathname;
    if (last?.path !== path) last = { path, route: parseRoute(path) };
    return last.route;
  },

  /** Moves to a path of ours; anything else goes to "/". Query and hash are dropped. */
  go(path: string, opts: { replace?: boolean } = {}): void {
    const url = new URL(path, location.origin);
    const route = matchRoute(url.pathname);
    const target = route ? hrefOf(route) : "/";
    if (target !== location.pathname || location.search !== "" || location.hash !== "") {
      if (opts.replace) history.replaceState(null, "", target);
      else history.pushState(null, "", target);
    }
    for (const fn of listeners) fn();
  },

  href(r: Route): string {
    return hrefOf(r);
  },
};

/** A link click that stays in the app; with ⌘/Ctrl/⇧ the page opens in a new tab that keeps the token. */
export function onLinkClick(e: MouseEvent, path: string): void {
  if (e.defaultPrevented || e.button !== 0 || e.altKey) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey) {
    // The token is in sessionStorage, which is per tab. A tab the browser opens from a link
    // starts without it ("open it from hh ui" screen); window.open without noopener copies it.
    window.open(new URL(path, location.origin).href, "_blank");
    return;
  }
  router.go(path);
}
```

`web/app/keys.ts`:
```ts
export function isMac(): boolean {
  return /Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform || navigator.userAgent);
}

/** ⌘ on a Mac, Ctrl elsewhere. */
export function modKey(e: KeyboardEvent): boolean {
  return isMac() ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
}

type Combo = { key: string; mod: boolean; shift: boolean; alt: boolean };

function parse(combo: string): Combo {
  const parts = combo.toLowerCase().split("+");
  return {
    key: parts.at(-1) ?? "",
    mod: parts.includes("mod"),
    shift: parts.includes("shift"),
    alt: parts.includes("alt"),
  };
}

function keyOf(e: KeyboardEvent): string {
  return e.key === " " ? "space" : e.key.toLowerCase();
}

function matches(e: KeyboardEvent, c: Combo): boolean {
  const mac = isMac();
  const mod = mac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
  const other = mac ? e.ctrlKey : e.metaKey;
  return (
    keyOf(e) === c.key && mod === c.mod && !other && e.shiftKey === c.shift && e.altKey === c.alt
  );
}

function editable(t: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  const el = t as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!el?.tagName) return false;
  return el.isContentEditable === true || ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(el.tagName);
}

type Entry = { combo: Combo; fn: (e: KeyboardEvent) => void };
const entries = new Set<Entry>();

function onKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent): void {
  if (e.isComposing) return;
  for (const en of entries) {
    if (!matches(e, en.combo)) continue;
    // A key without ⌘/Ctrl is for typing in a field or a dialog.
    if (!en.combo.mod && (editable(e.target) || document.querySelector('[aria-modal="true"]'))) {
      continue;
    }
    e.preventDefault();
    en.fn(e);
    return;
  }
}

/** Registers a shortcut such as "mod+k", "c" or "shift+mod+s"; returns the function that removes it. */
export function registerShortcut(combo: string, fn: (e: KeyboardEvent) => void): () => void {
  const entry: Entry = { combo: parse(combo), fn };
  if (entries.size === 0) window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeydown);
  entries.add(entry);
  return () => {
    entries.delete(entry);
    if (entries.size === 0) window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeydown);
  };
}

const symbols: Record<string, string> = {
  enter: "↵",
  arrowup: "↑",
  arrowdown: "↓",
  space: "Space",
};

/** "mod+k" as the label of the key: ⌘K on a Mac, Ctrl+K elsewhere. */
export function kbdLabel(combo: string): string {
  const c = parse(combo);
  const key = symbols[c.key] ?? c.key.toUpperCase();
  if (isMac()) return `${c.alt ? "⌥" : ""}${c.shift ? "⇧" : ""}${c.mod ? "⌘" : ""}${key}`;
  return [c.mod && "Ctrl", c.alt && "Alt", c.shift && "Shift", key].filter(Boolean).join("+");
}
```

`web/app/test-utils.ts`:
```ts
import { render } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { type Component, flushSync } from "svelte";
import { type AppCore, CORE_KEY } from "./context";

/** A minimal subscribe / emit pair for fakes of the core objects. */
export function emitter() {
  const fns = new Set<() => void>();
  return {
    subscribe(fn: () => void): () => void {
      fns.add(fn);
      return () => {
        fns.delete(fn);
      };
    },
    emit(): void {
      for (const fn of [...fns]) fn();
    },
    size(): number {
      return fns.size;
    },
  };
}

/** A core made of fakes; `over` replaces any of the six objects. */
export function fakeCore(over: Partial<Record<keyof AppCore, unknown>> = {}): AppCore {
  const e = emitter();
  const base = {
    api: {},
    conn: {
      state: "open",
      lastOpenAt: null,
      sync: new Map(),
      unread: 0,
      subscribe: e.subscribe,
      on: () => () => {},
      start() {},
      stop() {},
    },
    cache: { subscribe: e.subscribe },
    save: { subscribe: e.subscribe },
    stores: {
      stores: () => [],
      tone: () => "teal",
      workspaces: () => [],
      workspace: () => undefined,
      loaded: () => true,
      reload: async () => {},
      subscribe: e.subscribe,
    },
    schema: { subscribe: e.subscribe },
  };
  return { ...base, ...over } as unknown as AppCore;
}

/** Renders a component with a fake core in its context. */
export function renderWithCore<P extends Record<string, unknown>>(
  C: Component<P>,
  props: P,
  over: Partial<Record<keyof AppCore, unknown>> = {},
) {
  const c = fakeCore(over);
  const r = render(C as Component<Record<string, unknown>>, {
    props,
    context: new Map([[CORE_KEY, c]]),
  });
  flushSync(); // run the effects and onMount callbacks before the test looks
  return { core: c, ...r };
}
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from "svelte";
  import { takeToken } from "../core/token";
  import { setCore } from "./context";
  import { createCore } from "./core-factory";
  import { router } from "./router";
  import Toasts from "./ui/Toasts.svelte";

  const token = takeToken(location, sessionStorage, history);
  const c = createCore({ token: () => token });
  setCore(c);

  onMount(() => {
    router.go(location.pathname, { replace: true });
    c.conn.start();
    return () => c.conn.stop();
  });
```

---

### Task 8: サイドバー

Sidebar.dc.html のうち、4d-3 の範囲（ロゴとホスト、ワークスペース › プロジェクトの入れ子、同期のカード）だけを作る。検索の欄（⌘K）は Task 14（コマンドパレット）、「プロジェクトを追加」は Task 13（作成ダイアログ）が足す。押しても何も起きないボタンやキーを、まだない画面のために残さないため。利用者の決定 2 のとおり、マイタスク・通知・アクティビティ・保存済みビュー・ワークスペースの「…」メニュー・設定は出さない。

**Files:**
- Create: `web/app/sidebar/Sidebar.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/sidebar/SyncCard.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/sidebar/sync-summary.ts`
- Create: `web/app/online.ts`
- Modify: `web/app/App.svelte`（`<!-- sidebar -->` を `<Sidebar />` にする）
- Test: `web/app/sidebar/sync-summary.test.ts`、`web/app/sidebar/SyncCard.test.ts`、`web/app/sidebar/Sidebar.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core()`（`stores: StoreDirectory`、`conn: Connection`、`api`）、`useSubscribe`、`router`・`onLinkClick`、`registerShortcut`・`kbdLabel`、`toast`、`unwrap`、`errorMessage`、`formatRelative`、`StoreBadge`・`KindIcon`・`Icon`・`Button`
- Produces:
  - `app/online.ts`: `online: { readonly current: boolean }`（`navigator.onLine` を `online`・`offline` イベントで購読する）
  - `app/sidebar/sync-summary.ts`: `summarizeSync(statuses: Iterable<SyncStatus>, online: boolean): SyncSummary`、`syncMessage(reports: SyncReport[] | null): string`（Task 15 の `Banners` も使う）

同期のカードの4状態は、`Connection.sync`（Store ごとの `SyncStatus`）から全 Store を合わせて次の規則で決める。

1. 数えるのは remote を持つ Store（`remotes` が空でないもの）だけ。ひとつもなければ「同期済み」で、補足に「remote なし」と出す
2. `online`（ブラウザの `navigator.onLine`）が偽なら **オフライン**
3. そうでなく、どれかの Store に `last_error` か `error` があれば **push できません**（最初の1件の Store 名と文言を出す）
4. そうでなく、未 push の合計（`unpushed`）が 1 以上、または `pending_since` があれば **送信待ち**（右に `push_at` の最も早いものまでの残りを「n 秒後に push」「n 分後に push」、過ぎていれば「まもなく push」と出す）
5. それ以外は **同期済み**

最終同期は `last_success` の最も新しいもの、次の fetch は `next_fetch` の最も早いもの、push の時刻は `push_at`（Task 1 が足す。同期ループが次に push する時刻）の最も早いもの。「オフライン」は `navigator.onLine` が偽のときだけ（細部 37）で、リモートとの通信の失敗は「push できません」として `last_error` を出す。

- [ ] **Step 1: 失敗するテストを書く**

`web/app/sidebar/sync-summary.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { SyncStatus } from "../../core/connection";
import { summarizeSync, syncMessage } from "./sync-summary";

const st = (over: Partial<SyncStatus> & { store: string }): SyncStatus => ({
  remotes: ["origin"],
  ...over,
});

describe("summarizeSync", () => {
  it("remote のある Store がなければ同期済みで noRemote", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync([st({ store: "me", remotes: [] })], true);
    expect(s.kind).toBe("synced");
    expect(s.noRemote).toBe(true);
  });

  it("未 push がなくエラーもなければ同期済み。最終同期は一番新しいもの", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync(
      [
        st({
          store: "a",
          unpushed: 0,
          last_success: "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z",
          next_fetch: "2026-11-02T09:05:00Z",
        }),
        st({
          store: "b",
          unpushed: 0,
          last_success: "2026-11-02T09:02:00Z",
          next_fetch: "2026-11-02T09:04:00Z",
        }),
      ],
      true,
    );
    expect(s).toMatchObject({
      kind: "synced",
      noRemote: false,
      unpushed: 0,
      lastSuccess: "2026-11-02T09:02:00Z",
      nextFetch: "2026-11-02T09:04:00Z",
    });
  });

  it("未 push があれば送信待ち。合計と一番古い最初の変更を出す", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync(
      [
        st({
          store: "a",
          unpushed: 2,
          pending_since: "2026-11-02T09:01:00Z",
          push_at: "2026-11-02T09:03:00Z",
        }),
        st({
          store: "b",
          unpushed: 1,
          pending_since: "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z",
          push_at: "2026-11-02T09:02:00Z",
        }),
      ],
      true,
    );
    expect(s).toMatchObject({
      kind: "pending",
      unpushed: 3,
      pendingSince: "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z",
      pushAt: "2026-11-02T09:02:00Z",
    });
  });

  it("last_error があれば push できません。Store 名と文言を持つ", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync(
      [
        st({ store: "a", unpushed: 1, last_error: "push rejected" }),
        st({ store: "b", unpushed: 0 }),
      ],
      true,
    );
    expect(s.kind).toBe("error");
    expect(s.errors).toEqual([{ store: "a", message: "push rejected" }]);
  });

  it("読めない Store の error も push できませんにする", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync([st({ store: "a", error: "cannot read store" })], true);
    expect(s.kind).toBe("error");
    expect(s.errors[0]?.message).toBe("cannot read store");
  });

  it("オフラインはエラーや未 push より優先する", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync([st({ store: "a", unpushed: 5, last_error: "x" })], false);
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ kind: "offline", unpushed: 5 });
  });

  it("remote のない Store の未 push は数えない", () => {
    const s = summarizeSync(
      [st({ store: "me", remotes: [], unpushed: 4 }), st({ store: "a", unpushed: 0 })],
      true,
    );
    expect(s.unpushed).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("syncMessage", () => {
  const rep = (over: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
    store: "a",
    merges: 0,
    pushed: [],
    overwritten: [],
    repaired: [],
    invalid: [],
    notices: [],
    warnings: [],
    ...over,
  });

  it("失敗は Store 名と文言を出す", () => {
    expect(syncMessage([rep({ error: "auth failed" }) as never])).toBe(
      "同期に失敗しました: a: auth failed",
    );
  });

  it("remote がなければそう言う", () => {
    expect(syncMessage([rep({ no_remote: true }) as never])).toBe("同期する remote がありません");
    expect(syncMessage([])).toBe("同期する remote がありません");
    expect(syncMessage(null)).toBe("同期する remote がありません");
  });

  it("成功はマージの数を添える", () => {
    expect(syncMessage([rep({}) as never])).toBe("同期しました");
    expect(
      syncMessage([rep({ merges: 2 }) as never, rep({ store: "b", merges: 1 }) as never]),
    ).toBe("同期しました · マージ 3 件");
  });
});
```

`web/app/sidebar/SyncCard.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { SyncStatus } from "../../core/connection";
import { renderWithCore } from "../test-utils";
import { resetToasts, toasts } from "../toast.svelte";
import SyncCard from "./SyncCard.svelte";

const status = (over: Partial<SyncStatus> & { store: string }): SyncStatus => ({
  remotes: ["origin"],
  ...over,
});

function mount(statuses: SyncStatus[], post = vi.fn()) {
  const conn = {
    state: "open",
    lastOpenAt: null,
    sync: new Map(statuses.map((s) => [s.store, s])),
    unread: 0,
    subscribe: () => () => {},
    on: () => () => {},
    start() {},
    stop() {},
  };
  return { post, ...renderWithCore(SyncCard, {}, { conn, api: { POST: post } }) };
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({
    toFake: ["setInterval", "clearInterval", "setTimeout", "clearTimeout", "Date"],
  });
  vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-02T09:00:30Z"));
  resetToasts();
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.useRealTimers();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("SyncCard", () => {
  it("同期済み: 相対の最終同期・未 push・次の fetch のカウントダウン", () => {
    const { getByText } = mount([
      status({
        store: "a",
        unpushed: 0,
        last_success: "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z",
        next_fetch: "2026-11-02T09:01:42Z",
      }),
    ]);
    expect(getByText("同期済み")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("0 件")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("1:12")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("次の fetch は 1 秒ごとに進む", async () => {
    const { findByText } = mount([
      status({ store: "a", unpushed: 0, next_fetch: "2026-11-02T09:01:42Z" }),
    ]);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(2000);
    expect(await findByText("1:10")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("送信待ち: 未 push のコミット数と、push までの秒数", () => {
    const { getByText } = mount([
      status({
        store: "a",
        unpushed: 3,
        pending_since: "2026-11-02T08:58:30Z",
        push_at: "2026-11-02T09:00:52Z",
      }),
    ]);
    expect(getByText("送信待ち")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("3 コミット")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("22 秒後に push")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("送信待ち: push の時刻が過ぎていれば「まもなく push」、1 分以上先は分で出す", () => {
    const a = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 1, push_at: "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z" })]);
    expect(a.getByText("まもなく push")).toBeTruthy();
    cleanup();
    const b = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 1, push_at: "2026-11-02T09:02:00Z" })]);
    expect(b.getByText("2 分後に push")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("push できません: Store 名と文言", () => {
    const { getByText } = mount([
      status({ store: "org-a/hh-shared", unpushed: 1, last_error: "push rejected" }),
    ]);
    expect(getByText("push できません")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("org-a/hh-shared: push rejected")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("1 コミット")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("オフライン: navigator.onLine が偽", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "onLine", "get").mockReturnValue(false);
    const { getByText } = mount([
      status({ store: "a", unpushed: 5, last_success: "2026-11-02T00:12:00Z" }),
    ]);
    expect(getByText("オフライン")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("5 コミット")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("今すぐ同期で POST /api/sync を呼び、結果をトーストに出す", async () => {
    const post = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      data: [
        {
          store: "a",
          merges: 1,
          pushed: [],
          overwritten: [],
          repaired: [],
          invalid: [],
          notices: [],
          warnings: [],
        },
      ],
      response: new Response(),
    });
    const { getByRole } = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 0 })], post);
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: /今すぐ同期/ }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(post).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/sync");
    expect(toasts.list[0]?.message).toBe("同期しました · マージ 1 件");
  });

  it("⇧⌘S（Linux は Ctrl+Shift+S）でも同期する", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "platform", "get").mockReturnValue("Linux x86_64");
    const post = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ data: [], response: new Response() });
    mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 0 })], post);
    await fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "S", ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(post).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
  });

  it("同期の API が失敗したらエラーの文言をトーストに出す", async () => {
    const post = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("boom"));
    const { getByRole } = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 0 })], post);
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: /今すぐ同期/ }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(toasts.list).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(toasts.list[0]?.message).toBe("操作に失敗しました: boom");
  });

  it("同期中はボタンを無効にする", async () => {
    let resolve!: (v: unknown) => void;
    const post = vi.fn().mockReturnValue(new Promise((r) => (resolve = r)));
    const { getByRole } = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 0 })], post);
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: /今すぐ同期/ }));
    expect((getByRole("button", { name: /同期中/ }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
    resolve({ data: [], response: new Response() });
  });
});
```

```ts
});
```
