`web/app/online.ts`:
```ts
import { createSubscriber } from "svelte/reactivity";

const subscribe = createSubscriber((update) => {
  window.addEventListener("online", update);
  window.addEventListener("offline", update);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener("online", update);
    window.removeEventListener("offline", update);
  };
});

/** Whether the browser thinks it is online. */
export const online = {
  get current(): boolean {
    subscribe();
    return navigator.onLine;
  },
};
```

`web/app/sidebar/sync-summary.ts`:
```ts
import type { Schemas } from "../../core/api/client";
import type { SyncStatus } from "../../core/connection";

export type SyncReport = Schemas["SyncReport"];
export type SyncKind = "synced" | "pending" | "error" | "offline";
export type SyncSummary = {
  kind: SyncKind;
  noRemote: boolean;
  unpushed: number;
  lastSuccess: string | null;
  nextFetch: string | null;
  pendingSince: string | null;
  pushAt: string | null;
  errors: { store: string; message: string }[];
};

function newest(a: string | null, b: string | undefined): string | null {
  if (!b) return a;
  return a === null || Date.parse(b) > Date.parse(a) ? b : a;
}

function oldest(a: string | null, b: string | undefined): string | null {
  if (!b) return a;
  return a === null || Date.parse(b) < Date.parse(a) ? b : a;
}

/**
 * Folds the sync status of every store into the one state the card shows.
 * Only stores with a remote count. Offline wins over an error, an error over pending.
 */
export function summarizeSync(statuses: Iterable<SyncStatus>, online: boolean): SyncSummary {
  const s: SyncSummary = {
    kind: "synced",
    noRemote: true,
    unpushed: 0,
    lastSuccess: null,
    nextFetch: null,
    pendingSince: null,
    pushAt: null,
    errors: [],
  };
  for (const st of statuses) {
    if ((st.remotes ?? []).length === 0) continue;
    s.noRemote = false;
    s.unpushed += st.unpushed ?? 0;
    s.lastSuccess = newest(s.lastSuccess, st.last_success);
    s.nextFetch = oldest(s.nextFetch, st.next_fetch);
    s.pendingSince = oldest(s.pendingSince, st.pending_since);
    s.pushAt = oldest(s.pushAt, st.push_at);
    const message = st.last_error || st.error;
    if (message) s.errors.push({ store: st.store, message });
  }
  if (!online) s.kind = "offline";
  else if (s.errors.length > 0) s.kind = "error";
  else if (s.unpushed > 0 || s.pendingSince) s.kind = "pending";
  return s;
}

/** The toast that tells how "sync now" went. */
export function syncMessage(reports: SyncReport[] | null): string {
  const rs = reports ?? [];
  const failed = rs.filter((r) => r.error);
  const first = failed[0];
  if (first) {
    const more = failed.length > 1 ? ` ほか ${failed.length - 1} 件` : "";
    return `同期に失敗しました: ${first.store}: ${first.error}${more}`;
  }
  if (rs.length === 0 || rs.every((r) => r.no_remote)) return "同期する remote がありません";
  const merges = rs.reduce((n, r) => n + r.merges, 0);
  return merges > 0 ? `同期しました · マージ ${merges} 件` : "同期しました";
}
```

`web/app/sidebar/SyncCard.svelte`:
```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from "svelte";
  import { unwrap } from "../../core/api/client";
  import { formatRelative } from "../../core/format";
  import { errorMessage } from "../../core/messages";
  import { core } from "../context";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import { kbdLabel, registerShortcut } from "../keys";
  import { online } from "../online";
  import { useSubscribe } from "../svelte-store";
  import { toast } from "../toast.svelte";
  import { summarizeSync, syncMessage } from "./sync-summary";

  const { api, conn } = core();
  const statuses = useSubscribe(conn, () => conn.sync);
  const summary = $derived(summarizeSync(statuses.current.values(), online.current));

  let now = $state(Date.now());
  let busy = $state(false);

  onMount(() => {
    const tick = setInterval(() => {
      now = Date.now();
    }, 1000);
    const off = registerShortcut("shift+mod+s", () => {
      void syncNow();
    });
    return () => {
      clearInterval(tick);
      off();
    };
  });

  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

  function clock(iso: string): string {
    const d = new Date(iso);
    return `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
  }

  function countdown(iso: string): string {
    const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.parse(iso) - now) / 1000));
    return s === 0 ? "まもなく" : `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${pad(s % 60)}`;
  }

  function pushIn(iso: string): string {
    const s = Math.floor((Date.parse(iso) - now) / 1000);
    if (s <= 0) return "まもなく push";
    return s < 60 ? `${s} 秒後に push` : `${Math.ceil(s / 60)} 分後に push`;
  }

  async function syncNow() {
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    try {
      const reports = await unwrap(api.POST("/api/sync"));
      toast({ message: syncMessage(reports) });
    } catch (e) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(e) });
    } finally {
      busy = false;
    }
  }
</script>

<div class="card">
  {#if summary.kind === "synced"}
    <div class="head">
      <span class="dot ok"></span>
      <b>同期済み</b>
      <span class="aside">
        {#if summary.noRemote}
          remote なし
        {:else if summary.lastSuccess}
          {formatRelative(summary.lastSuccess, new Date(now))}
        {/if}
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="row"><span>未 push</span><span class="v">0 件</span></div>
    {#if summary.nextFetch}
      <div class="row">
        <span>次の fetch</span><span class="v mono">{countdown(summary.nextFetch)}</span>
      </div>
    {/if}
  {:else if summary.kind === "pending"}
    <div class="head">
      <span class="dot pending"></span>
      <b>送信待ち</b>
      {#if summary.pushAt}
        <span class="aside">{pushIn(summary.pushAt)}</span>
      {/if}
    </div>
    <div class="row"><span>未 push</span><span class="v">{summary.unpushed} コミット</span></div>
  {:else if summary.kind === "error"}
    <div class="head">
      <span class="dot bad"></span>
      <b class="bad-text">push できません</b>
    </div>
    {@const first = summary.errors[0]}
    <p class="msg">{first?.store}: {first?.message}</p>
    <div class="row"><span>未 push</span><span class="v">{summary.unpushed} コミット</span></div>
  {:else}
    <div class="head">
      <span class="dot off"></span>
      <b>オフライン</b>
      {#if summary.lastSuccess}
        <span class="aside">最終同期 {clock(summary.lastSuccess)}</span>
      {/if}
    </div>
    <div class="row"><span>未 push</span><span class="v">{summary.unpushed} コミット</span></div>
  {/if}
  <button
    type="button"
    class="now"
    disabled={busy}
    onclick={syncNow}
    title={kbdLabel("shift+mod+s")}
  >
    <Icon name="sync" size={14} />
    {busy ? "同期中…" : "今すぐ同期"}
  </button>
</div>

<style>
  .card {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    padding: var(--space-12);
    border: 1px solid var(--line-soft);
    border-radius: var(--radius-10);
    background: var(--surface);
  }
  .head {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
  }
  .head b {
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .bad-text {
    color: var(--danger-ink);
  }
  .dot {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
    border-radius: 999px;
  }
  .ok {
    background: var(--success);
  }
  .pending {
    background: var(--pending);
  }
  .bad {
    background: var(--danger);
  }
  .off {
    background: var(--muted-2);
  }
  .aside {
    margin-left: auto;
    color: var(--label);
    font-size: var(--fs-12);
  }
  .row {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;

  }
  .v {
    color: var(--ink);
  }

  }
  .msg {
    margin: 0;

  }
  .now {
    display: flex;

    gap: var(--space-6);
    min-height: 32px;
    border: 1px solid var(--line-input);
    border-radius: var(--radius-6);
    background: var(--surface);
    font-size: var(--fs-12);
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .now:disabled {
    color: var(--muted);
    cursor: progress;
  }
</style>
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { core } from "../context";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import KindIcon from "../icons/KindIcon.svelte";
  import { hrefOf, onLinkClick, router } from "../router";
  import { useSubscribe } from "../svelte-store";
  import StoreBadge from "../ui/StoreBadge.svelte";
  import SyncCard from "./SyncCard.svelte";

  const { stores } = core();
  const workspaces = useSubscribe(stores, () => stores.workspaces());
  const route = $derived(router.route);

  function workspaceActive(store: string, ulid: string, index: number): boolean {
    const r = route;
    if (r.name === "workspace") return r.store === store && r.ulid === ulid;
    return r.name === "home" && index === 0;
  }
</script>

<nav class="side" aria-label="メインナビゲーション">
  <div class="top">
    <span class="logo" aria-hidden="true">hh</span>
    <span class="host">{location.host}</span>
  </div>

  <section class="ws-section" aria-label="ワークスペース">
    <div class="ws-head">
      <span class="ws-title">ワークスペース</span>
    </div>

    {#each workspaces.current as w, i (`${w.store}/${w.ulid}`)}
      {@const href = hrefOf({ name: "workspace", store: w.store, ulid: w.ulid })}
      <div class="ws">
        <a
          {href}
          class="ws-link"
          class:active={workspaceActive(w.store, w.ulid, i)}
          aria-current={workspaceActive(w.store, w.ulid, i) ? "page" : undefined}
          onclick={(e) => onLinkClick(e, href)}
        >
          <Icon name="workspace" size={13} />
          <span class="ws-text">
            <span class="ws-name">{w.name}</span>
            <span class="ws-meta">
              <StoreBadge store={w.store} tone={w.tone} compact />
              <span class="prefix" title="ID の接頭辞">{w.prefix}</span>
            </span>
          </span>
        </a>
        <div class="projects">
          {#each w.projects as p (p.ulid)}
            {@const phref = hrefOf({ name: "project", ulid: p.ulid })}
            {@const here = route.name === "project" && route.ulid === p.ulid}
            <a
              href={phref}
              class="project"
              class:active={here}
              aria-current={here ? "page" : undefined}
              onclick={(e) => onLinkClick(e, phref)}
            >
              <KindIcon kind="project" size={14} />
              <span class="p-name">{p.title}</span>
              {#if p.progress}
                <span class="progress">{p.progress.done}/{p.progress.total}</span>
              {/if}
            </a>
          {/each}
          {#if w.projects.length === 0}
            <span class="none">プロジェクトなし</span>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </div>
    {/each}
  </section>

  <div class="sync"><SyncCard /></div>
</nav>
```

### Task 9: ツリー（表示・フィルタ・キーボード）

画面は `Main.dc.html`（ツリー）。4d の範囲外（PR の欄・「列」メニュー・「ビューを保存」・設定タブ・テンプレートで作るボタン・リポジトリのチップ・ガント／カンバン／テーブル／概要のタブ）は作らない。

この Task のコードは、Task 2・3〜8 の実物（`web/core/*`、`app/context.ts`・`svelte-store.ts`・`router.ts`・`keys.ts`・`toast.svelte.ts`・`selection.svelte.ts`・`ui/*`・`icons/*`）の上で `svelte-check --fail-on-warnings`・`vitest run`・`biome ci`・`vite build` を通したものを、そのまま載せている。

この Task で決めること（plan 前半の細部 22・23・38・39・44 を埋める）:

1. **ベースのクエリとユーザーの入力の合わせ方**: 画面が持つベースのクエリ（ワークスペースなら `workspace:<名前>`、プロジェクトなら `project:<ULID>`）を先頭に置き、ユーザーが入力した文字列を空白でつなぐだけにする（hh のクエリの項はすべて AND なので、連結が「ベースの中で絞る」になる）。フィルタ欄にはユーザーの入力だけを出し、ベースは欄の左に固定の表示（読み取りだけ）で出す。API へ送るのは ULID 版（`project:<ULID>`）、欄の左と足元の `hh ls` は人が読む版（`project:REL-15`、名前に空白があれば `"` で囲む）。足元の `hh ls` はワークスペースのツリーなら `--store <Store>` を付ける（細部 39）。ユーザーが `workspace:` や `project:` を足しても AND になるだけで、ベースの外には出られない。`sort:`・`group:` はツリーでは使えず、サーバーの `query.tree_order` の文言をそのまま欄の下に出す
2. **欄の既定は `-status:canceled`**（利用者の決定 6）。欄を消すと中止も出る。件数の「n 件」は、ツリーに含まれるタスクのうち、絞り込みで一致したもの（`context: true` の祖先の行と、プロジェクトの画面でのプロジェクト自身を除く）の数で、折りたたまれた子孫も数える。「· 中止 m 件を除外」は、欄に `-status:canceled` の項があるときだけ付け、m は「ベース + 入力から `-status:canceled` を除いたもの + `status:canceled`」の件数（`GET /tasks`、ツリーなし）
3. **仮想スクロール**（細部 44）: 展開済みの行を平らにした配列が 200 行を超えたときだけ TanStack Virtual（`@tanstack/svelte-virtual`）で描く。200 行以下は全部描く（キーボード操作・ドラッグ・テストが単純なまま）。行の高さは固定（39px。行 38px + 隙間 1px）。`createVirtualizer` は Svelte の store を返し、`setOptions` は store の値（インスタンス）にある。`$` 記法を使わず、`subscribe` で受けたインスタンスを `$state.raw` に持って `$effect` から `setOptions` を呼ぶ
4. **ブロック（「<ID> 待ち」）の判定**（細部 38）: `blocked_by` の先のタスクがキャッシュにあれば、その状態（カテゴリが done・canceled でなく、削除されていない）で決める。キャッシュになければ、ベースに `is:blocked` を足した問い合わせ（`GET /tasks`）の ULID の集合に入っていれば「最初の `blocked_by` の ID 待ち」を出す（`openBlocker`）
5. **制約違反・壊れたファイルの印**: `broken` があれば黄色の「壊れています」（`title` に `broken` の文言）、なければ `problems` の最初の1件を `problemLabel` で日本語にして黄色の印にする（`required` は「<field> 未入力」。canvas の「points 未入力」）。詳細の「`hh doctor` で確かめてください」は Task 11 の側
6. **Enter で下に追加**（細部 23）: 入力行を、選択している行の子孫の後ろ（同じ親の次の位置）に出す。タイトルを入れて Enter で作成し、続けてその下に次の入力行を出す（Esc か空の Enter で終わる）。最後の兄弟の後ろに作るときは `move` を呼ばない（作成の 1 コミットだけ）。それ以外は作成のあとに `move`（`after`）で 2 コミット。種類は、親の種類で `canParent` に合う既定の種類（なければ最初の work の種類）。合うものがなければトーストで理由を出して作らない
7. **展開・折りたたみ**: 既定はすべて展開。折りたたんだ ULID の集合は画面（TreeView）の中だけで持つ（画面を離れると戻る）。→ で展開（展開済みなら最初の子へ）、← で折りたたみ（折りたたみ済みなら親へ）
8. **S・K のメニュー**: S は `schema.statuses` を並べ、K は `schema.kinds` を並べる。K で今の親に置けない種類は無効にして、理由を `hint` に出す。選ぶと `PATCH`（`status` か `kind`）を 1 回送る。ワークフローの制約（遷移など）で拒否されたらトーストにサーバーの文言を出す
9. **Space**: 今のステータスのカテゴリが done なら todo カテゴリの既定のステータスへ、それ以外は done カテゴリの既定のステータスへ `PATCH`
10. **空の状態**: 行が0件で、クエリの誤りでもないとき、欄が空か `-status:canceled` だけで中止のタスクもなければ `Empty variant="empty"`（「<名前> にはまだタスクがありません」。「タスクを作成」のボタンは、作成ダイアログを作る Task 13 が `onCreate` を渡して足す）、そうでなければ `Empty variant="nomatch"`（「フィルタを解除」で `applied` を空にする）。`Empty.svelte` 自体もこの Task が置く（`TreeView` が import するので、Task 15 より前に要る）
11. **`TreeView` の prop は `view`**（`{ kind: "workspace"; store; ulid } | { kind: "project"; ulid }`）。`scope` という名前の prop は、Biome の `noHeaderScope` が HTML の属性と読んで `biome ci` を落とすため。中では `let { view: scope } = $props()` で `scope` と呼ぶ。`Empty` の `scope` prop も同じ理由でスプレッド（`{...emptyProps}`）で渡す
12. **スキーマを読む前に押したキー**: Space・Tab・⇧Tab・S・K は Store のスキーマ（種類とステータス）で動くので、読み終わる前に押すと黙って何も起きなくなる。押したキーを `waiting` に覚えておき、スキーマが届いたら押した順に実行する（`needsSchema` が「スキーマが要るキー」を決める。⌥↑↓・矢印・Enter は待たない）。スキーマが読めなかったときは、待っていたキーを捨ててトーストで理由を出し、その後に押したときも「schema.yaml を読み込めていません」と出す。入力行（`AddRow`）の `ready` と同じ考えで、ドラッグは `dropDecision`（Task 10）が「少し待ってからやり直してください」と断る

**Files:**
- Create: `web/app/tree/placement.ts`（`MoveArgs`・`Rejection`・`kindLabel`・`checkParent`・`pickKind`。ドラッグとキーボードが共有する「置けるか」）
- Create: `web/app/tree/flatten.ts`（`FlatRow`・`viewRoots`・`flatten`・`countMatched`・`countLabel`・`VIRTUALIZE_AT`・`ROW_HEIGHT`）
- Create: `web/app/tree/filter.ts`（クエリの項の分解とベースとの合成）
- Create: `web/app/tree/marks.ts`（`problemLabel`・`propText`・`openBlocker`）
- Create: `web/app/tree/keyboard.ts`
- Create: `web/app/tree/actions.ts`（`moveTask`・`patchTask`・`createBelow`。API を呼ぶ関数）
- Create: `web/app/tree/TreeView.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/tree/TreeRow.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/tree/FilterBar.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/tree/AddRow.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/states/Empty.svelte`（空の状態。ツリーが使う。`states/` の残りは Task 15）
- Modify: `web/app/App.svelte`（workspace・project のルートに `TreeView`）
- Test: `web/app/tree/fixtures.ts`（テスト用の部品）、`web/app/tree/placement.test.ts`、`flatten.test.ts`、`filter.test.ts`、`marks.test.ts`、`keyboard.test.ts`、`actions.test.ts`、`TreeView.test.ts`、`web/app/states/Empty.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes（plan 前半の「共通の型と関数」）: `Api`・`ApiError`・`unwrap`・`TaskView`（`core/api/client`）、`errorMessage`（`core/messages`）、`TaskCache`・`TreeNode`・`LiveMark`（`core/cache`）、`cliLs(q, tree, store?)`・`quoteArg`（`core/cli`）、`StoreDirectory`・`WorkspaceEntry`・`StoreTone`（`core/stores`）、`SchemaCache`・`SchemaView`・`canParent`・`parentRule`・`defaultStatus`（`core/schema`）、`formatDate`（`core/format`）
- Consumes（Task 7 の最終形）: `core()`・`AppCore`・`CORE_KEY`（`app/context`）、`useSubscribe`（`app/svelte-store`）、`router`（`go`・`href`）、`modKey`・`kbdLabel`（`app/keys`）、`toast`（`app/toast.svelte`）、`selection`（`app/selection.svelte`）、`emitter`（`app/test-utils`、テストだけ）、`ui/CliLine`（`command`）・`ui/IdLabel`（`id`・`ulid`）・`ui/Avatar`（`name`）・`ui/StoreBadge`（`store`・`tone`）・`ui/Menu`（`anchor`・`items`・`onselect`・`onclose`）、`icons/StatusIcon`（`status`・`category`）・`icons/KindIcon`（`kind`・`role`）・`icons/Icon`（`name`・`size`）、`states/Empty`（この Task が作る。props は `variant`・`scope`・`name`・`note`・`onCreate`・`onClear`）
- Produces:
  - `MoveArgs = { parent?: string; root?: boolean; before?: string; after?: string }`（`POST /tasks/{id}/move` の本文。`before`・`after` は同じ親の下の兄弟の ULID。`parent` は新しい親、`root` は親なし。`parent`・`root` を省くと親は変わらない）— Task 10・14 が使う
  - `Rejection = { title: string; reason: string }`、`checkParent(s, child, parent | null): Rejection | null`、`pickKind(s, parentKind | null): string | null`、`kindLabel(s, kind)` — Task 10 が使う
  - `moveTask(d, task, to)`・`patchTask(d, task, body)`・`createBelow(d, input)`（`tree/actions.ts`）— Task 14 が `patchTask` を使う
  - `FlatRow`、`TreeView` の prop `view`
  - `TreeRow` の props と `data-ulid` 属性（Task 10 が行にドラッグを付ける）
  - `TreeView` の変数名 `rows`・`loading`・`adding`・`treeError`・`applied`・`canceledCount`・`crumbTitle`・`scope`・`viewRoot` と、`.scroller` の中の読み込み中の `<p class="note">`（Task 15 が前提にしていた名前。変えていない）

`MoveInputBody` の意味（`internal/ops/move.go` の `moveTargetOf` / `rankAt` で確認済み）: `parent` を渡すとその子の末尾（`before`・`after` がなければ）、`root: true` で親なし、どちらもなければ今の親のまま。`before`・`after` は参照先が「移動後の親」の子でなければ `move.invalid`。同じ rank の兄弟があるときは、その組の外側に置かれる。

```ts
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";

export const schema = {
  statuses: [
    { name: "backlog", category: "todo", default: true },

export function task(
  ulid: string,
  kind: string,
  parent: string | null,
  extra: Partial<TaskView> = {},
): TaskView {

    updated: "2026-11-01T00:00:00Z",
    deleted_at: null,
    problems: [],
    ...extra,
  } as TaskView;
}
```

`web/app/tree/placement.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { SchemaView } from "../../core/schema";
import { schema, task } from "./fixtures";
import { checkParent, kindLabel, pickKind } from "./placement";

describe("checkParent", () => {
  it("accepts a placement the kinds allow", () => {
    expect(checkParent(schema, task("T1", "task", null), task("E1", "epic", null))).toBeNull();
    expect(checkParent(schema, task("E1", "epic", null), null)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rejects an epic under a task, in the words of the canvas", () => {
    const epic = task("E8", "epic", null, { id: "REL-8", title: "リリース作業" });
    const r = checkParent(schema, epic, task("T7", "task", null));
    expect(r?.title).toBe("エピックはタスクの下に置けません");
    expect(r?.reason).toBe(
      "「REL-8 リリース作業」はエピックです。エピックを置ける親はルートとプロジェクトだけです（shared の schema.yaml の kinds）。",
    );
  });

  it("rejects a project under an epic and a story at the root where it cannot go", () => {
    expect(checkParent(schema, task("P1", "project", null), task("E1", "epic", null))?.title).toBe(
      "プロジェクトはエピックの下に置けません",
    );
    const narrow = {
      ...schema,
      kinds: schema.kinds?.map((k) => (k.name === "story" ? { ...k, parents: ["epic"] } : k)),
    } as SchemaView;
    expect(checkParent(narrow, task("S1", "story", null), null)?.title).toBe(
      "ストーリーはルートに置けません",
    );
  });

  it("rejects a parent in another store", () => {
    const r = checkParent(
      schema,
      task("T1", "task", null),
      task("T2", "task", null, { store: "backend" }),
    );
    expect(r?.title).toBe("別の Store のタスクの下には置けません");
    expect(r?.reason).toContain("shared");
    expect(r?.reason).toContain("backend");
  });
});

describe("kindLabel / pickKind", () => {
  it("falls back to the name when the schema has no label", () => {
    expect(kindLabel(schema, "epic")).toBe("エピック");
    expect(kindLabel(schema, "unknown")).toBe("unknown");
  });

  it("picks the default kind when it fits the parent, else the first work kind that fits", () => {
    expect(pickKind(schema, "epic")).toBe("task");
    expect(pickKind(schema, null)).toBe("task");
    const noTask = {
      ...schema,
      kinds: schema.kinds?.map((k) => (k.name === "task" ? { ...k, parents: ["project"] } : k)),
    } as SchemaView;
    expect(pickKind(noTask, "epic")).toBe("story");
    expect(pickKind(noTask, "task")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/flatten.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { TreeNode } from "../../core/cache";
import { task } from "./fixtures";
import { countLabel, countMatched, flatten, viewRoots } from "./flatten";

const tasks = new Map([
  ["P", task("P", "project", null, { context: true })],
  ["E1", task("E1", "epic", "P")],
  ["T1", task("T1", "task", "E1")],
  ["T2", task("T2", "task", "E1", { context: true })],
  ["T3", task("T3", "task", "P")],
]);
const taskOf = (u: string) => tasks.get(u);
const tree: TreeNode[] = [
  {
    ulid: "P",
    children: [
      {
        ulid: "E1",
        children: [
          { ulid: "T1", children: [] },
          { ulid: "T2", children: [] },
        ],
      },
      { ulid: "T3", children: [] },
    ],
  },
];

describe("viewRoots", () => {
  it("returns the roots as they are for a workspace", () => {
    expect(viewRoots(tree, null)).toBe(tree);
  });
  it("returns the children of the project, which is itself left out", () => {
    expect(viewRoots(tree, "P").map((n) => n.ulid)).toEqual(["E1", "T3"]);
  });
  it("returns nothing when the project is not in the tree", () => {
    expect(viewRoots(tree, "ZZ")).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("flatten", () => {
  it("lists the expanded rows with depth and parent", () => {
    const rows = flatten(tree, "P", taskOf, new Set());
    expect(rows.map((r) => [r.ulid, r.depth, r.parent, r.expanded])).toEqual([
      ["E1", 0, "P", true],
      ["T1", 1, "E1", false],
      ["T2", 1, "E1", false],
      ["T3", 0, "P", false],
    ]);
  });
  it("leaves out the children of a collapsed row", () => {
    const rows = flatten(tree, "P", taskOf, new Set(["E1"]));
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.ulid)).toEqual(["E1", "T3"]);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ hasChildren: true, expanded: false, childCount: 2 });
  });
  it("uses a null parent at the top of a workspace", () => {
    const rows = flatten([{ ulid: "T3", children: [] }], null, taskOf, new Set());
    expect(rows[0]?.parent).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("countMatched / countLabel", () => {
  it("counts the matched tasks, including collapsed ones, and not the context rows", () => {
    expect(countMatched(tree, "P", taskOf)).toBe(3); // E1, T1 and T3; T2 is context
  });
  it("writes the count and the canceled tasks left out", () => {
    expect(countLabel(16, null)).toBe("16 件");
    expect(countLabel(16, 1)).toBe("16 件 · 中止 1 件を除外");
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/filter.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  baseQuery,
  combine,
  hasTerm,
  NO_CANCELED,
  splitTerms,
  toggleTerm,
  withoutTerm,
} from "./filter";

describe("splitTerms", () => {
  it("splits at white space outside double quotes", () => {
    expect(splitTerms('a:b  "c d" -status:canceled')).toEqual(["a:b", '"c d"', "-status:canceled"]);
    expect(splitTerms("   ")).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("baseQuery", () => {
  it("writes a workspace by name, quoted when it has a space", () => {
    expect(baseQuery({ kind: "workspace", name: "release" }, "api")).toBe("workspace:release");
    expect(baseQuery({ kind: "workspace", name: "my work" }, "cli")).toBe('workspace:"my work"');
  });
  it("writes a project by ULID for the API and by ID for the CLI", () => {
    const b = { kind: "project", ulid: "01JABC", id: "REL-15" } as const;
    expect(baseQuery(b, "api")).toBe("project:01JABC");
    expect(baseQuery(b, "cli")).toBe("project:REL-15");
  });
});

describe("combine", () => {
  it("puts the base first and joins the input with a space", () => {
    expect(combine("project:X", "assignee:@me")).toBe("project:X assignee:@me");
    expect(combine("project:X", "   ")).toBe("project:X");
  });
});

describe("terms", () => {
  it("finds, removes and toggles a term", () => {
    expect(hasTerm(`a:b ${NO_CANCELED}`, NO_CANCELED)).toBe(true);
    expect(hasTerm("a:b", NO_CANCELED)).toBe(false);
    expect(withoutTerm(`a:b ${NO_CANCELED} c`, NO_CANCELED)).toBe("a:b c");
    expect(toggleTerm("a:b", NO_CANCELED)).toBe(`a:b ${NO_CANCELED}`);
    expect(toggleTerm(`a:b ${NO_CANCELED}`, NO_CANCELED)).toBe("a:b");
    expect(toggleTerm("", NO_CANCELED)).toBe(NO_CANCELED);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { task } from "./fixtures";
import { openBlocker, problemLabel, propText } from "./marks";

describe("problemLabel", () => {
  it("says a missing required field as the canvas does", () => {
    expect(problemLabel({ code: "required", field: "points", message: "..." })).toBe(
      "points 未入力",
    );
  });
  it("names the other codes in Japanese and falls back to the code", () => {
    expect(problemLabel({ code: "missing_ref", field: "blocked_by", message: "" })).toBe(
      "参照先がありません",
    );
    expect(problemLabel({ code: "cycle", field: "parent", message: "" })).toBe("循環しています");
    expect(problemLabel({ code: "other_code", field: "", message: "" })).toBe("other_code");
  });
});

describe("propText", () => {
  it("returns a string property and ignores the others", () => {
    const t = task("T1", "task", null, { props: { due: "2026-11-07", points: 3 } });
    expect(propText(t, "due")).toBe("2026-11-07");
    expect(propText(t, "points")).toBeUndefined();
    expect(propText(t, "none")).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("openBlocker", () => {
  const blocked = task("T1", "task", null, {
    blocked_by: [
      { ulid: "B1", id: "REL-2", title: "a" },
      { ulid: "B2", id: "REL-3", title: "b" },
    ],
  });
  const closed = (t: { status: string }) => t.status === "done";

  it("names the first blocker that is still open", () => {
    const m = new Map([
      ["B1", task("B1", "task", null, { status: "done" })],
      ["B2", task("B2", "task", null, { status: "todo" })],
    ]);
    expect(openBlocker(blocked, (u) => m.get(u), closed, false)).toBe("REL-3");
  });
  it("is not blocked when every blocker is done or deleted", () => {
    const m = new Map([
      ["B1", task("B1", "task", null, { status: "done" })],
      ["B2", task("B2", "task", null, { deleted_at: "2026-11-01T00:00:00Z" })],
    ]);
    expect(openBlocker(blocked, (u) => m.get(u), closed, true)).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("asks the server when a blocker is not in the cache", () => {
    expect(openBlocker(blocked, () => undefined, closed, true)).toBe("REL-2");
    expect(openBlocker(blocked, () => undefined, closed, false)).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/keyboard.test.ts`（キー操作はこの関数で網羅する）:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";
import { schema, task } from "./fixtures";
import { flatten } from "./flatten";
import { type KeyContext, type KeyInput, keyAction, needsSchema } from "./keyboard";

// P (view root)
//  ├ E1 (epic)
//  │   ├ T1
//  │   └ T2
//  ├ T3
//  └ S1 (story, collapsed: child T4)
const tasks = new Map([
  ["P", task("P", "project", null)],
  ["E1", task("E1", "epic", "P")],
  ["T1", task("T1", "task", "E1")],
  ["T2", task("T2", "task", "E1", { status: "done" })],
  ["T3", task("T3", "task", "P")],
  ["S1", task("S1", "story", "P")],
  ["T4", task("T4", "task", "S1")],
]);
const tree = [
  {
    ulid: "P",
    children: [
      {
        ulid: "E1",
        children: [
          { ulid: "T1", children: [] },
          { ulid: "T2", children: [] },
        ],
      },
      { ulid: "T3", children: [] },
      { ulid: "S1", children: [{ ulid: "T4", children: [] }] },
    ],
  },
];
const taskOf = (u: string) => tasks.get(u);

function ctx(selected: string | null, over: Partial<KeyContext> = {}): KeyContext {
  return {
    rows: flatten(tree, "P", taskOf, new Set(["S1"])),
    selected,
    schema,
    viewRoot: "P",
    taskOf,
    ...over,
  };
}
const key = (k: string, m: Partial<KeyInput> = {}): KeyInput => ({
  key: k,
  shift: false,
  alt: false,
  mod: false,
  ...m,
});

describe("which keys need the schema", () => {
  it("is true for the keys that act through the kinds and the statuses", () => {
    for (const k of ["Tab", " ", "s", "S", "k", "K"]) {
      expect(needsSchema(key(k))).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(needsSchema(key("Tab", { shift: true }))).toBe(true);
  });
  it("is false for moving, folding, adding and reordering, and for a key with a modifier", () => {
    for (const k of ["ArrowDown", "ArrowLeft", "Enter", "a"]) {
      expect(needsSchema(key(k))).toBe(false);
    }
    expect(needsSchema(key("ArrowUp", { alt: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(needsSchema(key("Tab", { alt: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(needsSchema(key("s", { mod: true }))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("moving the selection", () => {
  it("selects the next and previous visible row", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowDown"), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "T1" });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowUp"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "E1" });
  });
  it("stays on the first and last row", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowUp"), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "E1" });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowDown"), ctx("S1"))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "S1" });
  });
  it("starts at the first row going down and at the last going up when nothing is selected", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowDown"), ctx(null))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "E1" });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowUp"), ctx(null))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "S1" });
  });
  it("does nothing on an empty tree", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowDown"), ctx(null, { rows: [] }))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
});

describe("expanding and collapsing", () => {
  it("collapses an expanded row with ← and goes to the parent from a leaf", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowLeft"), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({
      type: "toggle",
      ulid: "E1",
      open: false,
    });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowLeft"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "E1" });
  });
  it("does not go above the view root", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowLeft"), ctx("T3"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
  it("expands a collapsed row with → and goes to the first child of an expanded one", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowRight"), ctx("S1"))).toEqual({
      type: "toggle",
      ulid: "S1",
      open: true,
    });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowRight"), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({ type: "select", ulid: "T1" });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowRight"), ctx("T3"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
});

describe("Enter", () => {
  it("opens a row to add below the selected one, under the same parent", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Enter"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({
      type: "add-below",
      after: "T1",
      parent: "E1",
    });
    expect(keyAction(key("Enter"), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({
      type: "add-below",
      after: "E1",
      parent: "P",
    });
  });
  it("does nothing with the modifier: the detail is another screen", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Enter", { mod: true }), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
  it("does nothing with no selection or with Shift / Alt", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Enter"), ctx(null))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
    expect(keyAction(key("Enter", { shift: true }), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
});

describe("Tab / Shift+Tab", () => {
  it("indents under the previous sibling, at the end", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab"), ctx("T3"))).toEqual({
      type: "move",
      ulid: "T3",
      to: { parent: "E1" },
    });
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab"), ctx("T2"))).toEqual({
      type: "move",
      ulid: "T2",
      to: { parent: "T1" },
    });
  });
  it("leaves Tab alone on the first sibling", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab"), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
  it("rejects an indent the kinds do not allow", () => {
    // the previous sibling of S1 (a story) is T3 (a task)
    const r = keyAction(key("Tab"), ctx("S1"));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({
      type: "reject",
      rejection: { title: "ストーリーはタスクの下に置けません" },
    });
  });
  it("outdents after the parent", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab", { shift: true }), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({
      type: "move",
      ulid: "T1",
      to: { parent: "P", after: "E1" },
    });
  });
  it("outdents to the root of a workspace view", () => {
    // seen from a workspace the epic is a root: it has no parent
    const topTask = (u: string) =>
      u === "E1" ? { ...(tasks.get("E1") as TaskView), parent: null } : tasks.get(u);
    const rows = flatten(
      tree.flatMap((n) => n.children),
      null,
      topTask,
      new Set(["S1"]),
    );
    const c = ctx("T1", { rows, viewRoot: null, taskOf: topTask });
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab", { shift: true }), c)).toEqual({
      type: "move",
      ulid: "T1",
      to: { root: true, after: "E1" },
    });
  });
  it("does not outdent a top row of the view", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab", { shift: true }), ctx("T3"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
  it("rejects an outdent the kinds do not allow", () => {
    // a task under an epic can go to the project; make the project not accept tasks
    const narrow = {
      ...schema,
      kinds: schema.kinds?.map((k) => (k.name === "task" ? { ...k, parents: ["epic"] } : k)),
    } as typeof schema;
    expect(keyAction(key("Tab", { shift: true }), ctx("T1", { schema: narrow }))).toMatchObject({
      type: "reject",
      rejection: { title: "タスクはプロジェクトの下に置けません" },
    });
  });
});

describe("Alt+↑↓", () => {
  it("moves before the previous sibling and after the next one", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowUp", { alt: true }), ctx("T3"))).toEqual({
      type: "move",
      ulid: "T3",
      to: { before: "E1" },
    });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowDown", { alt: true }), ctx("E1"))).toEqual({
      type: "move",
      ulid: "E1",
      to: { after: "T3" },
    });
  });
  it("stays among its own siblings", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowUp", { alt: true }), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
    expect(keyAction(key("ArrowDown", { alt: true }), ctx("T2"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
});

describe("Space / S / K", () => {
  it("completes a task, and reopens a done one", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key(" "), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({
      type: "set-status",
      ulid: "T1",
      status: "done",
    });
    expect(keyAction(key(" "), ctx("T2"))).toEqual({
      type: "set-status",
      ulid: "T2",
      status: "backlog",
    });
  });
  it("needs the schema", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key(" "), ctx("T1", { schema: undefined }))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
  it("opens the status and kind menus", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("s"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "menu", which: "status", ulid: "T1" });
    expect(keyAction(key("k"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "menu", which: "kind", ulid: "T1" });
    expect(keyAction(key("S"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "menu", which: "status", ulid: "T1" });
  });
  it("ignores them with a modifier or no selection", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("s", { mod: true }), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
    expect(keyAction(key("s"), ctx(null))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
});

describe("other keys", () => {
  it("does nothing", () => {
    expect(keyAction(key("a"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
    expect(keyAction(key("Escape"), ctx("T1"))).toEqual({ type: "none" });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createBelow, moveTask, patchTask } from "./actions";
import { task } from "./fixtures";

const ok = (data: unknown) => ({ data, response: new Response(null, { status: 200 }) });

function deps() {
  const api = {
    POST: vi.fn(async (path: string) =>
      path === "/api/tasks"
        ? ok({ task: task("N1", "task", "P"), commit: "c1" })
        : ok({ task: task("N1", "task", "P", { rank: "b" }), commit: "c2" }),
    ),
    PATCH: vi.fn(async () =>
      ok({ task: task("T1", "task", "P", { status: "done" }), commit: "c3" }),
    ),
  };
  const cache = { applyWrite: vi.fn() };
  return { api, cache } as unknown as Parameters<typeof moveTask>[0] & {
    api: typeof api;
    cache: typeof cache;
  };
}

describe("moveTask", () => {
  it("posts the move with the store and records the write", async () => {
    const d = deps();
    await moveTask(d, task("T1", "task", "P"), { parent: "E1", after: "T2" });
    expect(d.api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: "T1" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { parent: "E1", after: "T2" },
    });
    expect(d.cache.applyWrite).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      task: expect.objectContaining({ ulid: "N1" }),
      commit: "c2",
    });
  });
});

describe("patchTask", () => {
  it("patches the status", async () => {
    const d = deps();
    await patchTask(d, task("T1", "task", "P"), { status: "done" });
    expect(d.api.PATCH).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}", {
      params: { path: { id: "T1" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { status: "done" },
    });
    expect(d.cache.applyWrite).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      task: expect.objectContaining({ status: "done" }),
      commit: "c3",
    });
  });
});

describe("createBelow", () => {
  const base = { title: "new", kind: "task", store: "shared", parent: "P", workspace: "REL" };

  it("creates and then moves after the row above it", async () => {
    const d = deps();
    const t = await createBelow(d, { ...base, after: "T1", afterIsLast: false });
    expect(d.api.POST).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, "/api/tasks", {
      params: { query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { title: "new", kind: "task", parent: "P" },
    });
    expect(d.api.POST).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, "/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: "N1" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { after: "T1" },
    });
    expect(t.ulid).toBe("N1");
  });

  it("does not move when it goes after the last sibling", async () => {
    const d = deps();
    await createBelow(d, { ...base, after: "T9", afterIsLast: true });
    expect(d.api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("names the workspace when there is no parent", async () => {
    const d = deps();
    await createBelow(d, { ...base, parent: null, after: null, afterIsLast: true });
    expect(d.api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks", {
      params: { query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { title: "new", kind: "task", workspace: "REL" },
    });
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/placement.ts`:

```ts
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";
import { canParent, parentRule, type SchemaView } from "../../core/schema";

// The body of POST /tasks/{id}/move. before and after name a sibling under the
// parent the task ends up in; parent and root set that parent, and leaving
// both out keeps the current one.
export type MoveArgs = { parent?: string; root?: boolean; before?: string; after?: string };

// Why a placement is refused: a short title and the full reason.
export type Rejection = { title: string; reason: string };

export function kindLabel(s: SchemaView, kind: string): string {
  return s.kinds?.find((k) => k.name === kind)?.label || kind;
}

// checkParent reports why child cannot sit under parent (null for the root),
// or null when it can. Whether parent is a descendant of child is the
// caller's to check.
export function checkParent(
  s: SchemaView,
  child: TaskView,
  parent: TaskView | null,
): Rejection | null {
  if (parent && parent.store !== child.store) {
    return {
      title: "別の Store のタスクの下には置けません",
      reason: `「${child.id} ${child.title}」は ${child.store}、「${parent.id} ${parent.title}」は ${parent.store} にあります。Store をまたぐ移動はドラッグではできません。`,
    };
  }
  if (canParent(s, child.kind, parent ? parent.kind : null)) return null;
  const mine = kindLabel(s, child.kind);
  return {
    title: parent
      ? `${mine}は${kindLabel(s, parent.kind)}の下に置けません`
      : `${mine}はルートに置けません`,
    reason: `「${child.id} ${child.title}」は${mine}です。${parentRule(s, child.kind)}（${child.store} の schema.yaml の kinds）。`,
  };
}

// pickKind is the kind a new task gets under a parent of parentKind: the
// default kind when it fits, else the first work kind that does.
export function pickKind(s: SchemaView, parentKind: string | null): string | null {
  const kinds = s.kinds ?? [];
  const fits = (name: string) => canParent(s, name, parentKind);
  const k =
    kinds.find((x) => x.default && fits(x.name)) ??
    kinds.find((x) => x.role === "work" && fits(x.name));
  return k?.name ?? null;
}
```

`web/app/tree/flatten.ts`:

```ts
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";
import type { TreeNode } from "../../core/cache";

// A list longer than this is drawn with TanStack Virtual; a shorter one is
// drawn whole.
export const VIRTUALIZE_AT = 200;
// A row is 38px with a 1px gap.
export const ROW_HEIGHT = 39;

export type FlatRow = {
  ulid: string;
  task: TaskView;
  depth: number;
  // The parent in the tree: the view root for the top rows of a project, null
  // at the top of a workspace.
  parent: string | null;
  hasChildren: boolean;
  childCount: number;
  expanded: boolean;
};

// viewRoots is what the view lists at its top: the roots of the tree for a
// workspace, the children of the project (which is itself left out) for a
// project.
export function viewRoots(roots: TreeNode[], viewRoot: string | null): TreeNode[] {
  if (viewRoot === null) return roots;
  const find = (nodes: TreeNode[]): TreeNode | undefined => {
    for (const n of nodes) {
      if (n.ulid === viewRoot) return n;
      const f = find(n.children);
      if (f) return f;
    }
    return undefined;
  };
  return find(roots)?.children ?? [];
}

export function flatten(
  roots: TreeNode[],
  viewRoot: string | null,
  taskOf: (ulid: string) => TaskView | undefined,
  collapsed: ReadonlySet<string>,
): FlatRow[] {
  const out: FlatRow[] = [];
  const walk = (nodes: TreeNode[], depth: number, parent: string | null) => {
    for (const n of nodes) {
      const task = taskOf(n.ulid);
      if (!task) continue;
      const expanded = n.children.length > 0 && !collapsed.has(n.ulid);
      out.push({
        ulid: n.ulid,
        task,
        depth,
        parent,
        hasChildren: n.children.length > 0,
        childCount: n.children.length,
        expanded,
      });
      if (expanded) walk(n.children, depth + 1, n.ulid);
    }
  };
  walk(viewRoots(roots, viewRoot), 0, viewRoot);
  return out;
}

// countMatched counts the tasks the query found: every task of the view,
// collapsed ones too, except the ancestors the tree adds for context.
export function countMatched(
  roots: TreeNode[],
  viewRoot: string | null,
  taskOf: (ulid: string) => TaskView | undefined,
): number {
  let n = 0;
  const walk = (nodes: TreeNode[]) => {
    for (const node of nodes) {
      const t = taskOf(node.ulid);
      if (t && !t.context) n++;
      walk(node.children);
    }
  };
  walk(viewRoots(roots, viewRoot));
  return n;
}

export function countLabel(matched: number, canceledLeftOut: number | null): string {
  return canceledLeftOut === null
    ? `${matched} 件`
    : `${matched} 件 · 中止 ${canceledLeftOut} 件を除外`;
}
```

`web/app/tree/filter.ts`:

```ts
import { quoteArg } from "../../core/cli";

export const NO_CANCELED = "-status:canceled";

// What the view fixes in the query: a workspace by name, or a project.
export type QueryBase =
  | { kind: "workspace"; name: string }
  | { kind: "project"; ulid: string; id: string };

// splitTerms cuts q at white space outside double quotes, as the query parser
// does; the quotes stay in the terms.
export function splitTerms(q: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  let cur = "";
  let quoted = false;
  for (const ch of q) {
    if (ch === '"') {
      quoted = !quoted;
      cur += ch;
    } else if (/\s/.test(ch) && !quoted) {
      if (cur) out.push(cur);
      cur = "";
    } else {
      cur += ch;
    }
  }
  if (cur) out.push(cur);
  return out;
}

// baseQuery writes the base for the API (the project by ULID) or for the
// command line shown to the user (the project by ID).
export function baseQuery(b: QueryBase, form: "api" | "cli"): string {
  if (b.kind === "workspace") return `workspace:${quoteArg(b.name)}`;
  return `project:${form === "api" ? b.ulid : b.id}`;
}

// combine puts the base before the user's input. Every term of a query is
// ANDed, so joining them narrows the base and never leaves it.
export function combine(base: string, user: string): string {
  const u = user.trim();
  return u ? `${base} ${u}` : base;
}

export const hasTerm = (user: string, term: string) => splitTerms(user).includes(term);

export const withoutTerm = (user: string, term: string) =>
  splitTerms(user)
    .filter((t) => t !== term)
    .join(" ");

export function toggleTerm(user: string, term: string): string {
  return hasTerm(user, term) ? withoutTerm(user, term) : [...splitTerms(user), term].join(" ");
}
```

注: `quoteArg` は plan 前半の `core/cli.ts`（空白・記号を含むときだけダブルクォートで囲む）。`workspace:` に使うと、名前に `:` などがあるときも囲まれる（クエリ側は引用符を外して読む）。

`web/app/tree/marks.ts`:

```ts
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";

type Problem = { code: string; field: string; message: string };

const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  unknown_kind: "未定義の種類です",
  unknown_status: "未定義のステータスです",
  missing_title: "タイトルがありません",
  placement: "置き場所が合いません",
  transition: "ステータスの遷移が合いません",
  missing_ref: "参照先がありません",
  cycle: "循環しています",
  no_id: "ID がありません",
  duplicate_id: "ID が重複しています",
  id_prefix: "ID の接頭辞が違います",
  parent_deleted: "親が削除されています",
};

// problemLabel is the short Japanese a row shows for what is wrong with a task.
export function problemLabel(p: Problem): string {
  if (p.code === "required") return `${p.field} 未入力`;
  if (p.code === "bad_value") return `${p.field} の値が不正です`;
  return LABELS[p.code] ?? p.code;
}

// propText is a property of the task when it holds a string.
export function propText(t: TaskView, name: string): string | undefined {
  const v = (t.props as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.[name];
  return typeof v === "string" ? v : undefined;
}

// openBlocker is the ID of the first blocker that still blocks the task: one
// whose state is in the cache and not done or canceled, or, when the blocker is
// not in the cache, the first one if the server says the task is blocked.
export function openBlocker(
  t: TaskView,
  taskOf: (ulid: string) => TaskView | undefined,
  isClosed: (blocker: TaskView) => boolean,
  serverBlocked: boolean,
): string | undefined {
  for (const ref of t.blocked_by ?? []) {
    const b = taskOf(ref.ulid);
    if (b ? !b.deleted_at && !isClosed(b) : serverBlocked) return ref.id;
  }
  return undefined;
}
```

`web/app/tree/keyboard.ts`:

```ts
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";
import { defaultStatus, type SchemaView } from "../../core/schema";
import type { FlatRow } from "./flatten";
import { checkParent, type MoveArgs, type Rejection } from "./placement";

// The key as the tree sees it. mod is ⌘ on macOS and Ctrl elsewhere.
export type KeyInput = { key: string; shift: boolean; alt: boolean; mod: boolean };

export type KeyContext = {
  rows: FlatRow[]; // the visible rows, in order
  selected: string | null;
  schema: SchemaView | undefined;
  viewRoot: string | null; // the project the view shows, or null for a workspace
  taskOf: (ulid: string) => TaskView | undefined;
};

// What the tree should do for a key. The caller runs it: this function only
// decides.
export type KeyAction =
  | { type: "none" }
  | { type: "select"; ulid: string }
  | { type: "toggle"; ulid: string; open: boolean }
  | { type: "add-below"; after: string; parent: string | null }
  | { type: "move"; ulid: string; to: MoveArgs }
  | { type: "set-status"; ulid: string; status: string }
  | { type: "menu"; which: "status" | "kind"; ulid: string }
  | { type: "reject"; rejection: Rejection };

const NONE: KeyAction = { type: "none" };

// The keys that act through the kinds and the statuses of the schema. Pressed before the schema is
// read, they wait for it instead of doing nothing.
export function needsSchema(k: KeyInput): boolean {
  if (k.mod || k.alt) return false;
  return k.key === "Tab" || k.key === " " || "sSkK".includes(k.key);
}

export function keyAction(k: KeyInput, c: KeyContext): KeyAction {
  const i = c.selected === null ? -1 : c.rows.findIndex((r) => r.ulid === c.selected);
  const row = i >= 0 ? c.rows[i] : undefined;

  if ((k.key === "ArrowDown" || k.key === "ArrowUp") && !k.mod) {
    if (k.alt) return k.shift || !row ? NONE : reorder(k.key === "ArrowUp" ? -1 : 1, row, c);
    if (c.rows.length === 0) return NONE;
    const down = k.key === "ArrowDown";
    const next =
      i < 0
        ? down
          ? 0
          : c.rows.length - 1
        : Math.min(Math.max(i + (down ? 1 : -1), 0), c.rows.length - 1);
    const target = c.rows[next];
    return target ? { type: "select", ulid: target.ulid } : NONE;
  }

  if (!row) return NONE;

  switch (k.key) {
    case "ArrowRight": {
      if (row.hasChildren && !row.expanded) return { type: "toggle", ulid: row.ulid, open: true };
      const first = c.rows[i + 1];
      return row.hasChildren && first?.parent === row.ulid
        ? { type: "select", ulid: first.ulid }
        : NONE;
    }
    case "ArrowLeft": {
      if (row.hasChildren && row.expanded) return { type: "toggle", ulid: row.ulid, open: false };
      const parent =
        row.parent === c.viewRoot ? undefined : c.rows.find((r) => r.ulid === row.parent);
      return parent ? { type: "select", ulid: parent.ulid } : NONE;
    }
    case "Enter":
      if (k.mod || k.shift || k.alt) return NONE;
      return { type: "add-below", after: row.ulid, parent: row.parent };
    case "Tab":
      if (k.mod || k.alt) return NONE;
      return k.shift ? outdent(row, c) : indent(row, c);
    case " ": {
      if (k.mod || k.alt || k.shift || !c.schema) return NONE;
      const done = c.schema.statuses?.find((s) => s.name === row.task.status)?.category === "done";
      return {
        type: "set-status",
        ulid: row.ulid,
        status: defaultStatus(c.schema, done ? "todo" : "done"),
      };
    }
    case "s":
    case "S":
      return k.mod || k.alt ? NONE : { type: "menu", which: "status", ulid: row.ulid };
    case "k":
    case "K":
      return k.mod || k.alt ? NONE : { type: "menu", which: "kind", ulid: row.ulid };
    default:
      return NONE;
  }
}

function siblingsOf(row: FlatRow, c: KeyContext): FlatRow[] {
  return c.rows.filter((r) => r.parent === row.parent);
}

function reorder(dir: -1 | 1, row: FlatRow, c: KeyContext): KeyAction {
  const sib = siblingsOf(row, c);
  const other = sib[sib.findIndex((r) => r.ulid === row.ulid) + dir];
  if (!other) return NONE;
  return {
    type: "move",
    ulid: row.ulid,
    to: dir < 0 ? { before: other.ulid } : { after: other.ulid },
  };
}

function indent(row: FlatRow, c: KeyContext): KeyAction {
  const sib = siblingsOf(row, c);
  const prev = sib[sib.findIndex((r) => r.ulid === row.ulid) - 1];
  if (!prev || !c.schema) return NONE;
  const rejection = checkParent(c.schema, row.task, prev.task);
  if (rejection) return { type: "reject", rejection };
  return { type: "move", ulid: row.ulid, to: { parent: prev.ulid } };
}

function outdent(row: FlatRow, c: KeyContext): KeyAction {
  if (row.parent === c.viewRoot || row.parent === null || !c.schema) return NONE;
  const p = c.taskOf(row.parent);
  if (!p) return NONE;
  const grand = p.parent ? (c.taskOf(p.parent) ?? null) : null;
  if (p.parent && !grand) return NONE;
  const rejection = checkParent(c.schema, row.task, grand);
  if (rejection) return { type: "reject", rejection };
  return {
    type: "move",
    ulid: row.ulid,
    to: p.parent ? { parent: p.parent, after: p.ulid } : { root: true, after: p.ulid },
  };
}
```

`web/app/tree/actions.ts`:

```ts
import { type TaskView, unwrap } from "../../core/api/client";
import type { AppCore } from "../context";
import type { MoveArgs } from "./placement";

type Deps = Pick<AppCore, "api" | "cache">;

export async function moveTask(d: Deps, task: TaskView, to: MoveArgs): Promise<TaskView> {
  const r = await unwrap(
    d.api.POST("/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: task.ulid }, query: { store: task.store } },
      body: to,
    }),
  );
  d.cache.applyWrite({ task: r.task, commit: r.commit });
  return r.task;
}

export async function patchTask(
  d: Deps,
  task: TaskView,
  body: { status?: string; kind?: string },
): Promise<TaskView> {
  const r = await unwrap(
    d.api.PATCH("/api/tasks/{id}", {
      params: { path: { id: task.ulid }, query: { store: task.store } },
      body,
    }),
  );
  d.cache.applyWrite({ task: r.task, commit: r.commit });
  return r.task;
}

export type CreateBelow = {
  title: string;
  kind: string;
  store: string;
  parent: string | null; // ULID; null for the top of a workspace
  workspace: string; // the prefix, used when there is no parent
  after: string | null; // the row it goes below; null for the end
  afterIsLast: boolean; // that row is the last sibling, so no move is needed
};

// createBelow adds a task and, unless it already is in place at the end of its
// siblings, moves it right after the row above. That is two commits.
export async function createBelow(d: Deps, i: CreateBelow): Promise<TaskView> {
  const r = await unwrap(
    d.api.POST("/api/tasks", {
      params: { query: { store: i.store } },
      body: {
        title: i.title,
        kind: i.kind,
        ...(i.parent ? { parent: i.parent } : { workspace: i.workspace }),
      },
    }),
  );
  d.cache.applyWrite({ task: r.task, commit: r.commit });
  if (i.after === null || i.afterIsLast) return r.task;
  return moveTask(d, r.task, { after: i.after });
}
```

`TreeView` が空の状態に出す部品。`variant="empty"` は `onCreate` があるときだけ「タスクを作成」のボタンを出す（作成ダイアログは Task 13 が作るので、この回のツリーは渡さない）。先にテストを書く。

`web/app/states/Empty.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { renderWithCore } from "../test-utils";
import Empty from "./Empty.svelte";

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("Empty", () => {
  it("empty: プロジェクトの名前とタスクを作成のボタン（C）", async () => {
    const onCreate = vi.fn();
    const { getByText, getByRole } = renderWithCore(Empty, {
      variant: "empty",
      scope: "project",
      name: "Web フロント刷新",
      onCreate,
    });
    expect(getByText("Web フロント刷新 にはまだタスクがありません")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: /タスクを作成/ }));
    expect(onCreate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("empty: onCreate がなければボタンを出さない", () => {
    const { queryByRole } = renderWithCore(Empty, {
      variant: "empty",
      scope: "workspace",
      name: "release",
    });
    expect(queryByRole("button")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("nomatch: 一致しないことと、note、フィルタを解除", async () => {
    const onClear = vi.fn();
    const { getByText, getByRole } = renderWithCore(Empty, {
      variant: "nomatch",
      note: "完了と中止を除外しています",
      onClear,
    });
    expect(getByText("一致するタスクはありません")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("完了と中止を除外しています")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "フィルタを解除" }));
    expect(onClear).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/TreeView.test.ts`（代表のふるまいだけ。キー操作の網羅は `keyboard.test.ts`）:

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../../core/api/client";
import { selection } from "../selection.svelte";
import { emitter } from "../test-utils";
import { schema, task } from "./fixtures";

const h = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  core: undefined as unknown,
  go: vi.fn(),
  toast: vi.fn(),
}));

vi.mock("../context", () => ({ core: () => h.core }));
vi.mock("../router", () => ({
  router: {
    route: { name: "project", ulid: "P" },
    go: h.go,
    href: (r: { ulid: string }) => `/tasks/${r.ulid}`,
  },
}));
vi.mock("../toast.svelte", () => ({ toast: h.toast }));
vi.mock("@tanstack/svelte-virtual", () => ({
  createVirtualizer: () => ({
    subscribe(fn: (v: unknown) => void) {
      fn({
        getVirtualItems: () =>
          Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => ({ index: i, start: i * 39, size: 39, key: i })),
        getTotalSize: () => 39 * 250,
        setOptions() {},
        scrollToIndex() {},
      });
      return () => {};
    },
    setOptions() {},
  }),
}));

import TreeView from "./TreeView.svelte";

const ok = (data: unknown) => ({ data, response: new Response(null, { status: 200 }) });

function setup(
  over: {
    roots?: unknown;
    error?: unknown;
    many?: number;
    noSchema?: boolean;
    schemaFails?: boolean;
  } = {},
) {
  const tasks = new Map([
    ["P", task("P", "project", null, { context: true, id: "REL-15", title: "リリース準備 v2.4" })],
    ["E1", task("E1", "epic", "P", { id: "REL-1", title: "API v2 への移行" })],
    ["T1", task("T1", "task", "E1", { id: "REL-4", title: "移行ガイドを書く" })],
    ["T3", task("T3", "task", "P", { id: "REL-6", title: "Web のクライアントを更新" })],
  ]);
  let roots = over.roots ?? [
    {
      ulid: "P",
      children: [
        { ulid: "E1", children: [{ ulid: "T1", children: [] }] },
        { ulid: "T3", children: [] },
      ],
    },
  ];
  if (over.many) {
    const kids = Array.from({ length: over.many }, (_, i) => {
      tasks.set(`M${i}`, task(`M${i}`, "task", "P", { id: `REL-${100 + i}`, title: `many ${i}` }));
      return { ulid: `M${i}`, children: [] };
    });
    roots = [{ ulid: "P", children: kids }];
  }
  const ev = emitter();
  let schemaNow: typeof schema | undefined = over.noSchema ? undefined : schema;
  const watchTree = vi.fn(() => ({
    roots: () => roots,
    error: () => over.error ?? null,
    loading: () => false,
    dispose() {},
  }));
  const api = { POST: vi.fn(), PATCH: vi.fn() };
  const core = {
    api,
    cache: {
      task: (u: string) => tasks.get(u),
      live: () => undefined,
      watchTree,
      watchList: vi.fn(() => ({
        ulids: () => [],
        error: () => null,
        loading: () => false,
        dispose() {},
      })),
      watchDetail: vi.fn(() => ({ ulid: () => "P", error: () => null, dispose() {} })),
      applyWrite: vi.fn(),
      subscribe: (fn: () => void) => ev.subscribe(fn),
    },
    stores: {
      workspace: () => ({
        store: "shared",
        tone: "purple",
        ulid: "W1",
        name: "release",
        prefix: "REL",
        projects: [],
      }),
      workspaces: () => [
        {
          store: "shared",
          tone: "purple",
          ulid: "W1",
          name: "release",
          prefix: "REL",
          projects: [],
        },
      ],
      tone: () => "purple",
      subscribe: (fn: () => void) => ev.subscribe(fn),
    },
    schema: {
      get: () => schemaNow,
      load: async () => {
        if (over.schemaFails) throw new Error("schema.yaml が読めません");
        return schema;
      },
      subscribe: (fn: () => void) => ev.subscribe(fn),
    },
  };
  h.core = core;
  return {
    core,
    api,
    watchTree,
    // The schema of the store arrives
    loadSchema() {
      schemaNow = schema;
      ev.emit();
    },
  };
}

beforeEach(() => {
  h.go.mockReset();
  h.toast.mockReset();
  selection.set(null);
});

describe("TreeView", () => {
  it("shows the rows of the project with the count of matched tasks", () => {
    setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect(screen.getByText("API v2 への移行")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("移行ガイドを書く")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("3 件 · 中止 0 件を除外")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("selects a row on click", async () => {
    setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByText("Web のクライアントを更新"));
    expect(selection.ulid).toBe("T3");
  });

  it("keeps the title typed before the schema is read, and adds the task once it is", async () => {
    const { api, loadSchema } = setup({ noSchema: true });
    api.POST.mockImplementation(async (path: string) =>
      path === "/api/tasks"
        ? ok({ task: task("N1", "task", "P", { id: "REL-9", title: "早い" }) })
        : ok({}),
    );
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /タスクを追加/ }));
    const input = screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "新しいタスクのタイトル" });
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute("readonly");
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute("placeholder", "読み込み中…");
    await fireEvent.input(input, { target: { value: "早い" } });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(api.POST).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(input).toHaveValue("早い");

    loadSchema();
    await waitFor(() => expect(input).not.toHaveAttribute("readonly"));
    await fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Enter" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks", expect.anything()));
  });

  it("runs a key pressed before the schema is read once it is, in the order pressed", async () => {
    const { api, loadSchema } = setup({ noSchema: true });
    api.PATCH.mockResolvedValue(
      ok({ task: task("T3", "task", "P", { status: "done" }), commit: "c3" }),
    );
    api.POST.mockResolvedValue(ok({ task: task("T3", "task", "E1"), commit: "c4" }));
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const row = screen
      .getByText("Web のクライアントを更新")
      .closest('[role="treeitem"]') as HTMLElement;
    await fireEvent.click(row);
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: " " });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: "Tab" });
    expect(api.PATCH).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(api.POST).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    loadSchema();
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(api.PATCH).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}", {
      params: { path: { id: "T3" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { status: "done" },
    });
    expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: "T3" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { parent: "E1" },
    });
  });

  it("does not wait for the schema for the keys that do not need it", async () => {
    setup({ noSchema: true });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const row = screen.getByText("API v2 への移行").closest('[role="treeitem"]') as HTMLElement;
    await fireEvent.click(row);
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: "ArrowDown" });
    expect(selection.ulid).toBe("T1");
  });

  it("says so when the schema cannot be read, and drops the keys that waited", async () => {
    const { api } = setup({ noSchema: true, schemaFails: true });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const row = screen
      .getByText("Web のクライアントを更新")
      .closest('[role="treeitem"]') as HTMLElement;
    await fireEvent.click(row);
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: " " });
    await waitFor(() => expect(h.toast).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: " " });
    expect(h.toast).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(api.PATCH).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("folds a row with its chevron", async () => {
    setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "折りたたむ: API v2 への移行" }));
    expect(screen.queryByText("移行ガイドを書く")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("adds a task below the selected row with Enter and puts it in place", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    api.POST.mockImplementation(async (path: string) =>
      path === "/api/tasks"
        ? ok({ task: task("N1", "task", "P", { id: "REL-20", title: "新しい" }), commit: "c1" })
        : ok({ task: task("N1", "task", "P"), commit: "c2" }),
    );
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const row = screen.getByText("API v2 への移行").closest('[role="treeitem"]') as HTMLElement;
    await fireEvent.click(row);
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: "Enter" });
    const input = await screen.findByPlaceholderText("タイトルを入力して Enter（Esc で閉じる）");
    await fireEvent.input(input, { target: { value: "新しい" } });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Enter" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
    expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, "/api/tasks", {
      params: { query: { store: "shared" } },

    });
    expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, "/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: "N1" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { after: "E1" },
    });

  it("completes the selected task with Space", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    api.PATCH.mockResolvedValue(
      ok({ task: task("T3", "task", "P", { status: "done" }), commit: "c3" }),
    );
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const row = screen
      .getByText("Web のクライアントを更新")
      .closest('[role="treeitem"]') as HTMLElement;
    await fireEvent.click(row);
    await fireEvent.keyDown(row, { key: " " });
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.PATCH).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(api.PATCH).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}", {
      params: { path: { id: "T3" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { status: "done" },
    });
  });

  it("does not run the keys while typing in the filter", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const row = screen
      .getByText("Web のクライアントを更新")
      .closest('[role="treeitem"]') as HTMLElement;
    await fireEvent.click(row);
    const filter = screen.getByLabelText("フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）");
    await fireEvent.keyDown(filter, { key: " " });
    expect(api.PATCH).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("asks for the base query plus the typed query, and shows the hh ls line", async () => {
    const { watchTree } = setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect(watchTree).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({
      q: "project:P -status:canceled",
      tree: true,
      store: "shared",
    });
    const filter = screen.getByLabelText("フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）");
    await fireEvent.input(filter, { target: { value: "assignee:@me" } });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(filter, { key: "Enter" });
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(watchTree).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({
        q: "project:P assignee:@me",
        tree: true,
        store: "shared",
      }),
    );
    expect(screen.getByText(/hh ls .*project:REL-15 assignee:@me/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows the message of query.invalid under the filter", () => {
    const error = Object.assign(Object.create(ApiError.prototype), {
      status: 400,
      type: "query.invalid",
      detail: "term 1 (foo:bar): unknown key",
      args: {},
      body: {},
    });
    setup({ error });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("term 1 (foo:bar): unknown key");
  });

  it("draws only the window of rows when the list is long", () => {
    setup({ many: 250 });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("treeitem")).toHaveLength(10);
  });
});

describe("TreeView empty states", () => {
  const empty = [{ ulid: "P", children: [] }];

  it("says that an empty project has no task yet, with no button for a screen that is not there", () => {
    setup({ roots: empty });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect(screen.getByText("リリース準備 v2.4 にはまだタスクがありません")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /タスクを作成/ })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("says that nothing matches a filter and clears it", async () => {
    setup({ roots: empty });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    const filter = screen.getByLabelText("フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）") as HTMLInputElement;
    await fireEvent.input(filter, { target: { value: "assignee:@me" } });
    await fireEvent.keyDown(filter, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(await screen.findByText("一致するタスクはありません")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "フィルタを解除" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(filter.value).toBe(""));
  });

  it("shows no empty state when the query is wrong", () => {
    const error = Object.assign(Object.create(ApiError.prototype), {
      status: 400,
      type: "query.invalid",
      detail: "x",
      args: {},
      body: {},
    });
    setup({ roots: empty, error });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect(screen.queryByText("一致するタスクはありません")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/にはまだタスクがありません/)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/AddRow.svelte`（足元の「タスクを追加」と、行の間に出る入力行を兼ねる）:

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";

  type Props = {
    editing: boolean;
    depth?: number;
    where: string; // "release / リリース準備 v2.4"
    kindLabel: string; // "タスク"
    ready?: boolean; // false while the schema of the store is not read: the title waits
    onstart: () => void;
    onsubmit: (title: string) => void | Promise<void>;
    oncancel: () => void;
  };
  let {
    editing,
    depth = 0,
    where,
    kindLabel,
    ready = true,
    onstart,
    onsubmit,
    oncancel,
  }: Props = $props();

  let title = $state("");
  let busy = $state(false);

  async function submit() {
    const t = title.trim();
    if (t === "") {
      oncancel();
      return;
    }
    if (busy || !ready) return;
    busy = true;
    try {
      await onsubmit(t);
      title = "";
    } finally {
      busy = false;
    }
  }

  function onkeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.isComposing) {
      e.preventDefault();
      void submit();
    } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
      e.preventDefault();
      oncancel();
    }
  }

  function focusOnMount(el: HTMLInputElement) {
    el.focus();
  }
</script>

{#if editing}
  <div class="add editing" style:padding-left="{2.4 + depth * 1.6}rem">
    <Icon name="plus" size={14} />
    <input
      class="title"
      type="text"
      aria-label="新しいタスクのタイトル"
      placeholder={ready ? "タイトルを入力して Enter（Esc で閉じる）" : "読み込み中…"}
      readonly={!ready}
      bind:value={title}
      {onkeydown}
      {@attach focusOnMount}
    >
    <span class="where">→ {where} · 種類: {kindLabel}</span>
  </div>
{:else}
  <button class="add" type="button" onclick={onstart}>
    <Icon name="plus" size={14} />
    <span>タスクを追加</span>
    <span class="where">→ {where} · 種類: {kindLabel}</span>
  </button>
{/if}
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { ApiError } from "../../core/api/client";
  import { errorMessage } from "../../core/messages";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import CliLine from "../ui/CliLine.svelte";

  type Props = {
    base: string; // the fixed part, as the user would type it
    applied: string; // what is applied now
    count: string; // "16 件 · 中止 1 件を除外"
    cli: string; // the hh ls line
    error: unknown;
    onchange: (q: string) => void;
  };
  let { base, applied, count, cli, error, onchange }: Props = $props();

  let typed = $state("");
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

  // Follow what is applied from outside (the empty state clears it, a new view).
  $effect(() => {
    typed = applied;
  });

  function input(e: Event) {
    typed = (e.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => onchange(typed.trim()), 300);
  }

  function keydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.isComposing) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      onchange(typed.trim());
    }
  }

  const message = $derived(error ? errorMessage(error) : null);
  const detail = $derived(
    error instanceof ApiError && error.type === "query.invalid" ? error.detail : null,
  );
</script>

<div class="bar">
  <div class="field" class:bad={message !== null}>
    <Icon name="filter" size={15} />
    <span class="base" title="この画面で固定のクエリ">{base}</span>
    <label class="sr" for="tree-q">フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）</label>
    <input
      id="tree-q"
      type="text"
      value={typed}
      spellcheck="false"
      autocomplete="off"
      placeholder="例: assignee:@me -status:done"
      oninput={input}
      onkeydown={keydown}
    >
    <span class="count">{count}</span>
    <CliLine command={cli} />
  </div>
  {#if message}
    <p class="error" role="alert">
      {message}
      {#if detail}
        <code>{detail}</code>
      {/if}
    </p>
  {/if}
</div>
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import type { LiveMark } from "../../core/cache";
  import { formatDate } from "../../core/format";
  import type { SchemaView } from "../../core/schema";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import KindIcon from "../icons/KindIcon.svelte";
  import StatusIcon from "../icons/StatusIcon.svelte";
  import Avatar from "../ui/Avatar.svelte";
  import IdLabel from "../ui/IdLabel.svelte";
  import type { FlatRow } from "./flatten";
  import { problemLabel, propText } from "./marks";

  type Props = {
    row: FlatRow;
    schema: SchemaView | undefined;
    selected: boolean;
    focusable: boolean; // tabindex 0: one row of the tree takes the Tab key
    live: LiveMark | undefined;
    blockedBy: string | undefined; // the ID of the first blocker, when the task is blocked
    onselect: () => void;
    ontoggle: () => void;
  };
  let { row, schema, selected, focusable, live, blockedBy, onselect, ontoggle }: Props = $props();

  const task = $derived(row.task);
  const category = $derived(schema?.statuses?.find((s) => s.name === task.status)?.category);
  const role = $derived(schema?.kinds?.find((k) => k.name === task.kind)?.role);
  const closed = $derived(category === "done" || category === "canceled");
  const problem = $derived(task.problems?.[0]);
  const due = $derived(propText(task, "due"));
  const priority = $derived(propText(task, "priority"));
  const assignee = $derived(task.assignees?.[0]);
  const progress = $derived(task.progress && task.progress.total > 0 ? task.progress : undefined);
  const percent = $derived(progress ? Math.round((progress.done / progress.total) * 100) : 0);
</script>

<!-- svelte-ignore a11y_click_events_have_key_events -->
<!-- biome-ignore lint/a11y/useKeyWithClickEvents: the keys are handled by the tree that holds the rows -->
<div
  class="row"
  class:selected
  role="treeitem"
  aria-level={row.depth + 1}
  aria-selected={selected}
  aria-expanded={row.hasChildren ? row.expanded : undefined}
  tabindex={focusable ? 0 : -1}
  data-ulid={row.ulid}
  onclick={onselect}
>
  <span class="lead" style:padding-left="{0.4 + row.depth * 1.6}rem">
    {#if row.hasChildren}
      <button
        type="button"
        class="chev"
        tabindex="-1"
        aria-label="{row.expanded ? "折りたたむ" : "展開する"}: {task.title}"
        aria-expanded={row.expanded}
        onclick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          ontoggle();
        }}
        ondblclick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <Icon name={row.expanded ? "chevron-down" : "chevron-right"} size={14} />
      </button>
    {:else}
      <span class="chev-gap"></span>
    {/if}
    <StatusIcon status={task.status} {category} />
    <KindIcon kind={task.kind} {role} />
    <IdLabel id={task.id} ulid={task.ulid} />
    <span class="title" class:closed class:container={task.kind === "epic"}>{task.title}</span>

    {#if progress}
      <span class="progress">
        <span class="bar"><span class="fill" style:width="{percent}%"></span></span>
        {progress.done}/{progress.total}
      </span>
    {:else if row.hasChildren && !row.expanded}
      <span class="progress">子 {row.childCount}</span>
    {/if}
    {#if task.checklist && task.checklist.total > 0}
      <span class="check" title="チェックリスト">
        <Icon name="checklist" size={10} />{task.checklist.done}/{task.checklist.total}
      </span>
    {/if}
    {#if live}
      <span class="live">{live.label}</span>
    {/if}
    {#if task.broken}
      <span class="warn" title={task.broken}> <Icon name="warning" size={11} />壊れています </span>
    {:else if problem}
      <span class="warn" title={problem.message}>
        <Icon name="warning" size={11} />{problemLabel(problem)}
      </span>
    {/if}
  </span>

  <span class="tail">
    {#if blockedBy}
      <span class="blocked"><Icon name="forbid" size={12} />{blockedBy} 待ち</span>
    {/if}
    {#if priority}
      <span class="priority {priority}">{priority}</span>
    {/if}
    <span class="due">{due ? formatDate(due, new Date()) : ""}</span>
    <span class="who">
      {#if assignee}
        <Avatar name={assignee} />
      {/if}
    </span>
  </span>
</div>
```
