`web/app/tree/TreeView.svelte`:

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { createVirtualizer } from "@tanstack/svelte-virtual";
  import { tick } from "svelte";
  import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";
  import { cliLs } from "../../core/cli";
  import { errorMessage } from "../../core/messages";
  import { core } from "../context";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import KindIcon from "../icons/KindIcon.svelte";
  import { modKey } from "../keys";
  import { router } from "../router";
  import { selection } from "../selection.svelte";
  import Empty from "../states/Empty.svelte";
  import { useSubscribe } from "../svelte-store";
  import { toast } from "../toast.svelte";
  import Menu from "../ui/Menu.svelte";
  import StoreBadge from "../ui/StoreBadge.svelte";
  import AddRow from "./AddRow.svelte";
  import { createBelow, moveTask, patchTask } from "./actions";
  import FilterBar from "./FilterBar.svelte";
  import { baseQuery, combine, hasTerm, NO_CANCELED, type QueryBase, withoutTerm } from "./filter";
  import {
    countLabel,
    countMatched,
    type FlatRow,
    flatten,
    ROW_HEIGHT,
    VIRTUALIZE_AT,
  } from "./flatten";
  import { type KeyAction, type KeyInput, keyAction, needsSchema } from "./keyboard";
  import { openBlocker } from "./marks";
  import { checkParent, kindLabel, pickKind } from "./placement";
  import TreeRow from "./TreeRow.svelte";

  type Scope =
    | { kind: "workspace"; store: string; ulid: string }
    | { kind: "project"; ulid: string };
  // The prop is not called scope: Biome reads that name as the HTML attribute.
  let { view: scope }: { view: Scope } = $props();

  const c = core();
  const cacheRev = useSubscribe(c.cache, () => ({}));
  const storesRev = useSubscribe(c.stores, () => ({}));
  const schemaRev = useSubscribe(c.schema, () => ({}));

  // --- what the view shows -------------------------------------------------
  const project = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return scope.kind === "project" ? c.cache.task(scope.ulid) : undefined;
  });
  const store = $derived(scope.kind === "workspace" ? scope.store : project?.store);
  const workspace = $derived.by(() => {
    storesRev.current;
    if (scope.kind === "workspace") return c.stores.workspace(scope.store, scope.ulid);
    if (!project) return undefined;
    return c.stores
      .workspaces()
      .find((w) => w.store === project.store && w.ulid === project.workspace.ulid);
  });
  const schema = $derived.by(() => {
    schemaRev.current;
    return store ? c.schema.get(store) : undefined;
  });
  const viewRoot = $derived(scope.kind === "project" ? scope.ulid : null);
  const base = $derived<QueryBase | null>(
    scope.kind === "workspace"
      ? workspace
        ? { kind: "workspace", name: workspace.name }
        : null
      : project
        ? { kind: "project", ulid: project.ulid, id: project.id }
        : null,
  );
  const apiBase = $derived(base ? baseQuery(base, "api") : null);
  const cliBase = $derived(base ? baseQuery(base, "cli") : "");

  $effect(() => {
    if (scope.kind !== "project") return;
    const h = c.cache.watchDetail(scope.ulid);
    return () => h.dispose();
  });
  // Keys that need the schema, pressed before it is read, wait here and run once it is.
  let waiting: KeyInput[] = [];
  let schemaFailed = false;
  $effect(() => {
    if (!store) return;
    schemaFailed = false;
    c.schema.load(store).catch((err) => {
      schemaFailed = true;
      if (waiting.length > 0) toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
      waiting = [];
    });
  });
  $effect(() => {
    if (!schema || waiting.length === 0) return;
    const keys = waiting;
    waiting = [];
    void (async () => {
      for (const k of keys) {
        const a = actionOf(k);
        if (a.type !== "none") await run(a);
      }
    })();
  });

  // --- the query -----------------------------------------------------------
  // The default filter leaves the canceled tasks out; clearing the field shows them.
  let applied = $state<string>(NO_CANCELED);

  type TreeHandle = ReturnType<typeof c.cache.watchTree>;
  type ListHandle = ReturnType<typeof c.cache.watchList>;
  let tree = $state<TreeHandle | null>(null);
  let blockedList = $state<ListHandle | null>(null);
  let canceledList = $state<ListHandle | null>(null);

  $effect(() => {
    if (!apiBase || !store) {
      tree = null;
      return;
    }
    const h = c.cache.watchTree({ q: combine(apiBase, applied), tree: true, store });
    tree = h;
    return () => h.dispose();
  });
  $effect(() => {
    if (!apiBase || !store) {
      blockedList = null;
      return;
    }
    const h = c.cache.watchList({ q: combine(apiBase, "is:blocked"), tree: false, store });
    blockedList = h;
    return () => h.dispose();
  });
  $effect(() => {
    if (!apiBase || !store || !hasTerm(applied, NO_CANCELED)) {
      canceledList = null;
      return;
    }
    const q = combine(apiBase, `${withoutTerm(applied, NO_CANCELED)} status:canceled`);
    const h = c.cache.watchList({ q, tree: false, store });
    canceledList = h;
    return () => h.dispose();
  });

  const roots = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return tree?.roots() ?? [];
  });
  const loading = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return base === null || (tree?.loading() ?? true);
  });
  const treeError = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return tree?.error() ?? null;
  });
  const blocked = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return new Set(blockedList?.ulids() ?? []);
  });
  const canceledCount = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return canceledList ? canceledList.ulids().length : null;
  });

  function isClosed(t: TaskView): boolean {
    const category = c.schema.get(t.store)?.statuses?.find((s) => s.name === t.status)?.category;
    return category === "done" || category === "canceled";
  }

  function taskOf(ulid: string): TaskView | undefined {
    return c.cache.task(ulid);
  }

  // --- the rows --------------------------------------------------------------
  let collapsed = $state<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const rows = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return flatten(roots, viewRoot, taskOf, collapsed);
  });
  const matched = $derived.by(() => {
    cacheRev.current;
    return countMatched(roots, viewRoot, taskOf);
  });

  type Adding = { after: string | null; parent: string | null; depth: number };
  let adding = $state<Adding | null>(null);
  type Item = { type: "row"; row: FlatRow } | { type: "add"; depth: number };
  const items = $derived.by<Item[]>(() => {
    const list: Item[] = rows.map((row) => ({ type: "row", row }));
    if (!adding) return list;
    let at = list.length;
    if (adding.after !== null) {
      const i = rows.findIndex((r) => r.ulid === adding?.after);
      if (i >= 0) {
        const depth = rows[i]?.depth ?? 0;
        at = i + 1;
        while (at < rows.length && (rows[at]?.depth ?? 0) > depth) at++;
      }
    }
    list.splice(at, 0, { type: "add", depth: adding.depth });
    return list;
  });

  function select(ulid: string | null) {
    selection.set(ulid);
  }
  async function focusRow(ulid: string) {
    const i = items.findIndex((it) => it.type === "row" && it.row.ulid === ulid);
    if (i >= 0) vinst?.scrollToIndex(i);
    await tick();
    scroller?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-ulid="${ulid}"]`)?.focus();
  }
  function toggle(ulid: string, openIt: boolean) {
    const next = new Set(collapsed);
    if (openIt) next.delete(ulid);
    else next.add(ulid);
    collapsed = next;
  }

  // --- virtual scrolling -------------------------------------------------------
  const virtualized = $derived(items.length > VIRTUALIZE_AT);
  let scroller = $state<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  type Virtualizer = {
    getVirtualItems(): { index: number; start: number }[];
    getTotalSize(): number;
    scrollToIndex(i: number): void;
    setOptions(o: { count: number; getScrollElement: () => HTMLDivElement | null }): void;
  };
  let vinst = $state.raw<Virtualizer | null>(null);
  let windowed = $state<{ index: number; start: number }[]>([]);
  let totalSize = $state(0);
  const virtualizer = createVirtualizer<HTMLDivElement, HTMLDivElement>({
    count: 0,
    getScrollElement: () => scroller,
    estimateSize: () => ROW_HEIGHT,
    overscan: 12,
  });
  $effect(() =>
    virtualizer.subscribe((v) => {
      vinst = v as unknown as Virtualizer;
      windowed = v.getVirtualItems();
      totalSize = v.getTotalSize();
    }),
  );
  // The window is only asked for while the list is long.
  $effect(() => {
    vinst?.setOptions({
      count: virtualized ? items.length : 0,
      getScrollElement: () => scroller,
    });
  });

  // --- keys ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  let menu = $state<{ which: "status" | "kind"; ulid: string } | null>(null);

  function actionOf(k: KeyInput): KeyAction {
    return keyAction(k, { rows, selected: selection.ulid, schema, viewRoot, taskOf });
  }

  function onkeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if ((e.target as HTMLElement).closest("input, textarea, select, [contenteditable='true']"))
      return;
    const k = { key: e.key, shift: e.shiftKey, alt: e.altKey, mod: modKey(e) };
    if (!schema && needsSchema(k)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (schemaFailed) toast({ message: `${store} の schema.yaml を読み込めていません` });
      else waiting.push(k);
      return;
    }
    const a = actionOf(k);
    if (a.type === "none") return;
    e.preventDefault();
    void run(a);
  }

  async function run(a: KeyAction) {
    try {
      switch (a.type) {
        case "select":
          select(a.ulid);
          await focusRow(a.ulid);
          break;
        case "toggle":
          toggle(a.ulid, a.open);
          break;
        case "add-below": {
          const depth = rows.find((r) => r.ulid === a.after)?.depth ?? 0;
          adding = { after: a.after, parent: a.parent, depth };
          break;
        }
        case "move": {
          const t = taskOf(a.ulid);
          if (!t) break;
          if (a.to.parent) toggle(a.to.parent, true);
          await moveTask(c, t, a.to);
          await focusRow(a.ulid);
          break;
        }
        case "set-status": {
          const t = taskOf(a.ulid);
          if (t) await patchTask(c, t, { status: a.status });
          break;
        }
        case "menu":
          menu = { which: a.which, ulid: a.ulid };
          break;
        case "reject":
          toast({ message: `${a.rejection.title}。${a.rejection.reason}` });
          break;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  }

  const menuTask = $derived(menu ? taskOf(menu.ulid) : undefined);
  const menuItems = $derived.by(() => {
    if (!menu || !menuTask || !schema) return [];
    if (menu.which === "status") {
      return (schema.statuses ?? []).map((s) => ({ value: s.name, label: s.label || s.name }));
    }
    const parent = menuTask.parent ? taskOf(menuTask.parent) : undefined;
    return (schema.kinds ?? []).map((k) => {
      const why = checkParent(schema, { ...menuTask, kind: k.name }, parent ?? null);
      return { value: k.name, label: k.label || k.name, disabled: why !== null, hint: why?.title };
    });
  });
  async function pickFromMenu(value: string) {
    const t = menuTask;
    const which = menu?.which;
    menu = null;
    if (!t || !which) return;
    try {
      await patchTask(c, t, which === "status" ? { status: value } : { kind: value });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  }

  // --- adding ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const addKind = $derived.by(() => {
    if (!schema) return null;
    const parentId = adding ? adding.parent : viewRoot;
    const parent = parentId ? taskOf(parentId) : undefined;
    return pickKind(schema, parent?.kind ?? null);
  });
  const whereLabel = $derived(
    scope.kind === "project" && project
      ? `${project.workspace.name} / ${project.title}`
      : (workspace?.name ?? ""),
  );

  function startAdd(a: Adding) {
    adding = a;
  }

  async function submitAdd(title: string) {
    const a = adding;
    if (!a || !store || !workspace || !schema) return;
    const parent = a.parent ? taskOf(a.parent) : undefined;
    const kind = pickKind(schema, parent?.kind ?? null);
    if (!kind) {
      toast({
        message: `${parent ? `${kindLabel(schema, parent.kind)}の下` : "ルート"}に置ける種類がありません（${store} の schema.yaml の kinds）`,
      });
      adding = null;
      return;
    }
    const siblings = rows.filter((r) => r.parent === a.parent);
    try {
      const created = await createBelow(c, {
        title,
        kind,
        store,
        parent: a.parent,
        workspace: workspace.prefix,
        after: a.after,
        afterIsLast: a.after === null || siblings[siblings.length - 1]?.ulid === a.after,
      });
      select(created.ulid);
      // keep going below the new row, as an outliner does
      adding = { after: created.ulid, parent: a.parent, depth: a.depth };
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  }

  // --- reading the screen ---------------------------------------------------------------
  const cli = $derived(
    cliLs(combine(cliBase, applied), true, scope.kind === "workspace" ? store : undefined),
  );
  const rowFocus = $derived(selection.ulid ?? rows[0]?.ulid ?? null);
  const noTasks = $derived(
    withoutTerm(applied, NO_CANCELED).trim() === "" && !(canceledCount && canceledCount > 0),
  );
  const crumbTitle = $derived(
    scope.kind === "project" ? (project?.title ?? "") : (workspace?.name ?? ""),
  );
  // Spread, because Biome reads a prop called scope as the HTML attribute.
  const emptyProps = $derived({ scope: scope.kind, name: crumbTitle });
</script>

<section class="tree" aria-label="ツリー">
  <header class="head">
    <nav class="crumb" aria-label="パンくず">
      {#if store}
        <StoreBadge {store} tone={c.stores.tone(store)} />
      {/if}
      {#if scope.kind === "project"}
        {#if workspace}
          <a
            class="ws"
            href={router.href({ name: "workspace", store: workspace.store, ulid: workspace.ulid })}
          >
            <Icon name="workspace" size={16} />{workspace.name}
          </a>
          <span class="sep" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
        {/if}
        <KindIcon kind="project" />
        <h1>{crumbTitle}</h1>
        {#if project}
          <span class="id">{project.id}</span>
        {/if}
      {:else}
        <Icon name="workspace" size={16} />
        <h1>{crumbTitle}</h1>
        {#if workspace}
          <span class="id">{workspace.prefix}</span>
        {/if}
      {/if}
    </nav>
  </header>

  <nav class="tabs" aria-label="ビューの切り替え">
    <span class="tab" aria-current="page">ツリー</span>
  </nav>

  <FilterBar
    base={cliBase}
    {applied}
    count={countLabel(matched, canceledCount)}
    {cli}
    error={treeError}
    onchange={(q) => (applied = q)}
  />

  <!-- svelte-ignore a11y_no_noninteractive_tabindex -->
  <div
    class="scroller"
    role="tree"
    aria-label="{crumbTitle} のタスク"
    tabindex="-1"
    bind:this={scroller}
    {onkeydown}
  >
    {#snippet entry(item: Item)}
      {#if item.type === "row"}
        <TreeRow
          row={item.row}
          {schema}
          selected={selection.ulid === item.row.ulid}
          focusable={rowFocus === item.row.ulid}
          live={c.cache.live(item.row.ulid)}
          blockedBy={openBlocker(item.row.task, taskOf, isClosed, blocked.has(item.row.ulid))}
          onselect={() => select(item.row.ulid)}
          ontoggle={() => toggle(item.row.ulid, !item.row.expanded)}
        />
      {:else}
        <AddRow
          editing={true}
          depth={item.depth}
          where={whereLabel}
          kindLabel={schema && addKind ? kindLabel(schema, addKind) : ""}
          ready={!!(store && workspace && schema)}
          onstart={() => {}}
          onsubmit={submitAdd}
          oncancel={() => (adding = null)}
        />
      {/if}
    {/snippet}

    {#if loading && rows.length === 0}
      <p class="note">読み込み中…</p>
    {:else if rows.length === 0 && !adding && !treeError}
      {#if noTasks}
        <Empty variant="empty" {...emptyProps} />
      {:else}
        <Empty
          variant="nomatch"
          note={hasTerm(applied, NO_CANCELED) ? "中止を除外しています" : undefined}
          onClear={() => (applied = "")}
        />
      {/if}
    {/if}

    {#if virtualized}
      <div class="vbox" style:height="{totalSize}px">
        {#each windowed as v (v.index)}
          {@const item = items[v.index]}
          {#if item}
            <div class="vrow" style:transform="translateY({v.start}px)">
              {@render entry(item)}
            </div>
          {/if}
        {/each}
      </div>
    {:else}
      <div class="list">
        {#each items as item (item.type === "row" ? item.row.ulid : "add")}
          {@render entry(item)}
        {/each}
      </div>
    {/if}

    {#if !adding}
      <div class="foot-add">
        <AddRow
          editing={false}
          where={whereLabel}
          kindLabel={schema && addKind ? kindLabel(schema, addKind) : ""}
          onstart={() => startAdd({ after: null, parent: viewRoot, depth: 0 })}
          onsubmit={submitAdd}
          oncancel={() => {}}
        />
      </div>
    {/if}
  </div>

  <footer class="keys">
    <span><kbd>Enter</kbd> 下に追加</span>
    <span><kbd>Tab</kbd> / <kbd>⇧Tab</kbd> 字下げ・戻す</span>
    <span><kbd>⌥↑↓</kbd> 並べ替え</span>
    <span><kbd>Space</kbd> 完了</span>
    <span><kbd>S</kbd> ステータス</span>
    <span><kbd>K</kbd> 種類</span>
    <span class="dnd">ドラッグで並べ替え・親の付け替え（置ける親の種類だけ受け付けます）</span>
  </footer>
</section>

{#if menu && menuItems.length > 0}
  <Menu
    anchor={scroller?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-ulid="${menu.ulid}"]`) ?? null}
    items={menuItems}
    onselect={(v) => void pickFromMenu(v)}
    onclose={() => {
      const u = menu?.ulid;
      menu = null;
      if (u) void focusRow(u);
    }}
  />
{/if}

<style>

    box-sizing: border-box;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    height: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    min-height: 0;
  }

    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;

  }

  }
  .ws {

    color: var(--ink-2);
    text-decoration: none;
  }
  .sep {
    color: var(--muted-2);
  }

  }

    min-height: 2.25rem;

    margin-bottom: calc(var(--hairline) * -1);

  }
  .scroller {
    flex: 1 1 auto;

  }
  .list {

  .vbox {

    padding: 0.625rem 1.75rem;
    border-top: var(--hairline) solid var(--line);
    color: var(--muted);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
  }
  kbd {
    padding: 0 0.3rem;

    font-size: 0.7rem;
    color: var(--ink-2);
  }
  .dnd {
    margin-left: auto;
  }
</style>
```

```svelte
        {#key `${router.route.store}/${router.route.ulid}`}

          />
        {/key}
      {:else if router.route.name === "project"}
```

（Task 11 が task のルートを `{:else if}` で足し、Task 15 が home のルート（最初のワークスペースのツリー）を足す。それまでの分岐は触らない。）

### Task 10: ツリーのドラッグ

細部 24。`@atlaskit/pragmatic-drag-and-drop` の `draggable` と `dropTargetForElements`、`-hitbox` の `tree-item` の `attachInstruction` / `extractInstruction`、`-auto-scroll` を使う。置けるかの判定は DOM に触れない純粋な関数 `dropDecision` に分け、テストで網羅する（ブラウザでのドラッグ操作そのものは Task 16 の E2E で確かめる）。

この Task で決めること（承認待ち）:

1. **ドロップの位置**: hitbox の `standard` モード。行の上の 1/4 が「前」（`reorder-above`）、下の 1/4 が「後ろ」（`reorder-below`）、中央が「子にする」（`make-child`）。細部 24 の「中央の帯で子にする」をこの割合で実装する。`reparent`（字下げの位置で親を決める）は使わない。親を変えるのは「子にする」か、別の親の下の行の前・後ろ
2. **`move` の引数**: 「子にする」は `{ parent: <対象> }`（末尾）。「前・後ろ」は対象の親を `parent` に渡し、対象を `before` / `after` に渡す。対象がワークスペース直下（親なし）なら `{ root: true, before | after }`。`MoveInputBody` の `before`・`after` は「移動後の親」の子でなければ `move.invalid` になるため、対象の親を必ず合わせる
3. **拒否するもの**（ドラッグ中に赤枠と「ここには置けません」、離したらトーストで理由を出してデータは変えない）: 別の Store、別のワークスペース、自分の子孫の下、スキーマの `kinds[].parents` に合わない親。スキーマがまだ読み込めていないときも拒否する（理由は「少し待ってからやり直してください」）。自分自身の上、すでにその位置にある前・後ろへの移動は何もしない（拒否の表示も出さない）
4. **拒否の文言**: 「<種類>は<親の種類>の下に置けません」（canvas の「エピックはタスクの下に置けません」）と、理由「「<ID> <タイトル>」は<種類>です。<種類>を置ける親は…だけです（<Store> の schema.yaml の kinds）。」（Task 9 の `checkParent` と同じ文言）。canvas の下部の「種類の設定」リンクは 4f（Store の設定）の画面への入口なので 4d では作らない
5. **別ワークスペースへの移動はドラッグでしない**: `move` は別のワークスペースの親の下へ置けるが、スプリントやリリースが外れる（`Detached`）。ツリーの中での事故を避けるため、ドラッグは同じワークスペースの中に限る

**Files:**
- Create: `web/app/tree/dnd.ts`
- Modify: `web/app/tree/TreeRow.svelte`（props `dnd`・`dragging`・`dropMark` と、赤枠・「ここには置けません」・挿入線の表示。全文を下に載せる）
- Modify: `web/app/tree/TreeView.svelte`（ドラッグの状態、`dropOn`、拒否の説明、自動スクロール）
- Test: `web/app/tree/dnd.test.ts`、`web/app/tree/TreeView.dnd.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9 の `MoveArgs`・`checkParent`・`moveTask`・`TreeRow`・`TreeView`（prop は `view`）・`fixtures`、`SchemaView`（`core/schema`）、`TaskView`
- Produces:
  - `DropPlace = "before" | "after" | "child"`
  - `dropDecision(i: DropInput): DropDecision`、`placeOf(instruction)`、`rowDnd(o: RowDndOptions): Attachment<HTMLElement>`、`treeAutoScroll(): Attachment<HTMLElement>`（`tree/dnd.ts`）

- [ ] **Step 1: 失敗するテストを書く**

`web/app/tree/dnd.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { dropDecision, placeOf } from "./dnd";
import { schema, task } from "./fixtures";

// P (project)
//  ├ E1 (epic) ─ T1, T2
//  ├ T7 (task) ─ T8
//  └ E8 (epic, REL-8 リリース作業)
const all = [
  task("P", "project", null),
  task("E1", "epic", "P"),
  task("T1", "task", "E1"),
  task("T2", "task", "E1"),
  task("T7", "task", "P"),
  task("T8", "task", "T7"),
  task("E8", "epic", "P", { id: "REL-8", title: "リリース作業" }),
];
const map = new Map(all.map((t) => [t.ulid, t]));
const taskOf = (u: string) => map.get(u);
const at = (u: string) => map.get(u) as NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof taskOf>>;
const siblings = ["E1", "T7", "E8"]; // under P, in order

function decide(
  dragged: string,
  target: string,
  place: "before" | "after" | "child",
  sib = siblings,
) {
  return dropDecision({
    dragged: at(dragged),
    target: at(target),
    place,
    schema,
    taskOf,
    siblings: sib,
  });
}

describe("placeOf", () => {
  it("maps the hitbox instruction to a place", () => {
    expect(placeOf({ type: "reorder-above" } as never)).toBe("before");
    expect(placeOf({ type: "reorder-below" } as never)).toBe("after");
    expect(placeOf({ type: "make-child" } as never)).toBe("child");
    expect(placeOf({ type: "instruction-blocked" } as never)).toBeNull();
    expect(placeOf(null)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("dropDecision: where the task goes", () => {
  it("makes a child at the end of the target", () => {
    expect(decide("T7", "E1", "child")).toEqual({ ok: true, to: { parent: "E1" } });
  });
  it("puts it before or after the target, under the target's parent", () => {
    expect(decide("E8", "E1", "before")).toEqual({ ok: true, to: { parent: "P", before: "E1" } });
    expect(decide("E8", "E1", "after")).toEqual({ ok: true, to: { parent: "P", after: "E1" } });
  });
  it("puts it at the top level next to a root", () => {
    const root = task("R1", "task", null);
    expect(
      dropDecision({
        dragged: at("T1"),
        target: root,
        place: "after",
        schema,
        taskOf,
        siblings: ["R1"],
      }),
    ).toEqual({ ok: true, to: { root: true, after: "R1" } });
  });
  it("moves among the siblings of another parent", () => {
    expect(decide("T8", "T1", "before", ["T1", "T2"])).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      to: { parent: "E1", before: "T1" },
    });
  });
});

describe("dropDecision: what it refuses", () => {
  it("refuses an epic under a task, in the words of the canvas", () => {
    expect(decide("E8", "T7", "child")).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      silent: false,
      title: "エピックはタスクの下に置けません",
      reason:
        "「REL-8 リリース作業」はエピックです。エピックを置ける親はルートとプロジェクトだけです（shared の schema.yaml の kinds）。",
    });
  });
  it("refuses a place whose parent is a task when the kind needs another parent", () => {
    // before T8: the parent would be T7, a task
    expect(decide("E8", "T8", "before", ["T8"])).toMatchObject({
      ok: false,
      title: "エピックはタスクの下に置けません",
    });
  });
  it("refuses another store", () => {
    const other = task("X1", "task", null, { store: "backend" });
    expect(
      dropDecision({
        dragged: at("T7"),
        target: other,
        place: "child",
        schema,
        taskOf,
        siblings: [],
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: false, title: "別の Store のタスクの下には置けません" });
    expect(
      dropDecision({
        dragged: at("T7"),
        target: other,
        place: "after",
        schema,
        taskOf,
        siblings: [],
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ ok: false, title: "別の Store の並びには置けません" });
  });
  it("refuses another workspace", () => {
    const other = task("W2", "task", "P", {
      workspace: { ulid: "W9", name: "other", id_prefix: "OTH" },
    });
    expect(
      dropDecision({
        dragged: at("T7"),
        target: other,
        place: "child",
        schema,
        taskOf,
        siblings: [],
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: false, title: "別のワークスペースには置けません" });
  });
  it("refuses its own descendants as a parent", () => {
    expect(decide("E1", "T1", "child")).toMatchObject({
      ok: false,
      silent: false,
      title: "自分の子孫の下には置けません",
    });
    expect(decide("E1", "T1", "before", ["T1", "T2"])).toMatchObject({
      ok: false,
      title: "自分の子孫の下には置けません",
    });
    expect(decide("T7", "T8", "child")).toMatchObject({
      ok: false,
      title: "自分の子孫の下には置けません",
    });
  });
  it("refuses while the schema is not loaded", () => {
    expect(
      dropDecision({
        dragged: at("T7"),
        target: at("E1"),
        place: "child",
        schema: undefined,
        taskOf,
        siblings,
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: false, title: "少し待ってからやり直してください" });
  });
  it("refuses when the new parent is not loaded", () => {
    const orphan = task("O1", "task", "MISSING");
    expect(
      dropDecision({
        dragged: at("T7"),
        target: orphan,
        place: "after",
        schema,
        taskOf,
        siblings: ["O1"],
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ ok: false, title: "置き先の親を読み込めていません" });
  });
});

describe("dropDecision: what it ignores", () => {
  it("ignores a drop on itself", () => {
    expect(decide("T7", "T7", "child")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: true });
    expect(decide("T7", "T7", "before")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: true });
  });
  it("ignores a move to where the task already is", () => {
    // the order is E1, T7, E8
    expect(decide("T7", "E1", "after")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: true });
    expect(decide("T7", "E8", "before")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, silent: true });
  });
  it("does not ignore a real reorder", () => {
    expect(decide("T7", "E1", "before")).toEqual({ ok: true, to: { parent: "P", before: "E1" } });
    expect(decide("T7", "E8", "after")).toEqual({ ok: true, to: { parent: "P", after: "E8" } });
  });
});
```

`web/app/tree/TreeView.dnd.test.ts`（`rowDnd` を差し替えて、`TreeView` がドラッグの結果をどう扱うかを確かめる）:

```ts
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { schema, task } from "./fixtures";

type Opts = {
  ulid: string;
  onDragStart: () => void;
  onDragEnd: () => void;
  onOver: (place: string | null, source: string | null) => void;
  onDrop: (source: string, place: string) => void;
};
const h = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  core: undefined as unknown,
  toast: vi.fn(),
  rows: new Map<string, unknown>(),
}));

vi.mock("../context", () => ({ core: () => h.core }));
vi.mock("../router", () => ({
  router: { route: { name: "project", ulid: "P" }, go: vi.fn(), href: () => "/" },
}));
vi.mock("../toast.svelte", () => ({ toast: h.toast }));
vi.mock("@tanstack/svelte-virtual", () => ({
  createVirtualizer: () => ({
    subscribe(fn: (v: unknown) => void) {
      fn({ getVirtualItems: () => [], getTotalSize: () => 0, scrollToIndex() {}, setOptions() {} });
      return () => {};
    },
    setOptions() {},
  }),
}));
vi.mock("./dnd", async (orig) => {
  const real = await orig<typeof import("./dnd")>();
  return {
    ...real,
    rowDnd: (o: Opts) => () => {
      h.rows.set(o.ulid, o);
      return () => {};
    },
    treeAutoScroll: () => () => () => {},
  };
});

import TreeView from "./TreeView.svelte";

const ok = (data: unknown) => ({ data, response: new Response(null, { status: 200 }) });

function setup() {
  const tasks = new Map([
    ["P", task("P", "project", null, { context: true, id: "REL-15", title: "リリース準備 v2.4" })],
    ["E8", task("E8", "epic", "P", { id: "REL-8", title: "リリース作業" })],
    ["T7", task("T7", "task", "P", { id: "REL-7", title: "スキーマを再生成" })],
    ["T9", task("T9", "task", "P", { id: "REL-9", title: "負荷試験" })],
  ]);
  const roots = [
    {
      ulid: "P",
      children: [
        { ulid: "E8", children: [] },
        { ulid: "T7", children: [] },
        { ulid: "T9", children: [] },
      ],
    },
  ];
  const none = () => () => {};
  const ws = {
    store: "shared",
    tone: "purple",
    ulid: "W1",
    name: "release",
    prefix: "REL",
    projects: [],
  };
  const api = { POST: vi.fn(), PATCH: vi.fn() };
  h.core = {
    api,
    cache: {
      task: (u: string) => tasks.get(u),
      live: () => undefined,
      watchTree: () => ({
        roots: () => roots,
        error: () => null,
        loading: () => false,
        dispose() {},
      }),
      watchList: () => ({ ulids: () => [], error: () => null, loading: () => false, dispose() {} }),
      watchDetail: () => ({ ulid: () => "P", error: () => null, dispose() {} }),
      applyWrite: vi.fn(),
      subscribe: none,
    },
    stores: { workspace: () => ws, workspaces: () => [ws], tone: () => "purple", subscribe: none },
    schema: { get: () => schema, load: async () => schema, subscribe: none },
  };
  return { api };
}

const row = (u: string) => h.rows.get(u) as Opts;

beforeEach(() => {
  h.toast.mockReset();
  h.rows.clear();
});

describe("TreeView drag and drop", () => {
  it("registers every row for dragging", () => {
    setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    expect([...h.rows.keys()].sort()).toEqual(["E8", "T7", "T9"]);
  });

  it("marks the row a drop would be refused on, and explains why", async () => {
    setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    row("E8").onDragStart();
    row("T7").onOver("child", "E8");
    expect(await screen.findByText("ここには置けません")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("エピックはタスクの下に置けません");
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("（shared の schema.yaml の kinds）");
    row("T7").onOver(null, null);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText("ここには置けません")).toBeNull());
  });

  it("marks a place a drop would be accepted on, and says nothing", async () => {
    setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    row("T9").onOver("after", "T7");
    const el = screen.getByText("負荷試験").closest('[role="treeitem"]');
    await waitFor(() => expect(el).toHaveAttribute("data-drop", "after"));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("status")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("refuses a drop with a toast and does not change the data", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    row("T7").onDrop("E8", "child");
    await waitFor(() => expect(h.toast).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(h.toast.mock.calls[0]?.[0].message).toContain("エピックはタスクの下に置けません");
    expect(api.POST).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("makes a child on an accepted drop", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    api.POST.mockResolvedValue(ok({ task: task("T9", "task", "E8"), commit: "c1" }));
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    row("E8").onDrop("T9", "child");
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: "T9" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { parent: "E8" },
    });
  });

  it("reorders next to a sibling", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    api.POST.mockResolvedValue(ok({ task: task("T9", "task", "P"), commit: "c1" }));
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    row("E8").onDrop("T9", "before");
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(api.POST).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tasks/{id}/move", {
      params: { path: { id: "T9" }, query: { store: "shared" } },
      body: { parent: "P", before: "E8" },
    });
  });

  it("shows a toast when the server refuses the move", async () => {
    const { api } = setup();
    api.POST.mockResolvedValue({
      error: {
        type: "move.invalid",
        title: "Invalid",
        status: 422,
        detail: "x is not under the same parent",
      },
      response: new Response(null, { status: 422 }),
    });
    render(TreeView, { view: { kind: "project", ulid: "P" } });
    row("E8").onDrop("T9", "before");
    await waitFor(() => expect(h.toast).toHaveBeenCalled());
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 実装する**

`web/app/tree/dnd.ts`:

```ts
import { combine } from "@atlaskit/pragmatic-drag-and-drop/combine";
import {
  draggable,
  dropTargetForElements,
} from "@atlaskit/pragmatic-drag-and-drop/element/adapter";
import { autoScrollForElements } from "@atlaskit/pragmatic-drag-and-drop-auto-scroll/element";
import {
  attachInstruction,
  extractInstruction,
  type Instruction,
} from "@atlaskit/pragmatic-drag-and-drop-hitbox/tree-item";
import type { Attachment } from "svelte/attachments";
import type { TaskView } from "../../core/api/client";
import type { SchemaView } from "../../core/schema";
import { checkParent, type MoveArgs } from "./placement";

export type DropPlace = "before" | "after" | "child";

export type DropInput = {
  dragged: TaskView;
  target: TaskView;
  place: DropPlace;
  schema: SchemaView | undefined;
  taskOf: (ulid: string) => TaskView | undefined;
  // The ULIDs of the target's siblings as listed, the target included (and the
  // dragged task, when it is one of them).
  siblings: string[];
};

// silent: nothing to do and nothing to say (a drop on itself, or where the
// task already is).
export type DropDecision =
  | { ok: true; to: MoveArgs }
  | { ok: false; silent: boolean; title: string; reason: string };

const silent: DropDecision = { ok: false, silent: true, title: "", reason: "" };

function refuse(title: string, reason: string): DropDecision {
  return { ok: false, silent: false, title, reason };
}

function name(t: TaskView): string {
  return `「${t.id} ${t.title}」`;
}

// dropDecision says whether dragged may go to the place next to target, and
// the move to make when it may. It reads no DOM; the checks come in the order
// the reasons are worth giving in.
export function dropDecision(i: DropInput): DropDecision {
  const { dragged, target, place } = i;
  if (dragged.ulid === target.ulid) return silent;

  if (dragged.store !== target.store) {
    return refuse(
      place === "child"
        ? "別の Store のタスクの下には置けません"
        : "別の Store の並びには置けません",
      `${name(dragged)}は ${dragged.store}、${name(target)}は ${target.store} にあります。Store をまたぐ移動はドラッグではできません。`,
    );
  }
  if (dragged.workspace.ulid !== target.workspace.ulid) {
    return refuse(
      "別のワークスペースには置けません",
      `${name(dragged)}は ${dragged.workspace.name}、${name(target)}は ${target.workspace.name} にあります。ワークスペースをまたぐ移動はドラッグではできません（スプリントやリリースが外れるため）。`,
    );
  }

  const parentUlid = place === "child" ? target.ulid : target.parent;
  let up: string | null = parentUlid;
  for (let n = 0; up && n < 10000; n++) {
    if (up === dragged.ulid) {
      return refuse(
        "自分の子孫の下には置けません",
        `${name(dragged)}の下の階層に置くことになり、親子が循環します。`,
      );
    }
    up = i.taskOf(up)?.parent ?? null;
  }

  const parent = parentUlid ? i.taskOf(parentUlid) : null;
  if (parentUlid && !parent) {
    return refuse("置き先の親を読み込めていません", "少し待ってからやり直してください。");
  }
  if (!i.schema) {
    return refuse(
      "少し待ってからやり直してください",
      `${dragged.store} の schema.yaml を読み込んでいます。`,
    );
  }
  const rejection = checkParent(i.schema, dragged, parent ?? null);
  if (rejection) return refuse(rejection.title, rejection.reason);

  if (place !== "child" && dragged.parent === target.parent) {
    const from = i.siblings.indexOf(dragged.ulid);
    const to = i.siblings.indexOf(target.ulid);
    if (from >= 0 && to >= 0 && (place === "before" ? from + 1 === to : from - 1 === to)) {
      return silent;
    }
  }

  if (place === "child") return { ok: true, to: { parent: target.ulid } };
  const next = place === "before" ? { before: target.ulid } : { after: target.ulid };
  return {
    ok: true,
    to: target.parent ? { parent: target.parent, ...next } : { root: true, ...next },
  };
}

// placeOf is the place the hitbox instruction means; the other instructions
// (blocked, reparent) mean no drop.
export function placeOf(ins: Instruction | null): DropPlace | null {
  switch (ins?.type) {
    case "reorder-above":
      return "before";
    case "reorder-below":
      return "after";
    case "make-child":
      return "child";
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

const ROW = "tree-row";
// One level of indentation, passed to the hitbox, which does not use it in the
// standard mode.
const INDENT = 26;

export type RowDndOptions = {
  ulid: string;
  depth: number;
  onDragStart: () => void;
  onDragEnd: () => void;
  // The place a drop on this row would mean and the task being dragged; both
  // null when the pointer leaves.
  onOver: (place: DropPlace | null, sourceUlid: string | null) => void;
  onDrop: (sourceUlid: string, place: DropPlace) => void;
};

// rowDnd makes a row draggable and a drop target. Use it as {@attach rowDnd(o)}.
export function rowDnd(o: RowDndOptions): Attachment<HTMLElement> {
  return (el) =>
    combine(
      draggable({
        element: el,
        getInitialData: () => ({ type: ROW, ulid: o.ulid }),
        onDragStart: o.onDragStart,
        onDrop: o.onDragEnd,
      }),
      dropTargetForElements({
        element: el,
        canDrop: ({ source }) => source.data.type === ROW,
        getData: ({ input, element }) =>
          attachInstruction(
            { type: ROW, ulid: o.ulid },
            { input, element, currentLevel: o.depth, indentPerLevel: INDENT, mode: "standard" },
          ),
        onDrag: ({ self, source }) =>
          o.onOver(placeOf(extractInstruction(self.data)), String(source.data.ulid)),
        onDragLeave: () => o.onOver(null, null),
        onDrop: ({ self, source }) => {
          const place = placeOf(extractInstruction(self.data));
          o.onOver(null, null);
          if (place) o.onDrop(String(source.data.ulid), place);
        },
      }),
    );
}

// treeAutoScroll scrolls the list when a drag nears its edge.
export function treeAutoScroll(): Attachment<HTMLElement> {
  return (el) => autoScrollForElements({ element: el });
}
```

`web/app/tree/TreeRow.svelte` を次の全文に置き換える（Task 9 のものに `dnd`・`dragging`・`dropMark` を足したもの）:

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import type { Attachment } from "svelte/attachments";
  import type { LiveMark } from "../../core/cache";
  import { formatDate } from "../../core/format";
  import type { SchemaView } from "../../core/schema";
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";
  import KindIcon from "../icons/KindIcon.svelte";
  import StatusIcon from "../icons/StatusIcon.svelte";
  import Avatar from "../ui/Avatar.svelte";
  import IdLabel from "../ui/IdLabel.svelte";
  import type { FlatRow } from "./flatten";
  import { problemLabel, propText } from "./marks";

  type Props = {
    row: FlatRow;
    schema: SchemaView | undefined;
    selected: boolean;
    focusable: boolean; // tabindex 0: one row of the tree takes the Tab key
    live: LiveMark | undefined;
    blockedBy: string | undefined; // the ID of the first blocker, when the task is blocked
    onselect: () => void;
    ontoggle: () => void;
    dnd?: Attachment<HTMLElement>; // makes the row draggable and a drop target
    dragging?: boolean; // this row is being dragged
    dropMark?: "before" | "after" | "child" | "reject" | null; // what a drop on this row would do
  };
  let {
    row,
    schema,
    selected,
    focusable,
    live,
    blockedBy,
    onselect,
    ontoggle,
    dnd,
    dragging = false,
    dropMark = null,
  }: Props = $props();

  const task = $derived(row.task);
  const category = $derived(schema?.statuses?.find((s) => s.name === task.status)?.category);
  const role = $derived(schema?.kinds?.find((k) => k.name === task.kind)?.role);
  const closed = $derived(category === "done" || category === "canceled");
  const problem = $derived(task.problems?.[0]);
  const due = $derived(propText(task, "due"));
  const priority = $derived(propText(task, "priority"));
  const assignee = $derived(task.assignees?.[0]);
  const progress = $derived(task.progress && task.progress.total > 0 ? task.progress : undefined);
  const percent = $derived(progress ? Math.round((progress.done / progress.total) * 100) : 0);
</script>

<!-- svelte-ignore a11y_click_events_have_key_events -->
<!-- biome-ignore lint/a11y/useKeyWithClickEvents: the keys are handled by the tree that holds the rows -->
<div
  class="row"
  class:selected
  class:dragging
  data-drop={dropMark ?? undefined}
  role="treeitem"
  aria-level={row.depth + 1}
  aria-selected={selected}
  aria-expanded={row.hasChildren ? row.expanded : undefined}
  tabindex={focusable ? 0 : -1}
  data-ulid={row.ulid}
  onclick={onselect}
  {@attach dnd}
>
  <span class="lead" style:padding-left="{0.4 + row.depth * 1.6}rem">
    {#if row.hasChildren}
      <button
        type="button"
        class="chev"
        tabindex="-1"
        aria-label="{row.expanded ? "折りたたむ" : "展開する"}: {task.title}"
        aria-expanded={row.expanded}
        onclick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          ontoggle();
        }}
        ondblclick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <Icon name={row.expanded ? "chevron-down" : "chevron-right"} size={14} />
      </button>
    {:else}
      <span class="chev-gap"></span>
    {/if}
    <StatusIcon status={task.status} {category} />
    <KindIcon kind={task.kind} {role} />
    <IdLabel id={task.id} ulid={task.ulid} />
    <span class="title" class:closed class:container={task.kind === "epic"}>{task.title}</span>

    {#if progress}
      <span class="progress">
        <span class="bar"><span class="fill" style:width="{percent}%"></span></span>
        {progress.done}/{progress.total}
      </span>
    {:else if row.hasChildren && !row.expanded}
      <span class="progress">子 {row.childCount}</span>
    {/if}
    {#if task.checklist && task.checklist.total > 0}
      <span class="check" title="チェックリスト">
        <Icon name="checklist" size={10} />{task.checklist.done}/{task.checklist.total}
      </span>
    {/if}
    {#if live}
      <span class="live">{live.label}</span>
    {/if}
    {#if task.broken}
      <span class="warn" title={task.broken}> <Icon name="warning" size={11} />壊れています </span>
    {:else if problem}
      <span class="warn" title={problem.message}>
        <Icon name="warning" size={11} />{problemLabel(problem)}
      </span>
    {/if}
    {#if dropMark === "reject"}
      <span class="refuse"><Icon name="forbid" size={11} />ここには置けません</span>
    {/if}
  </span>

  <span class="tail">
    {#if blockedBy}
      <span class="blocked"><Icon name="forbid" size={12} />{blockedBy} 待ち</span>
    {/if}
    {#if priority}
      <span class="priority {priority}">{priority}</span>
    {/if}
    <span class="due">{due ? formatDate(due, new Date()) : ""}</span>
    <span class="who">
      {#if assignee}
        <Avatar name={assignee} />
      {/if}
    </span>
  </span>
</div>
```

`web/app/tree/TreeView.svelte` を次のとおり変える（どれも足すだけ。Task 9 で `biome check --write` が整形した後でも探せるよう、行そのものではなく名前で場所を指す）:

1. `import { type KeyAction, keyAction } from "./keyboard";` の次の行に足す:

```ts
  import { type DropDecision, type DropPlace, dropDecision, rowDnd, treeAutoScroll } from "./dnd";
```

2. 見出しコメント `// --- reading the screen` の直前に、次のブロックを足す:

```ts
  // --- drag and drop ---------------------------------------------------------------------
  let dragging = $state<string | null>(null);
  let over = $state<{ ulid: string; place: DropPlace; decision: DropDecision } | null>(null);

  function decide(sourceUlid: string, targetUlid: string, place: DropPlace): DropDecision | null {
    const dragged = taskOf(sourceUlid);
    const target = taskOf(targetUlid);
    if (!dragged || !target) return null;
    const siblings = rows.filter((r) => r.parent === target.parent).map((r) => r.ulid);
    return dropDecision({ dragged, target, place, schema, taskOf, siblings });
  }

  function setOver(targetUlid: string, place: DropPlace | null, sourceUlid: string | null) {
    if (!place || !sourceUlid) {
      if (over?.ulid === targetUlid) over = null;
      return;
    }
    if (over?.ulid === targetUlid && over.place === place) return;
    const decision = decide(sourceUlid, targetUlid, place);
    over = decision ? { ulid: targetUlid, place, decision } : null;
  }

  async function dropOn(sourceUlid: string, targetUlid: string, place: DropPlace) {
    over = null;
    dragging = null;
    const d = decide(sourceUlid, targetUlid, place);
    const t = taskOf(sourceUlid);
    if (!d || !t) return;
    if (!d.ok) {
      if (!d.silent) toast({ message: `${d.title}。${d.reason}` });
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (d.to.parent) toggle(d.to.parent, true);
      await moveTask(c, t, d.to);
    } catch (err) {
      toast({ message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  }

  function dropMarkOf(ulid: string): "before" | "after" | "child" | "reject" | null {
    if (over?.ulid !== ulid) return null;
    if (over.decision.ok) return over.place;
    return over.decision.silent ? null : "reject";
  }
  const refusal = $derived(over && !over.decision.ok && !over.decision.silent ? over.decision : null);
```

3. `{#snippet entry(item: Item)}` の中の `<TreeRow` に、`ontoggle={() => toggle(item.row.ulid, !item.row.expanded)}` の次の行として次の props を足す:

```svelte
          dragging={dragging === item.row.ulid}
          dropMark={dropMarkOf(item.row.ulid)}
          dnd={rowDnd({
            ulid: item.row.ulid,
            depth: item.row.depth,
            onDragStart: () => (dragging = item.row.ulid),
            onDragEnd: () => {
              dragging = null;
              over = null;
            },
            onOver: (place, source) => setOver(item.row.ulid, place, source),
            onDrop: (source, place) => void dropOn(source, item.row.ulid, place),
          })}
```

4. `<div class="scroller" role="tree" …>` の開始タグに、`{onkeydown}` の次の行として `{@attach treeAutoScroll()}` を足す。

5. `.scroller` の閉じ `</div>` と `<footer class="keys">` の間に足す:

```svelte
  {#if refusal && !refusal.ok}
    <aside class="refusal" role="status">
      <strong>{refusal.title}</strong>
      <p>{refusal.reason}</p>
    </aside>
  {/if}
```

### Task 15: 異常系・空の状態・Onboarding と CreateStore

States.dc.html と Onboarding.dc.html のうち 4d の範囲を作る。サーバーに接続できない（全画面）、トークンがない（細部 8）、帯（オフライン・push できない）、空の状態（タスクがない・フィルタに一致しない。クエリの誤りは Task 9 の `FilterBar` が欄の下に出す）、Onboarding（Store が1つもないとき）、CreateStore（`POST /stores`）。次は 4d では作らない: 認証切れの帯・履歴の書き換え・閲覧のみ・レート制限・上書きされた値・Store の外の参照（フェーズ3・5 の機能）、通知がない（4e）、Onboarding の「アカウント」「Store を選ぶ」の段（Store の発見とマウントは 3・4f）、「Issue から取り込む」（GitHub）。

CreateStore のアートボードは canvas にない。spec の「CreateStore: store init（名前・接頭辞・最初のワークスペース・remote）」と `api/openapi.yaml` の `StoreInitInputBody`（`name`・`prefix` が必須、`workspace`・`remotes`・`account` が任意）から作る。ダイアログの寸法・部品・足元の CLI は CreateProject.dc.html に合わせる。接頭辞の形は `internal/model/taskid.go` の `ValidIDPrefix`（英大文字と数字の 2〜10 文字、英字から始まる）と同じにする。`account` は入力欄を持たない（GitHub の remote でアカウントが決まらないときのサーバーのエラー `choice.required` は、`errorMessage` の文言を出す）。

**Files:**
- Create: `web/app/auth-state.svelte.ts`
- Create: `web/app/states/NoToken.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/states/Disconnected.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/states/Banners.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/states/Onboarding.svelte`
- Create: `web/app/create/CreateStore.svelte`
- Modify: `web/app/App.svelte`（ゲートと帯を足す。Task 7 が置いた目印に対する Edit）
- Test: `web/app/states/states.test.ts`、`web/app/create/CreateStore.test.ts`、`web/app/gates.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core()`（`conn`・`stores`・`api`）、`useSubscribe`、`router`・`hrefOf`、`toast`、`unwrap`、`errorMessage`、`cliStoreInit`、`summarizeSync`（Task 8 の `sync-summary.ts`）、`online`（Task 8）、`Dialog`・`Button`・`CliLine`・`Icon`（Task 7）、`renderWithCore`・`fakeCore`（Task 7 の `test-utils.ts`）。`StoreDirectory` は、起動時と `Connection` の `resync` で自分で読み込み、`loaded()` が真になる前は `stores()` が空でも Onboarding を出さないものとする
- Produces:
  - `app/auth-state.svelte.ts`: `auth: { readonly lost: boolean; markLost(): void }`（`createApi` の `onUnauthorized` から呼ぶ。401 `auth.token` でトークンの画面に切り替える）
  - `app/create/CreateStore.svelte`: props `open: boolean`、`onclose: () => void`
  - `app/states/Onboarding.svelte`・`Disconnected.svelte`（props `lastOpenAt: number | null`）・`NoToken.svelte`・`Banners.svelte`: `App.svelte` が出す

ゲートの出し方: `NoToken`（トークンがない、または 401）と `Onboarding`（Store が1つもない）は画面全体をおおう固定の面（`z-index` は Dialog の下）、`Disconnected` は読み込み済みの画面の上にかぶせる面（編集を止める。`z-index` は Dialog の上、トーストの下）。`Banners` は本文の上に積む帯。

```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { SyncStatus } from "../../core/connection";
import { renderWithCore } from "../test-utils";
import Banners from "./Banners.svelte";
import Disconnected from "./Disconnected.svelte";
import NoToken from "./NoToken.svelte";
import Onboarding from "./Onboarding.svelte";

beforeEach(() => {
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", {
    value: { writeText: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) },
    configurable: true,
  });
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("NoToken", () => {
  it("hh ui の URL からだけ使えると伝え、hh ui をコピーできる", () => {
    const { getByText, getByRole } = renderWithCore(NoToken, {});
    expect(
      getByText(
        "この画面は hh ui が開いた URL からだけ使えます。端末で hh ui を実行してください。",
      ),
    ).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByRole("button", { name: /hh ui/ })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("Disconnected", () => {
  it("最後に接続していた時刻と、5 秒ごとに再接続していることを出す", () => {
    const at = new Date(2026, 10, 2, 11, 42).getTime();
    const { getByRole, getByText } = renderWithCore(Disconnected, { lastOpenAt: at });
    expect(getByRole("alert")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("Hedgehug のサーバーに接続できません")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText(/表示中の内容は 11:42 時点のもので、編集はできません/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("5 秒ごとに再接続しています")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("一度も接続できていなければ時刻を出さない", () => {
    const { queryByText, getByText } = renderWithCore(Disconnected, { lastOpenAt: null });
    expect(queryByText(/時点のもの/)).toBeNull();
    expect(getByText(/端末で hh ui を実行してください/)).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("Banners", () => {
  const status = (over: Partial<SyncStatus> & { store: string }): SyncStatus => ({
    remotes: ["origin"],
    ...over,
  });
  const mount = (statuses: SyncStatus[]) =>
    renderWithCore(
      Banners,
      {},
      {
        conn: {
          state: "open",
          lastOpenAt: null,
          sync: new Map(statuses.map((s) => [s.store, s])),
          unread: 0,
          subscribe: () => () => {},
          on: () => () => {},
          start() {},
          stop() {},
        },
      },
    );

  it("同期済みなら何も出さない", () => {
    const { container } = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 0 })]);
    expect(container.querySelector("[role='status'], [role='alert']")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("オフラインの帯", () => {
    vi.spyOn(navigator, "onLine", "get").mockReturnValue(false);
    const { getByText } = mount([status({ store: "a", unpushed: 5 })]);
    expect(
      getByText("オフラインです。変更はこのマシンにコミットし、つながったら push します。"),
    ).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("push できない Store ごとに帯を出し、サーバーの文言を添える", () => {
    const { getAllByRole } = mount([
      status({ store: "org-a/hh-shared", unpushed: 1, last_error: "push rejected" }),
      status({ store: "me", unpushed: 0 }),
    ]);
    const bands = getAllByRole("alert");
    expect(bands).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(bands[0]?.textContent).toContain("org-a/hh-shared を push できません");
    expect(bands[0]?.textContent).toContain("push rejected");
    expect(bands[0]?.textContent).toContain("変更はこのマシンに残っています");
  });
});

describe("Onboarding", () => {
  it("ようこそと、Store を作るボタンを出し、押すと CreateStore が開く", async () => {
    const { getByText, getByRole, queryByRole } = renderWithCore(Onboarding, {});
    expect(getByText("Hedgehug へようこそ")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByRole("button", { name: /hh store init/ })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }));
    expect(getByRole("dialog", { name: "Store を作成" })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`web/app/create/CreateStore.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { renderWithCore } from "../test-utils";
import { resetToasts, toasts } from "../toast.svelte";
import CreateStore from "./CreateStore.svelte";

const created = {
  name: "me",
  path: "/home/u/.local/share/hedgehug/stores/me",
  workspace: {
    ulid: "01HWSME",
    name: "me",
    id_prefix: "ME",
    prefix_set: "2026-11-02T00:00:00Z",
    tasks: 0,
  },
  remotes: [],
  pushed: [],
};

function mount(post = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ data: created, response: new Response() })) {
  const reload = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
  const onclose = vi.fn();
  const r = renderWithCore(
    CreateStore,
    { open: true, onclose },
    { api: { POST: post }, stores: { reload, subscribe: () => () => {} } },
  );
  return { post, reload, onclose, ...r };
}

const type = (el: HTMLElement, value: string) => fireEvent.input(el, { target: { value } });

beforeEach(() => {
  history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
  resetToasts();
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", {
    value: { writeText: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) },
    configurable: true,
  });
});
afterEach(cleanup);

describe("CreateStore", () => {
  it("名前と接頭辞が空なら送らず、理由を出す", async () => {
    const { getByRole, getByText, post } = mount();
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }));
    expect(post).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(getByText("名前を入れてください")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getByText("接頭辞は英大文字と数字の 2〜10 文字で、英字から始めてください")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("接頭辞は大文字にそろえ、形が違えば送らない", async () => {
    const { getByLabelText, getByRole, post } = mount();
    const prefix = getByLabelText("ID の接頭辞") as HTMLInputElement;
    await type(prefix, "me");
    expect(prefix.value).toBe("ME");
    await type(getByLabelText("名前"), "me");
    await type(prefix, "1A");
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }));
    expect(post).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("作成: POST /api/stores、reload、トースト、最初のワークスペースのツリーへ", async () => {
    const { getByLabelText, getByRole, post, reload, onclose } = mount();
    await type(getByLabelText("名前"), " me ");
    await type(getByLabelText("ID の接頭辞"), "me");
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onclose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(post).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/stores", {
      body: { name: "me", prefix: "ME", workspace: undefined, remotes: undefined },
    });
    expect(reload).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(toasts.list.map((t) => t.message)).toEqual(["Store「me」を作りました"]);
    expect(location.pathname).toBe("/workspaces/me/01HWSME");
  });

  it("最初のワークスペースと remote を渡す", async () => {
    const { getByLabelText, getByRole, post } = mount();
    await type(getByLabelText("名前"), "team");
    await type(getByLabelText("ID の接頭辞"), "TM");
    await type(getByLabelText("最初のワークスペース（任意）"), "backend");
    await type(getByLabelText("remote（任意）"), "git@github.com:org/hh-team.git");
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(post).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(post).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/stores", {
      body: {
        name: "team",
        prefix: "TM",
        workspace: "backend",
        remotes: ["git@github.com:org/hh-team.git"],
      },
    });
  });

  it("Enter（入力欄の中）でも作成する", async () => {
    const { getByLabelText, post } = mount();
    await type(getByLabelText("名前"), "me");
    const prefix = getByLabelText("ID の接頭辞");
    await type(prefix, "me");
    await fireEvent.keyDown(prefix, { key: "Enter" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(post).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
  });

  it("失敗したらエラーの文言を出し、ダイアログは閉じない", async () => {
    const post = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("boom"));
    const { getByLabelText, getByRole, onclose } = mount(post);
    await type(getByLabelText("名前"), "me");
    await type(getByLabelText("ID の接頭辞"), "ME");
    await fireEvent.click(getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }));
    const alert = await vi.waitFor(() => getByRole("alert"));
    expect(alert.textContent).toContain("操作に失敗しました: boom");
    expect(onclose).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect((getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });

  it("足元に同じ操作の hh コマンドを出す", async () => {
    const { getByLabelText, getByRole } = mount();
    await type(getByLabelText("名前"), "me");
    await type(getByLabelText("ID の接頭辞"), "me");
    expect(getByRole("button", { name: /hh store init me/ }).textContent).toContain("ME");
  });
});
```

`web/app/gates.test.ts`:
```ts
import { cleanup, render } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import { flushSync } from "svelte";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import App from "./App.svelte";

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", () => new Promise(() => {}));
  sessionStorage.clear();
  history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe("App のゲート", () => {
  it("トークンがなければトークンの画面を出す", () => {
    const { getByText } = render(App);
    flushSync();
    expect(
      getByText(
        "この画面は hh ui が開いた URL からだけ使えます。端末で hh ui を実行してください。",
      ),
    ).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("トークンがあればトークンの画面は出さない", () => {
    history.replaceState(null, "", "/#token=tkn");
    const { queryByText } = render(App);
    flushSync();
    expect(queryByText(/hh ui が開いた URL からだけ/)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import Icon from "../icons/Icon.svelte";

  let { lastOpenAt }: { lastOpenAt: number | null } = $props();

  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  const clock = $derived.by(() => {
    if (lastOpenAt === null) return null;
    const d = new Date(lastOpenAt);
    return `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
  });
</script>
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { core } from "../context";

  const statuses = useSubscribe(conn, () => conn.sync);

</script>

{#if summary.kind === "offline" || summary.kind === "error"}

{/if}
```

`web/app/create/CreateStore.svelte`:
```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { unwrap } from "../../core/api/client";
  import { cliStoreInit } from "../../core/cli";
  import { errorMessage } from "../../core/messages";
  import { core } from "../context";
  import { hrefOf, router } from "../router";
  import { toast } from "../toast.svelte";
  import Button from "../ui/Button.svelte";
  import Dialog from "../ui/Dialog.svelte";

  type Props = { open: boolean; onclose: () => void };
  let { open, onclose }: Props = $props();

  const { api, stores } = core();
  const uid = $props.id();

  // The same rule as ValidIDPrefix in internal/model: 2 to 10 upper-case letters and digits, starting with a letter.
  const PREFIX = /^[A-Z][A-Z0-9]{1,9}$/;

  let name = $state("");
  let prefix = $state("");
  let workspace = $state("");
  let remote = $state("");
  let touched = $state(false);
  let busy = $state(false);
  let failure = $state<string | null>(null);

  const nameError = $derived(name.trim() === "" ? "名前を入れてください" : null);
  const prefixError = $derived(
    PREFIX.test(prefix) ? null : "接頭辞は英大文字と数字の 2〜10 文字で、英字から始めてください",
  );
  const remotes = $derived(remote.trim() ? [remote.trim()] : undefined);
  const cli = $derived(
    cliStoreInit({
      name: name.trim() || "NAME",
      prefix: prefix || "PREFIX",
      workspace: workspace.trim() || undefined,
      remotes,
    }),
  );

  function reset() {
    name = "";
    prefix = "";
    workspace = "";
    remote = "";
    touched = false;
    failure = null;
  }

  async function submit() {
    touched = true;
    if (busy || nameError || prefixError) return;
    busy = true;
    failure = null;
    try {
      const r = await unwrap(
        api.POST("/api/stores", {
          body: { name: name.trim(), prefix, workspace: workspace.trim() || undefined, remotes },
        }),
      );
      await stores.reload();
      toast({ message: `Store「${r.name}」を作りました` });
      reset();
      onclose();
      router.go(hrefOf({ name: "workspace", store: r.name, ulid: r.workspace.ulid }));
    } catch (e) {
      failure = errorMessage(e);
    } finally {
      busy = false;
    }
  }

  function onkeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && (e.target as HTMLElement).tagName === "INPUT") {
      e.preventDefault();
      void submit();
    }
  }
</script>

<Dialog {open} title="Store を作成" width={620} {cli} {onclose}>
  <div class="form" role="presentation" {onkeydown}>
    <div class="field">
      <label for="{uid}-name">名前</label>
      <input
        id="{uid}-name"
        type="text"
        value={name}
        oninput={(e) => (name = e.currentTarget.value)}
        aria-invalid={touched && nameError ? "true" : undefined}
        aria-describedby="{uid}-name-msg"
      >
      <span id="{uid}-name-msg" class="msg" class:bad={touched && nameError}>
        {touched && nameError ? nameError : "この Store を呼ぶ名前です"}
      </span>
    </div>

    <div class="field">
      <label for="{uid}-prefix">ID の接頭辞</label>
      <input
        id="{uid}-prefix"
        class="mono"
        type="text"
        value={prefix}
        oninput={(e) => (prefix = e.currentTarget.value.toUpperCase())}
        aria-invalid={touched && prefixError ? "true" : undefined}
        aria-describedby="{uid}-prefix-msg"
      >
      <span id="{uid}-prefix-msg" class="msg" class:bad={touched && prefixError}>
        {touched && prefixError
          ? prefixError
          : "タスクの ID が ME-1 のようになります。最初のワークスペースの接頭辞です"}
      </span>
    </div>

    <div class="field">
      <label for="{uid}-ws">最初のワークスペース（任意）</label>
      <input
        id="{uid}-ws"
        type="text"
        value={workspace}
        placeholder={name.trim()}
        oninput={(e) => (workspace = e.currentTarget.value)}
      >
      <span class="msg">空なら Store と同じ名前にします</span>
    </div>

    <div class="field">
      <label for="{uid}-remote">remote（任意）</label>
      <input
        id="{uid}-remote"
        class="mono"
        type="text"
        value={remote}
        placeholder="git@github.com:org/hh-tasks.git"
        oninput={(e) => (remote = e.currentTarget.value)}
      >
      <span class="msg">あれば最初の push までします。なくてもこのマシンだけで使えます</span>
    </div>

    {#if failure}
      <p class="failure" role="alert">{failure}</p>
    {/if}
  </div>

  {#snippet footer()}
    <Button onclick={onclose}>キャンセル</Button>
    <Button variant="primary" disabled={busy} onclick={submit}>
      {busy ? "作成中…" : "Store を作る"}
    </Button>
  {/snippet}
</Dialog>

<style>
  .form {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-16);
  }
  .field {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-6);
  }
  label {
    color: var(--ink-2);
    font-size: var(--fs-12);
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  input {
    min-height: 40px;
    padding: 0 var(--space-12);
    border: 1px solid var(--line-input);
    border-radius: var(--radius-8);
    font-size: var(--fs-14);
  }
  input[aria-invalid="true"] {
    border: 1.5px solid var(--danger);
  }
  .mono {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: var(--fs-12);
  }
  .msg {
    color: var(--muted);
    font-size: var(--fs-12);
    line-height: 1.55;
  }
  .bad {
    color: var(--danger);
  }
  .failure {
    margin: 0;
    padding: var(--space-10) var(--space-12);
    border-radius: var(--radius-10);
    background: var(--danger-weak);
    color: var(--danger-ink);
    line-height: 1.6;
  }
</style>
```

`web/app/App.svelte` の Modify（Task 7 が置いた行に対する Edit。Task 8〜14 の Edit で足された行は動かさない）:

1. `import Toasts from "./ui/Toasts.svelte";` を次に置き換える（この行を残したまま、前に import を足す）:
```ts
  import { auth } from "./auth-state.svelte";
  import Banners from "./states/Banners.svelte";
  import Disconnected from "./states/Disconnected.svelte";
  import NoToken from "./states/NoToken.svelte";
  import Onboarding from "./states/Onboarding.svelte";
  import { useSubscribe } from "./svelte-store";
  import Toasts from "./ui/Toasts.svelte";
```
2. `const c = createCore({ token: () => token });` を次に置き換える:
```ts
  const c = createCore({ token: () => token, onUnauthorized: () => auth.markLost() });
```
3. `setCore(c);` を次に置き換える:
```ts
  setCore(c);

  const conn = useSubscribe(c.conn, () => ({ state: c.conn.state, lastOpenAt: c.conn.lastOpenAt }));
  const dir = useSubscribe(c.stores, () => {
    const first = c.stores.workspaces()[0];
    return {
      loaded: c.stores.loaded(),
      count: c.stores.stores().length,
      first: first ? { store: first.store, ulid: first.ulid } : null,
    };
  });
  const noToken = $derived(token === null || auth.lost);
  const onboarding = $derived(dir.current.loaded && dir.current.count === 0);
```
4. `c.conn.start();` を次に置き換える（トークンがなければ接続しない）:
```ts
    if (token) c.conn.start();
```
5. `<!-- banners -->` を `<Banners />` に置き換える。
6. `<Toasts />` を次に置き換える（ダイアログ類の後・トーストの前に、ゲートの面を出す）:
```svelte
{#if noToken}
  <NoToken />
{:else if onboarding}
  <Onboarding />
{/if}
{#if !noToken && conn.current.state === "down"}
  <Disconnected lastOpenAt={conn.current.lastOpenAt} />
{/if}
<Toasts />
```

7. ホーム（`/`）は最初のワークスペースのツリーを出す（細部 17）。Task 9 が足した `{#if router.route.name === "workspace"}` の分岐の末尾（`{/if}` の前）に、次の分岐を足す。ストアの一覧が読めるまでは何も出さない:
```svelte
      {:else if router.route.name === "home" && dir.current.first}
        <!-- "/" is the tree of the first workspace -->
        {#key `${dir.current.first.store}/${dir.current.first.ulid}`}
          <TreeView view={{ kind: "workspace", ...dir.current.first }} />
        {/key}
```

`Empty.svelte`（空の状態）と、`TreeView` への組み込み（空の状態と「フィルタを解除」）は、Task 9 が置く。ここでは `TreeView.svelte` も `Empty.svelte` も変えない。
