### Task 16a: E2E（ハーネスと、4d-2 の画面だけで通る spec）

細部 32 のとおり。Playwright（chromium だけ）。テストごとに `hh ui` を新しい `HEDGEHUG_HOME` で起動し、準備のデータは CLI（`hh add` など）と、300 件のときだけ API の `POST`（`hh` を 300 回起動するより速い）で作る。タスクの詳細・作成ダイアログ・コマンドパレットはまだないので、この回の spec はそれらを使わない（Task 16b が足す）。4d-2 の実物（`make web`・`make build`）の上で実際に `make e2e` を通した（17 テスト）。

**画面の文言は、Task 7〜10・15 の plan の文言と role に合わせてある。** 各 spec の冒頭に「前提にする文言・role」を書き、全体で共通のものは `screens.ts` の冒頭に書いた。文言や role が変わったら、この一覧とその画面を一緒に直す（spec を通すために画面を壊さない）。

**この E2E が見つけて、Task 2・7・15 に直しを入れたもの**（単体テストは jsdom で、実ブラウザのレイアウトと実際のタイミングを持たないため）:
- Task 2 の `vite.config.ts`: CodeMirror の分け方で循環が起き、実行時に画面が真っ白になった（Task 2 の注）
- Task 7 の `App.svelte`: `.app` が `min-height: 100vh` だと `.tree { height: 100% }` が効かず、ツリーのスクロールの箱が内容の高さに伸びて、300 行が全部描かれた（仮想スクロールが効かない）。`.app` を `height: 100vh`、`.main` を `overflow: auto` にした
- Task 15（`App.svelte`）: ホーム（`/`）が何も出していなかった。最初のワークスペースのツリーを出す

**Files:**
- Create: `web/e2e/harness.ts`、`web/e2e/screens.ts`、`web/e2e/tree.spec.ts`、`web/e2e/live.spec.ts`、`web/e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`、`web/e2e/newtab.spec.ts`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci-frontend.yaml`（`e2e` ジョブ）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 の `playwright.config.ts`・`make e2e`・`make build`（`bin/hedgehug`）。Task 4 の `Connection`: ページが `hello` を受けたあとに `PUT /api/presence` を送る（細部 12）。`harness.ts` の `open` はこのリクエストを待ち、イベントの流れが開いたと見なす。Task 7〜10・15 の画面（各 spec の冒頭の一覧）
- Produces: `e2e/harness.ts` の `test`（fixture `hh`・`store`）、`expect`、`startHedgehug`、`Hedgehug`（`hh(...args)`・`json<T>(...args)`・`add(title, ...args)`・`show(id)`・`post(path, body)`・`open(page, path)`・`origin`・`token`・`home`）、`TaskJson`、`StoreSpec`。`e2e/screens.ts` の `row`・`toast`・`dragRow`・`treeReady`。Task 16b と、4e が足す spec（カンバン・通知・ビュー・undo）も同じ `harness.ts` を使う

- [ ] **Step 1: ハーネスと画面の取り方を書く**

`HEDGEHUG_HOME` と `HOME`・`GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL` を一時ディレクトリにする（コミットの author は git の global config から取るので、CI のように設定がない環境でも動くよう、ハーネスが書く）。`HEDGEHUG_AUTO_SYNC=0` で CLI が背景の同期を起こさないようにする。cwd も一時ディレクトリにして、リポジトリの中で `hh add` したときの「いまのプロジェクト」の推測が入らないようにする。

`web/e2e/harness.ts`:
```ts
import { type ChildProcess, execFileSync, spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { test as base, expect, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

// The binary under test. `make e2e` builds it into bin/hedgehug; HEDGEHUG_BIN
// points at another one.
const BIN = process.env.HEDGEHUG_BIN ?? resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../../bin/hedgehug");

export type StoreSpec = { name: string; prefix: string; workspace?: string };

/** The store every test starts with, unless it says store: null. */
export const DEFAULT_STORE: StoreSpec = { name: "demo", prefix: "DM" };

export type TaskJson = {
  ulid: string;
  id: string;
  title: string;
  kind: string;
  status: string;
  parent: string | null;
  rank: string;
  labels: string[];
  props: Record<string, unknown>;
  deleted_at: string | null;
  body?: string;
  history?: unknown[];
};

/**
 * One hh ui process on its own HEDGEHUG_HOME, and the CLI against the same
 * home, so that a test can change the store the way a terminal would.
 */
export class Hedgehug {
  constructor(
    readonly home: string,
    /** http://127.0.0.1:<port> */
    readonly origin: string,
    readonly token: string,
    private readonly proc: ChildProcess,
    readonly stderr: () => string,
  ) {}

  /** The environment of every hh process of this test. */
  static env(home: string): NodeJS.ProcessEnv {
    return {
      ...process.env,
      HEDGEHUG_HOME: join(home, "hh"),
      HEDGEHUG_AUTO_SYNC: "0",
      // The author of the commits comes from the global git config.
      HOME: join(home, "user"),
      GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: join(home, "user", ".gitconfig"),
    };
  }

  /** Runs hh and returns its standard output. A non-zero exit throws. */
  hh(...args: string[]): string {
    return execFileSync(BIN, args, {
      cwd: this.home, // not inside a repository, so hh add does not pick a project from it
      env: Hedgehug.env(this.home),
      encoding: "utf8",
    });
  }

  /** Runs hh with --json and parses the output. */
  json<T>(...args: string[]): T {
    return JSON.parse(this.hh(...args, "--json")) as T;
  }

  add(title: string, ...args: string[]): TaskJson {
    return this.json<TaskJson>("add", title, ...args);
  }

  show(id: string): TaskJson {
    return this.json<TaskJson>("show", id);
  }

  /**
   * Calls the API of this server, as the page does. For a test that needs many tasks:
   * one request is much quicker than one hh process.
   */
  async post<T>(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<T> {
    const r = await fetch(`${this.origin}${path}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`POST ${path}: ${r.status} ${await r.text()}`);
    return (await r.json()) as T;
  }

  /** Opens the page the way hh ui's URL does, and waits until the page is live. */
  async open(page: Page, path = "/"): Promise<void> {
    // The page sends PUT /api/presence once the hello of the event stream
    // arrived. After that a change made by the CLI reaches the page as an event.
    const live = page.waitForRequest(
      (r) => r.method() === "PUT" && new URL(r.url()).pathname === "/api/presence",
    );
    await page.goto(`${this.origin}${path}#token=${this.token}`);
    await live;
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.proc.exitCode === null) {
      const exited = new Promise<void>((done) => this.proc.once("exit", () => done()));
      this.proc.kill("SIGINT");
      const timer = setTimeout(() => this.proc.kill("SIGKILL"), 5000);
      await exited;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
    rmSync(this.home, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

/** Starts hh ui on a new home with the store, or with no store when it is null. */
export async function startHedgehug(store: StoreSpec | null): Promise<Hedgehug> {
  const home = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "hh-e2e-"));
  mkdirSync(join(home, "user"));
  writeFileSync(
    join(home, "user", ".gitconfig"),
    "[user]\n\tname = E2E\n\temail = e2e@example.com\n",
  );
  const env = Hedgehug.env(home);
  if (store) {
    const args = ["store", "init", store.name, "--prefix", store.prefix];
    if (store.workspace) args.push("--workspace", store.workspace);
    execFileSync(BIN, args, { cwd: home, env, encoding: "utf8" });
  }

  const proc = spawn(BIN, ["ui", "--no-open"], { cwd: home, env });
  let out = "";
  let err = "";
  proc.stderr.on("data", (c: Buffer) => {
    err += c.toString();
  });
  const url = await new Promise<string>((resolveURL, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`hh ui printed no URL in 15s\n${err}`)), 15000);
    proc.once("exit", (code) => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      reject(new Error(`hh ui exited with ${code} before it printed its URL\n${err}`));
    });
    proc.stdout.on("data", (c: Buffer) => {
      out += c.toString();
      const m = /^Hedgehug UI: (\S+)$/m.exec(out);
      if (m?.[1]) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        resolveURL(m[1]);
      }
    });
  }).catch(async (e) => {
    proc.kill("SIGKILL");
    rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
    throw e;
  });

  const u = new URL(url);
  const token = new URLSearchParams(u.hash.slice(1)).get("token");
  if (!token) throw new Error(`no token in ${url}`);
  return new Hedgehug(home, u.origin, token, proc, () => err);
}

type Fixtures = {
  /** The store hh starts with; null starts hh with no store at all. */
  store: StoreSpec | null;
  hh: Hedgehug;
};

export const test = base.extend<Fixtures>({
  store: [DEFAULT_STORE, { option: true }],
  hh: async ({ store }, use) => {
    const h = await startHedgehug(store);
    try {
      await use(h);
    } finally {
      await h.stop();
    }
  },
});

export { expect };
```

`web/e2e/screens.ts`（`toast`: 保存の状態や帯も `role="status"` なので、トーストは文言で探す）:
```ts
import { expect, type Locator, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

// The words and roles the specs find the screens by. They are the ones the screens of
// Tasks 7 to 15 have (the tests of those tasks check the same words); when a screen
// changes a word, change it here and in the spec together.
//
//   Tree        the list is role "tree" named "<workspace or project> のタスク"; a row is a
//               flat element with role "treeitem" (data-ulid, aria-level, aria-expanded,
//               aria-selected) that shows its ID and title; a row changed by the CLI shows
//               "CLI で更新 · 今"; the whole row is the drag handle; the status icon of a
//               row is an svg with data-shape ("check" is done, "ring" is todo)
//   Add row     Enter on a selected row opens the textbox "新しいタスクのタイトル" below it
//   Dialogs     role "dialog", named by the title; the footer has the buttons
//   Toasts      one role "status" holds all the toasts: each has its message, and a link
//               ("開く") or the button "取り消し"; other regions with role "status" exist
//               (the save state, the bands of a task), so find a toast by its text

/** The row of the tree whose text has the title. Titles in the specs are unique. */
export function row(page: Page, title: string): Locator {
  return page.getByRole("treeitem").filter({ hasText: title });
}

/**
 * Waits until the tree has read the schema of the store: the add row then names the kind it
 * makes. A drop before that is refused ("少し待ってからやり直してください"); the keys that depend
 * on the schema (Space, Tab, S, K) wait for it, so they need no such wait.
 */
export async function treeReady(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: /種類: \S/ })).toBeVisible();
}

/** The toasts that have the text. */
export function toast(page: Page, text: string | RegExp): Locator {
  return page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: text });
}

/**
 * Drags the row `from` onto the row `to`: onto its upper quarter ("before"),
 * its lower quarter ("after") or its middle ("child"). `hold` runs while the
 * pointer is over the target and the button is still down.
 */
export async function dragRow(
  page: Page,
  from: Locator,
  to: Locator,
  where: "before" | "after" | "child",
  hold?: () => Promise<void>,
): Promise<void> {
  const a = await from.boundingBox();
  const b = await to.boundingBox();
  if (!a || !b) throw new Error("both rows must be visible to drag");
  const at = { before: 0.2, child: 0.5, after: 0.8 }[where];
  const x = b.x + b.width / 2;
  const y = b.y + b.height * at;
  await page.mouse.move(a.x + a.width / 2, a.y + a.height / 2);
  await page.mouse.down();
  // A first small move starts the drag; the rest passes over the target.
  await page.mouse.move(a.x + a.width / 2, a.y + a.height / 2 + 8, { steps: 2 });
  await page.mouse.move(x, y, { steps: 12 });
  await hold?.();
  await page.mouse.up();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: ツリーの操作・ドラッグ・フィルタ・300 件の spec**

`dragRow` は Playwright のマウスでドラッグを起こす（Chromium では HTML5 のドラッグ＆ドロップとして扱われ、Pragmatic drag and drop が拾う）。300 件のテストは、画面に出る行が 100 未満であることと、ホイールで末尾までスクロールすると最後の行が出て最初の行が消えることを確かめる（仮想スクロール）。

`web/e2e/tree.spec.ts`:
```ts
// Screens this spec relies on (see screens.ts for the shared list):
//   - "/" is the tree of the first workspace; the filter box "フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）"
//     starts as "-status:canceled" and the foot of the tree shows the `hh ls` of the filter
//   - a click on a row selects and focuses it; the keys below act on the focused row
//       Enter       opens the textbox "新しいタスクのタイトル" below the row; Enter in it adds
//                   a sibling after the row (an add, then a move: two commits)
//       Tab/Shift+Tab   indent under the previous sibling / outdent after the parent
//       Alt+Up      move before the previous sibling
//       Space       toggles between the done and the todo default statuses
//   - a drag over a row's upper quarter puts the task before it, the lower quarter after it,
//     the middle makes it a child; a target the schema does not allow shows the chip
//     "ここには置けません" on the row and the status with the reason under the tree, and a
//     drop there shows a toast with the reason and changes nothing
//   - a tree of more than 200 rows is drawn as a window of rows (virtual scrolling)
import { expect, type Hedgehug, test } from "./harness";
import { dragRow, row, toast, treeReady } from "./screens";

/** The titles of the tasks at the root, in the order of their ranks. */
function rootOrder(hh: Hedgehug): string[] {
  return hh
    .json<{ title: string; rank: string; parent: string | null }[]>("ls")
    .filter((t) => t.parent === null)
    .sort((x, y) => (x.rank < y.rank ? -1 : 1))
    .map((t) => t.title);
}

test("Enter adds a sibling right below the selected row", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  hh.add("Alpha");
  hh.add("Bravo");
  await hh.open(page);

  await row(page, "Alpha").click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "新しいタスクのタイトル" }).fill("Alpha two");
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(row(page, "Alpha two")).toBeVisible();

  await expect.poll(() => rootOrder(hh)).toEqual(["Alpha", "Alpha two", "Bravo"]);
});

test("Tab indents under the previous sibling and Shift+Tab brings it back", async ({
  page,
  hh,
}) => {
  const a = hh.add("Alpha");
  const b = hh.add("Bravo");
  await hh.open(page);

  await row(page, "Bravo").click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
  await expect.poll(() => hh.show(b.id).parent).toBe(a.ulid);
  await expect(row(page, "Bravo")).toHaveAttribute("aria-level", "2");

  await page.keyboard.press("Shift+Tab");
  await expect.poll(() => hh.show(b.id).parent).toBeNull();
  await expect(row(page, "Bravo")).toHaveAttribute("aria-level", "1");
});

test("Alt+Up moves a task before its previous sibling", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  hh.add("Alpha");
  hh.add("Bravo");
  hh.add("Charlie");
  await hh.open(page);

  await row(page, "Charlie").click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Alt+ArrowUp");
  await expect.poll(() => rootOrder(hh)).toEqual(["Alpha", "Charlie", "Bravo"]);
});

test("Space toggles done and back", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  const t = hh.add("Finish me");
  await hh.open(page);

  await row(page, "Finish me").click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Space");
  await expect.poll(() => hh.show(t.id).status).toBe("done");
  // The next press reads the status the row shows: wait until the row shows done.
  await expect(row(page, "Finish me").locator('svg[data-shape="check"]')).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press("Space");
  await expect.poll(() => hh.show(t.id).status).toBe("todo");
});

test("dragging a row after another reorders; onto the middle makes it a child", async ({
  page,
  hh,
}) => {
  hh.add("Alpha");
  const b = hh.add("Bravo");
  const c = hh.add("Charlie");
  await hh.open(page);
  await treeReady(page);

  await dragRow(page, row(page, "Alpha"), row(page, "Charlie"), "after");
  await expect.poll(() => rootOrder(hh)).toEqual(["Bravo", "Charlie", "Alpha"]);
  // Wait for the page to show the new order before the next drag measures the rows.
  await expect(page.getByRole("treeitem").last()).toContainText("Alpha");

  await dragRow(page, row(page, "Bravo"), row(page, "Charlie"), "child");
  await expect.poll(() => hh.show(b.id).parent).toBe(c.ulid);
  await expect(row(page, "Bravo")).toHaveAttribute("aria-level", "2");
});

test("a drop the schema does not allow says why and changes nothing", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  // The default schema lets an epic sit at the root or under a project only.
  const epic = hh.add("Big epic", "--kind", "epic");
  hh.add("Small task");
  await hh.open(page);
  await treeReady(page);

  await dragRow(page, row(page, "Big epic"), row(page, "Small task"), "child", async () => {
    await expect(page.getByText("ここには置けません")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("エピックはタスクの下に置けません")).toBeVisible();
  });
  await expect(toast(page, "エピックを置ける親はルートとプロジェクトだけです")).toBeVisible();
  expect(hh.show(epic.id).parent).toBeNull();
});

test("the filter box narrows the tree and shows the command", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  hh.add("Keep this");
  hh.add("Hide this");
  const canceled = hh.add("Dropped");
  hh.hh("set", canceled.id, "status=canceled");
  await hh.open(page);

  await expect(row(page, "Keep this")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(row(page, "Dropped")).toHaveCount(0); // canceled tasks are out by default
  await expect(page.getByText("中止 1 件を除外")).toBeVisible();

  const filter = page.getByLabel("フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）");
  await filter.fill("keep");
  await expect(row(page, "Hide this")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(row(page, "Keep this")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/hh ls .*keep/)).toBeVisible();
});

test("a tree of 300 tasks is drawn as a window of rows and scrolls to the end", async ({
  page,
  hh,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(120_000);
  for (let i = 1; i <= 300; i++) {
    await hh.post("/api/tasks", { title: `Task ${String(i).padStart(3, "0")}` });
  }
  await hh.open(page);

  const rows = page.getByRole("treeitem");
  await expect(row(page, "Task 001")).toBeVisible();
  // Only the rows near the window are in the page.
  expect(await rows.count()).toBeLessThan(100);
  await expect(row(page, "Task 300")).toHaveCount(0);

  // Scroll to the end: the last row is drawn then, and the rows far from it are not.
  await row(page, "Task 001").hover();
  await page.mouse.wheel(0, 1_000_000);
  await expect(row(page, "Task 300")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(row(page, "Task 001")).toHaveCount(0);
  expect(await rows.count()).toBeLessThan(100);
});
```

Run: `make build && cd web && pnpm exec playwright install chromium && pnpm e2e e2e/tree.spec.ts`
Expected: PASS（8 tests）。300 件のテストは 15 秒ほどかかる。落ちたら、`pnpm exec playwright show-trace test-results/<テスト名>/trace.zip` で画面を見て、画面か一覧のどちらが plan とずれているかを決めて直す

- [ ] **Step 3: CLI の変更が反映される spec（SSE）**

自分の書き込みに印が付かないことは、ツリーの入力行（Enter で下に追加）で確かめる。

`web/e2e/live.spec.ts`:
```ts
// Screens this spec relies on (see screens.ts for the shared list):
//   - a row changed by someone else (the CLI) shows "CLI で更新 · 今" for 10 seconds
//   - a change the page made itself shows no such mark
//   - hh.open waits for the page's PUT /api/presence, which it sends once the event
//     stream is open, so a change made by the CLI after hh.open reaches the page as an event
//   - the sidebar shows the sync card: "未 push" and "今すぐ同期" for the store
import { expect, test } from "./harness";
import { row } from "./screens";

const MARK = "CLI で更新 · 今";

test("a task added by the CLI appears with the mark, which goes away", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  hh.add("Already here");
  await hh.open(page);
  await expect(row(page, "Already here")).toBeVisible();

  hh.add("From the terminal");
  const added = row(page, "From the terminal");
  await expect(added).toBeVisible();
  await expect(added).toContainText(MARK);

  await expect(added).not.toContainText(MARK, { timeout: 15000 });
});

test("a title changed by the CLI is shown in place, with the mark", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  const t = hh.add("Old title");
  await hh.open(page);
  await expect(row(page, "Old title")).toBeVisible();

  hh.hh("set", t.id, "title=New title");
  await expect(row(page, "New title")).toContainText(MARK);
  await expect(row(page, "Old title")).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("a task deleted by the CLI leaves the tree", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  const t = hh.add("Doomed");
  hh.add("Stays");
  await hh.open(page);
  await expect(row(page, "Doomed")).toBeVisible();

  hh.hh("rm", t.id);
  await expect(row(page, "Doomed")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(row(page, "Stays")).toBeVisible();
});

test("a task added in the page does not get the mark", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  const other = hh.add("Someone else's");
  await hh.open(page);

  await row(page, "Someone else's").click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "新しいタスクのタイトル" }).fill("Made here");
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(row(page, "Made here")).toBeVisible();

  // The events arrive in order: once the CLI's change has shown its mark, the
  // page's own change has been handled too.
  hh.hh("set", other.id, "title=Someone else's, changed");
  await expect(row(page, "Someone else's, changed")).toContainText(MARK);
  await expect(row(page, "Made here")).not.toContainText(MARK);
});

test("the sidebar lists the workspace and the sync card is there", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  await hh.open(page);
  const nav = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "メインナビゲーション" });
  await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name: /demo/ })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(nav.getByRole("button", { name: /今すぐ同期/ })).toBeVisible();
});
```

Run: `cd web && pnpm e2e e2e/live.spec.ts`
Expected: PASS（5 tests）。印が消えるテストは最大 15 秒かかる

- [ ] **Step 4: Onboarding からの Store 作成の spec**

入力行は、新しい Store のスキーマを読むまで読み取り専用（「読み込み中…」）で、`fill` は書き込めるようになるまで待つ。

`web/e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`:
```ts
// Screens this spec relies on (see screens.ts for the shared list):
//   - with no store, the page shows Onboarding: the text "Hedgehug へようこそ" and the
//     button "Store を作る", with no GitHub account to register first
//   - the button opens the dialog "Store を作成" (CreateStore) with the textboxes
//     "名前", "ID の接頭辞" (typed letters become upper case) and "最初のワークスペース（任意）"
//     (empty means the store's name), the line "hh store init <name> --prefix <PREFIX>"
//     at the foot, and the buttons "キャンセル" and "Store を作る"
//   - a prefix that is not an upper-case letter followed by upper-case letters or
//     digits (2 to 10 in all) is refused with a message and nothing is made
//   - after the create the toast says "Store「<name>」を作りました", and the page shows
//     the tree of the new store's first workspace, which the sidebar lists as a link
import { expect, test } from "./harness";
import { row, toast } from "./screens";

test.use({ store: null });

test("a first store is made from Onboarding", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  await hh.open(page);
  await expect(page.getByText("Hedgehug へようこそ")).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }).click();
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Store を作成" });
  await dialog.getByLabel("名前", { exact: true }).fill("team");
  await dialog.getByLabel("ID の接頭辞").fill("tm");
  await expect(dialog.getByLabel("ID の接頭辞")).toHaveValue("TM");
  await expect(
    dialog.getByRole("button", { name: /hh store init team --prefix TM/ }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }).click();

  await expect(toast(page, "Store「team」を作りました")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: /team/ })).toBeVisible();
  expect(hh.hh("store", "list")).toContain("team");

  // The new store takes tasks: the first one gets the prefix.
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^タスクを追加/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "新しいタスクのタイトル" }).fill("First task");
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(row(page, "First task")).toContainText("TM-1");
});

test("a prefix of the wrong shape is refused and nothing is made", async ({ page, hh }) => {
  await hh.open(page);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }).click();
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Store を作成" });
  await dialog.getByLabel("名前", { exact: true }).fill("team");
  await dialog.getByLabel("ID の接頭辞").fill("1A");
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Store を作る" }).click();

  await expect(
    dialog.getByText("接頭辞は英大文字と数字の 2〜10 文字で、英字から始めてください"),
  ).toBeVisible();
  expect(hh.hh("store", "list")).not.toContain("team");
});
```

Run: `cd web && pnpm e2e e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`
Expected: PASS（2 tests）

- [ ] **Step 5: 新しいタブにトークンが引き継がれる spec**

サイドバーのリンクを Ctrl クリックすると `window.open(url, "_blank")`（`noopener` なし）で開く（Task 7 の `onLinkClick`）。ブラウザが `sessionStorage` を複製するので、トークンが新しいタブに引き継がれる。ブラウザが自分でリンクを新しいタブに開くと、引き継がれずに「hh ui が開いた URL からだけ使えます」の画面になる（Chromium で確かめた）。

`web/e2e/newtab.spec.ts`:
```ts
// Screens this spec relies on (see screens.ts for the shared list):
//   - the sidebar lists the workspace as a link named by it; Ctrl+click on it opens the
//     workspace in a new tab that keeps the token (the click calls window.open without
//     noopener, so the browser copies sessionStorage to the tab)
//   - a tab with no token (a fresh browser context) shows "この画面は hh ui が開いた URL
//     からだけ使えます"
import { expect, test } from "./harness";

test("Ctrl+click on a sidebar link opens a tab that keeps the token", async ({
  page,
  hh,
  context,
}) => {
  // The browser opens the tab as a copy of this one (Chromium copies sessionStorage to a tab
  // opened from a link, with a modifier key or not), so the token goes with it.
  hh.add("Anything");
  await hh.open(page);
  const link = page
    .getByRole("navigation", { name: "メインナビゲーション" })
    .getByRole("link", { name: /demo/ });
  await expect(link).toBeVisible();
  const [tab] = await Promise.all([
    context.waitForEvent("page"),
    link.click({ modifiers: ["Control"] }),
  ]);
  await expect(tab).toHaveURL(/\/workspaces\/demo\//);
  await expect(tab.getByRole("tree", { name: "demo のタスク" })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(tab.getByText("この画面は hh ui が開いた URL からだけ使えます")).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("a tab opened by a plain link has no token", async ({ browser, hh }) => {
  // The token lives in sessionStorage of the tab hh ui opened (the spec's decision): a
  // fresh tab on the same URL without it shows the screen that says to start from hh ui.
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const fresh = await context.newPage();
  await fresh.goto(`${hh.origin}/`);
  await expect(fresh.getByText("この画面は hh ui が開いた URL からだけ使えます")).toBeVisible();
  await context.close();
});
```

Run: `cd web && pnpm e2e e2e/newtab.spec.ts`
Expected: PASS（2 tests）

- [ ] **Step 6: 全体を通す**

Run: `cd web && pnpm fix && pnpm lint && pnpm check && cd .. && make e2e`
Expected: Biome・svelte-check にエラーなし。`make e2e` が 17 テストすべて PASS（`pnpm exec playwright test --repeat-each=3` でも）。並列で走らせて不安定なテストがあれば、まず原因（待ち方・文言）を直す。`workers` を減らして隠さない

- [ ] **Step 7: CI の e2e ジョブ**

`.github/workflows/ci-frontend.yaml` の末尾（`web` ジョブの後）に足す。ステップの間は1行空ける（ほかのワークフローと同じ）。ブラウザと共有ライブラリは `--with-deps` で入れ、`make e2e` の `playwright install chromium` はそれが済んでいれば何もしない。

```yaml

  # 実際のバイナリ（GUI を埋め込んだもの）をブラウザで動かす。
  e2e:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false

      - uses: actions/setup-go@b7ad1dad31e06c5925ef5d2fc7ad053ef454303e # v7.0.0
        with:
          go-version-file: go.mod
          cache: false

      - uses: jdx/mise-action@82a612c5ab8bcda7cc5db6b7e81a8e7584c3dc6e # v5.2.3
        with:
          install_args: node pnpm

      - name: Install dependencies
        working-directory: web
        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile

      # ブラウザと、その共有ライブラリ（apt）を入れる。make e2e は同じブラウザを入れ直さない。
      - name: Install the browser
        working-directory: web
        run: pnpm exec playwright install --with-deps chromium

      - name: E2E
        run: make e2e
```

Run: `actionlint .github/workflows/ci-frontend.yaml`
Expected: エラーなし

- [ ] **Step 8: コミット**
```bash
git add web/e2e .github/workflows/ci-frontend.yaml
git commit -m "test(web): Playwright の E2E（ツリー・SSE・Onboarding・新しいタブ）と CI の e2e ジョブを足す"
```

---

## 付録: E2E が前提にする文言と role（ツリーとコマンドパレット）

ツリー（`TreeView`）:
- 全体は `section[aria-label="ツリー"]`。ツリー本体は `role="tree"`、`aria-label="<ワークスペース名またはプロジェクト名> のタスク"`。行は `role="treeitem"`（`data-ulid`、`aria-level`、`aria-selected`、`aria-expanded`、Task 10 で `data-drop="before|after|child|reject"`）。選択の ULID は `selection.ulid`
- 折りたたみは行の中の `button`、名前は「折りたたむ: <タイトル>」「展開する: <タイトル>」
- フィルタ欄は `getByLabel("フィルタ（hh ls と同じクエリ）")`（`#tree-q`）。既定の値は `-status:canceled`。欄の左のベースは `project:REL-15`（プロジェクト）か `workspace:<名前>`、件数は「n 件 · 中止 m 件を除外」、足元は `$ hh ls --store <Store> --tree …`（`CliLine`）。クエリの誤りは `role="alert"`（`errorMessage` の文言だけ。detail は重ねて出さない）
- 追加: 足元の `button`「タスクを追加」（右に「→ <ワークスペース> / <プロジェクト> · 種類: <種類>」）。行を選んで Enter で入力行（`getByLabel("新しいタスクのタイトル")`、placeholder「タイトルを入力して Enter（Esc で閉じる）」）
- 空の状態: 「<名前> にはまだタスクがありません」と button「タスクを作成」、フィルタで0件は「一致するタスクはありません」と button「フィルタを解除」
- ドラッグの拒否: 対象の行に chip「ここには置けません」、下に `role="status"`（`<strong>` が「エピックはタスクの下に置けません」など、`<p>` が理由）。離すとトースト（「<題>。<理由>」）
- 印: 「CLI で更新 · 今」「同期で更新 · 今」、「壊れています」、「<field> 未入力」、「<ID> 待ち」
- キーは行（`treeitem`）にフォーカスがあるときだけ効く: Enter、Tab、⇧Tab、⌥↑↓、Space、S、K（`Menu`）、⌘↵（Ctrl+Enter。詳細へ）、←/→

コマンドパレット（`CommandPalette`）:
- `role="dialog"`、名前「ID・検索・コマンド」。入力は `role="combobox"`、一覧は `role="listbox"`、項目は `role="option"`（`aria-selected`）、グループは `role="group"`（名前はグループ名）
- モードのチップは `[data-mode]`、文字は「ID」「クエリ」「コマンド」「ステータス」
- グループ名: 「タスク」「クエリに一致 · n 件」「コマンド」「最近開いたもの」「開く」「<ID> に対して」「<ID> のステータス」。旧 ID で一致したときの補足「旧 ID SH-17 で一致」。0件は「一致するものがありません」、エラーは `role="alert"`
- コマンド名: 「今すぐ同期する」「タスクを作成」「プロジェクトを作成」「<ワークスペース名> を開く」「<プロジェクト名> を開く」「ステータスを変更…」「子タスクを作る」「詳細を開く」
